_G.__Elite_GrappleFired = true  -- AYDN: Hook tp REMOVED (flag kept for legacy checks only)
_G.__Elite_KillQ = {}
-- ===========================================================================
-- EXACT ANTI-DETECTION GUI HOST: every AYDN gui lives in the executor's
-- PROTECTED CoreGui view -- gethui() when the executor provides one (the game
-- cannot enumerate that folder), plain CoreGui otherwise, PlayerGui as the
-- last resort. syn.protect_gui / protect_gui is applied on top when present,
-- so a gui scanner walking CoreGui children has nothing to flag.
-- ===========================================================================
_G.AYDN_GuiHost = function()
    local _host = nil
    pcall(function()
        if typeof(gethui) == "function" then
            local _h = gethui()
            if _h then _host = _h end
        end
    end)
    if not _host then
        pcall(function() _host = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
    end
    if not _host then
        pcall(function()
            _host = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
                or game:GetService("CoreGui")
        end)
    end
    return _host
end
_G.AYDN_ProtectGui = function(gui)
    if not gui then return end
    pcall(function()
        if syn and syn.protect_gui then syn.protect_gui(gui) end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if protect_gui then protect_gui(gui) end
    end)
end
-- OPTIMIZER master switch (default ON; toggle lives in AYDN SETTINGS)
if _G.__Elite_OptimizerOn == nil then _G.__Elite_OptimizerOn = true end
if _G.ElitePreferFrontOnRow == nil then _G.ElitePreferFrontOnRow = true end
if _G.EliteSideTPRange == nil then _G.EliteSideTPRange = 100 end
pcall(function()
if type(setfpscap) == "function" then
setfpscap(999)
elseif type(set_fps_cap) == "function" then
set_fps_cap(999)
end
end)
do
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local LP = Players.LocalPlayer
while not LP do task.wait(); LP = Players.LocalPlayer end
_G.EliteNoCollide = true
_OTHER_CLONES = _OTHER_CLONES or {}
local _MY_CLONE = tostring(LP.UserId) .. "_Clone"
local _seen = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local _ncBag = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local function _isPlayerChar(inst)
if not inst then return false end
if inst == LP.Character then return true end
for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if pl.Character == inst then return true end
end
return false
end
local function _isOtherClone(inst)
if not inst or not inst:IsA("Model") then return false end
if inst == LP.Character then return false end
if _isPlayerChar(inst) then return false end
local n = tostring(inst.Name or "")
if n == _MY_CLONE then return false end
if n:match("^%d+_Clone$") then return true end
local pid = nil
pcall(function()
pid = inst:GetAttribute("ParentId") or inst:GetAttribute("ParentID") or inst:GetAttribute("OwnerId")
end)
if pid ~= nil then
local hasHrp = inst:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or inst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
local hasHum = inst:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if hasHrp and hasHum then return true end
end
if n:find("Clone", 1, true) or n:find("clone", 1, true) then
if inst:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and inst:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
return true
end
end
return false
end
local function _linkNoCollide(clone)
local mine = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not mine then return end
local bag = _ncBag[clone]
if not bag then bag = {}; _ncBag[clone] = bag end
for _, pn in ipairs({ "HumanoidRootPart", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso", "Torso", "Head" }) do
local p = clone:FindFirstChild(pn)
if p and p:IsA("BasePart") and not bag[pn] then
pcall(function()
local k = Instance.new("NoCollisionConstraint")
k.Part0 = mine
k.Part1 = p
k.Parent = mine
bag[pn] = k
end)
end
end
end
local function _neutralize(inst)
if not inst then return end
if not _seen[inst] then
_seen[inst] = true
_OTHER_CLONES[#_OTHER_CLONES + 1] = inst
inst.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
if d:IsA("BasePart") then
pcall(function() if d.CanCollide then d.CanCollide = false end end)
end
end)
inst.Destroying:Connect(function()
_seen[inst] = nil
for i = #_OTHER_CLONES, 1, -1 do
if _OTHER_CLONES[i] == inst then table.remove(_OTHER_CLONES, i); break end
end
end)
end
for _, d in ipairs(inst:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.CanCollide then
pcall(function() d.CanCollide = false end)
end
end
_linkNoCollide(inst)
end
local function _scan(inst)
if _isOtherClone(inst) then _neutralize(inst) end
end
for _, c in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do _scan(c) end
workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(c)
_scan(c)
task.defer(function() if c and c.Parent == workspace then _scan(c) end end)
task.delay(0.2, function() if c and c.Parent == workspace then _scan(c) end end)
end)
local function _pin(p)
if not p:IsA("BasePart") then return end
if p.CanCollide then p.CanCollide = false end
pcall(function()
p:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanCollide"):Connect(function()
if p.CanCollide then p.CanCollide = false end
end)
end)
end
local function _pinChar(ch)
if not ch then return end
for _, d in ipairs(ch:GetDescendants()) do _pin(d) end
ch.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d) task.defer(_pin, d) end)
end
local function _hookPlayer(pl)
if pl == LP then return end
if pl.Character then _pinChar(pl.Character); _linkNoCollide(pl.Character) end
pl.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
task.wait(0.05)
_pinChar(c)
_linkNoCollide(c)
end)
end
for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do _hookPlayer(pl) end
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(_hookPlayer)
local _ncAcc = 0
RunService.Stepped:Connect(function(_, dt)
if _G.EliteNoCollide == false or _G.isCloning == true then return end
_ncAcc = _ncAcc + (dt or 0.016)
if _ncAcc < 0.1 then return end
_ncAcc = 0
local c = LP.Character
local myHrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not myHrp then return end
local mp = myHrp.Position
local rng = tonumber(_G.EliteNoCollideRange) or 28
for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
local ch = (pl ~= LP) and pl.Character
local h = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if h and (h.Position - mp).Magnitude <= rng then
for _, d in ipairs(ch:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.CanCollide then d.CanCollide = false end
end
end
end
for _, cl in ipairs(_OTHER_CLONES) do
if cl and cl.Parent and cl.Name ~= _MY_CLONE then
local h = cl:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if h and (h.Position - mp).Magnitude <= rng then
for _, d in ipairs(cl:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.CanCollide then d.CanCollide = false end
end
_linkNoCollide(cl)
end
end
end
end)
task.spawn(function()
while true do
task.wait(0.4)
for _, c in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
if not _seen[c] then _scan(c) end
end
end
end)
end
do
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Workspace = workspace
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
local LP = Players.LocalPlayer
while not LP do
task.wait()
LP = Players.LocalPlayer
end
_G.M4rcAntiRagdoll = true
_G.M4rcAntiBee = true
_G.M4rcAntiDisco = true
local AntiRagdollV2Data = { conns = {}, charConn = nil, beat = nil, acs = nil, acsChar = nil, rootAC = nil }
local antiRagV2On = false
local function _arv2_anyACDisabled(ch)
if not ch then return false end
if AntiRagdollV2Data.acsChar ~= ch or not AntiRagdollV2Data.acs then
AntiRagdollV2Data.acsChar = ch
AntiRagdollV2Data.acs = {}
AntiRagdollV2Data.rootAC = nil
for _, d in ipairs(ch:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("AnimationConstraint") then
table.insert(AntiRagdollV2Data.acs, d)
if d.Name == "Root" then AntiRagdollV2Data.rootAC = d end
end
end
end
for _, ac in ipairs(AntiRagdollV2Data.acs) do
if ac.Parent and not ac.Enabled then return true end
end
return false
end
local _ARV2_LAUNCH = {
BodyVelocity = true, BodyForce = true, BodyThrust = true, BodyGyro = true,
BodyAngularVelocity = true, VectorForce = true, AngularVelocity = true, RocketPropulsion = true,
}
local _ARV2_WHITELIST = {
SpeedForce = true, InvisSpeedForce = true, JumpForce = true,
FlightPower = true, FlightSpin = true, FlightHold = true,
_M4rcDragLV = true, _M4rcDragAtt = true,
EliteDragLV = true, EliteFlight = true,
}
local function _arv2_isFlying(ch)
local hrp = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not hrp then return false end
return hrp:FindFirstChild("FlightPower") ~= nil
or hrp:FindFirstChild("FlightSpin") ~= nil
or hrp:FindFirstChild("FlightHold") ~= nil
end
local function _arv2_considerLaunch(d)
if not d then return false end
if _ARV2_WHITELIST[d.Name] then return false end
if d.Name:find("M4rc", 1, true) or d.Name:find("Flight", 1, true) or d.Name:find("Elite", 1, true) then
return false
end
local cn = d.ClassName
local launch = _ARV2_LAUNCH[cn] == true
if not launch and cn == "LinearVelocity" then launch = true end
if not launch and (cn:find("Force") or cn:find("Velocity") or cn:find("Body") or cn:find("Propulsion")) then
if d.Name:find("Impulse") or d.Name:find("Knockback") or d.Name:find("Launch") then
launch = true
end
end
if launch then
pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
return true
end
return false
end
local function _arv2_stripLaunchForces(ch)
local found = false
local root = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if root then
for _, d in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
if _arv2_considerLaunch(d) then found = true end
end
end
return found
end
local function _arv2_controls(enable)
pcall(function()
local scripts = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
local module = scripts and scripts:FindFirstChild("PlayerModule")
if module then
local controls = require(module):GetControls()
if enable then controls:Enable() else controls:Disable() end
end
end)
end
local function cleanRagdollV2(char)
if not char then return end
local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
pcall(function()
if hum then
hum.PlatformStand = false
hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false)
hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics, false)
hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding, false)
local st = hum:GetState()
if st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding then
hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
end
end
end)
for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") then
pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
elseif d:IsA("AnimationConstraint") then
if not d.Enabled then pcall(function() d.Enabled = true end) end
end
end
local rootAC = AntiRagdollV2Data.rootAC
if rootAC and rootAC.Parent and (rootAC.Attachment0 == nil or not rootAC.Attachment0.Parent) then
local rra = hrp and hrp:FindFirstChild("RootRigAttachment")
if rra then pcall(function() rootAC.Attachment0 = rra end) end
end
_arv2_stripLaunchForces(char)
if hrp then
pcall(function() hrp.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero end)
end
_arv2_controls(true)
pcall(function() LP:SetAttribute("RagdollEndTime", nil) end)
end
local function _arv2_killSource()
if typeof(getconnections) ~= "function" then return end
pcall(function()
local pkg = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Packages")
local re
local net = pkg and pkg:FindFirstChild("Net")
if net then re = net:FindFirstChild("RE/Ragdoll") end
if not re then
local rag = pkg and pkg:FindFirstChild("Ragdoll")
re = rag and rag:FindFirstChild("Ragdoll")
end
if re and re:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(re.OnClientEvent)) do
local ok = pcall(function() c:Disable() end)
if not ok then pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
end
end
end)
end
local function _arv2_disconnectHooks()
for _, c in ipairs(AntiRagdollV2Data.conns) do
pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
end
AntiRagdollV2Data.conns = {}
end
local function hookAntiRagV2(char)
if not antiRagV2On or not char then return end
_arv2_disconnectHooks()
_arv2_killSource()
cleanRagdollV2(char)
local cET = LP:GetAttributeChangedSignal("RagdollEndTime"):Connect(function()
if not antiRagV2On then return end
local et = LP:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime")
if et and (et - Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()) > 0 then
cleanRagdollV2(LP.Character)
end
end)
table.insert(AntiRagdollV2Data.conns, cET)
local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if hum then
local cSt = hum.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, newState)
if not antiRagV2On then return end
if newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
or newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
or newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
or newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding then
cleanRagdollV2(char)
end
end)
table.insert(AntiRagdollV2Data.conns, cSt)
end
local cDA = char.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
if not antiRagV2On then return end
if d:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") then
task.defer(function() pcall(function() d:Destroy() end) end)
elseif d:IsA("AnimationConstraint") then
AntiRagdollV2Data.acs = nil
else
task.defer(function() _arv2_considerLaunch(d) end)
end
end)
table.insert(AntiRagdollV2Data.conns, cDA)
end
local function stopAntiRagdollV2()
antiRagV2On = false
_arv2_disconnectHooks()
if AntiRagdollV2Data.charConn then
pcall(function() AntiRagdollV2Data.charConn:Disconnect() end)
AntiRagdollV2Data.charConn = nil
end
if AntiRagdollV2Data.beat then
pcall(function() AntiRagdollV2Data.beat:Disconnect() end)
AntiRagdollV2Data.beat = nil
end
end
local function startAntiRagdollV2(enabled)
stopAntiRagdollV2()
antiRagV2On = true
_G.M4rcAntiRagdoll = true
if LP.Character then
task.spawn(function() hookAntiRagV2(LP.Character) end)
end
AntiRagdollV2Data.charConn = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(ch)
if not antiRagV2On then return end
pcall(function() ch:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10) end)
hookAntiRagV2(ch)
task.spawn(function()
for _ = 1, 12 do
task.wait(0.25)
if antiRagV2On then _arv2_killSource() else break end
end
end)
end)
local _arvLastScan = 0
local _arvLastVel = Vector3.zero
AntiRagdollV2Data.beat = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not antiRagV2On then return end
_G.M4rcAntiRagdoll = true
local tpBusy = (_G.M4rcIsTeleporting == true)
or (_G.M4rc_TpActive == true)
or _G.isCloning
or (_G.__Elite_VelFlying == true)
or (_G.EliteIsTeleporting == true)
local ch = LP.Character
if not ch then return end
local et = LP:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime")
local ragdolled = et ~= nil and (et - Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()) > 0
local flying = _arv2_isFlying(ch)
if not ragdolled and not flying then
local hum = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if hum then
local s = hum:GetState()
ragdolled = (s == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
or s == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
or s == Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding
or hum.PlatformStand == true)
end
end
if not ragdolled then ragdolled = _arv2_anyACDisabled(ch) end
local _r = AntiRagdollV2Data.rootAC
if _r and _r.Parent and (_r.Attachment0 == nil or not _r.Attachment0.Parent) then
local _hrp = ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local _rra = _hrp and _hrp:FindFirstChild("RootRigAttachment")
if _rra then pcall(function() _r.Attachment0 = _rra end) end
end
if not ragdolled then
local now = tick()
if now - _arvLastScan > (tonumber(_G.M4rcRagScanInterval) or 0.2) then
_arvLastScan = now
ragdolled = ch:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BallSocketConstraint", true) ~= nil
end
end
-- AYDN FIX: while a TP/clone/fly is running we NEVER clean/rewind the
-- character -- that cleanup was exactly what kept resetting you mid-tp.
if ragdolled and not tpBusy then
_G.M4rcRagdollPhysLast = os.clock()
cleanRagdollV2(ch)
end
local hrp = ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if hrp and ragdolled and not flying and not tpBusy then
local v = hrp.Velocity
if (v - _arvLastVel).Magnitude > (tonumber(_G.M4rcLaunchSpikeThreshold) or 110) then
hrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero
hrp.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
v = Vector3.zero
end
_arvLastVel = v
elseif hrp then
_arvLastVel = hrp.Velocity
end
end)
end
_G.M4rcStartAntiRagdoll = startAntiRagdollV2
_G.M4rcStopAntiRagdoll = function() end
_G.M4rcCleanRagdoll = cleanRagdollV2
_G.EliteStartAntiRagdoll = startAntiRagdollV2
_G.EliteCleanRagdoll = cleanRagdollV2
task.spawn(function()
startAntiRagdollV2(true)
end)
task.spawn(function()
while true do
task.wait(2)
_G.M4rcAntiRagdoll = true
if not antiRagV2On then
startAntiRagdollV2(true)
end
end
end)
local BAD = {
Blue = true, DiscoEffect = true, BeeBlur = true,
Flashbang = true, ColorCorrection = true,
Disco = true, DiscoBall = true, DiscoFloor = true, BeeEffect = true,
}
local KILL_CLASSES = {
BlurEffect = true, BloomEffect = true, SunRaysEffect = true,
ColorCorrectionEffect = true, DepthOfFieldEffect = true,
}
local function nuke(o)
if _G.__Elite_OptimizerOn ~= true then return end
if not o or not o.Parent then return end
if BAD[o.Name] then
pcall(function() o:Destroy() end)
return
end
local n = tostring(o.Name or "")
if n:find("Disco", 1, true) or n:find("BeeBlur", 1, true) or n:find("BeeEffect", 1, true) then
pcall(function() o:Destroy() end)
return
end
if KILL_CLASSES[o.ClassName] then
pcall(function() o.Enabled = false end)
end
end
local buzz
local function muteBuzz()
pcall(function()
if not (buzz and buzz.Parent) then
local ctl = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Controllers")
local item = ctl and ctl:FindFirstChild("ItemController")
local bee = item and item:FindFirstChild("BeeLauncherController")
local s = bee and bee:FindFirstChild("Buzzing")
if s and s:IsA("Sound") then buzz = s end
end
if buzz then
buzz.Volume = 0
if buzz.IsPlaying then buzz:Stop() end
end
end)
end
local guarded = {}
local function guard(Controls, original)
if not Controls or guarded[Controls] then return end
local base = original or Controls.moveFunction
if not base then return end
local function safeMove(self, mv, rtc)
return base(self, mv, rtc)
end
guarded[Controls] = safeMove
Controls.moveFunction = safeMove
local _mfTick = 0
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
_mfTick = _mfTick + 1
if _mfTick < 10 then return end
_mfTick = 0
if Controls.moveFunction ~= safeMove then
Controls.moveFunction = safeMove
end
end)
end
local function protect()
pcall(function()
local cc = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Controllers")
local mod = cc and cc:FindFirstChild("CharacterController")
if mod then
local okm, m = pcall(require, mod)
if okm and type(m) == "table" then
guard(m.Controls, m.originalMoveFunction)
end
end
end)
pcall(function()
local ps = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts") or LP:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts", 5)
local pm = ps and ps:FindFirstChild("PlayerModule")
if pm then
local okm, mod = pcall(require, pm)
if okm and mod and type(mod.GetControls) == "function" then
guard(mod:GetControls())
end
end
end)
end
local function killBeeHive(ch)
if _G.__Elite_OptimizerOn ~= true then return end
if not ch then return end
local n = ch.Name
if string.sub(n, 1, 7) == "BeeHive" or string.sub(n, 1, 7) == "Sentry_" then
pcall(function() ch:Destroy() end)
end
end
task.spawn(function()
pcall(function() LP:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts", 8) end)
Lighting.DescendantAdded:Connect(nuke)
for _, o in ipairs(Lighting:GetDescendants()) do
nuke(o)
end
protect()
for _, ch in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
killBeeHive(ch)
end
workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(killBeeHive)
local acc = 1
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
_G.M4rcAntiBee = true
_G.M4rcAntiDisco = true
local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
if cam and math.abs(cam.FieldOfView - 20) < 0.01 then
cam.FieldOfView = tonumber(_G.M4rcFOV) or 70
end
acc = acc + dt
if acc < 0.5 then return end
acc = 0
for _, o in ipairs(Lighting:GetDescendants()) do
nuke(o)
end
muteBuzz()
end)
end)
LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
task.delay(1, protect)
end)
end
local function __eliteSha256(message)
local band, bxor, bnot = bit32.band, bit32.bxor, bit32.bnot
local rrotate, rshift = bit32.rrotate, bit32.rshift
local U32 = 4294967296
local K = {
0x428a2f98,0x71374491,0xb5c0fbcf,0xe9b5dba5,0x3956c25b,0x59f111f1,0x923f82a4,0xab1c5ed5,
0xd807aa98,0x12835b01,0x243185be,0x550c7dc3,0x72be5d74,0x80deb1fe,0x9bdc06a7,0xc19bf174,
0xe49b69c1,0xefbe4786,0x0fc19dc6,0x240ca1cc,0x2de92c6f,0x4a7484aa,0x5cb0a9dc,0x76f988da,
0x983e5152,0xa831c66d,0xb00327c8,0xbf597fc7,0xc6e00bf3,0xd5a79147,0x06ca6351,0x14292967,
0x27b70a85,0x2e1b2138,0x4d2c6dfc,0x53380d13,0x650a7354,0x766a0abb,0x81c2c92e,0x92722c85,
0xa2bfe8a1,0xa81a664b,0xc24b8b70,0xc76c51a3,0xd192e819,0xd6990624,0xf40e3585,0x106aa070,
0x19a4c116,0x1e376c08,0x2748774c,0x34b0bcb5,0x391c0cb3,0x4ed8aa4a,0x5b9cca4f,0x682e6ff3,
0x748f82ee,0x78a5636f,0x84c87814,0x8cc70208,0x90befffa,0xa4506ceb,0xbef9a3f7,0xc67178f2,
}
local h0,h1,h2,h3 = 0x6a09e667,0xbb67ae85,0x3c6ef372,0xa54ff53a
local h4,h5,h6,h7 = 0x510e527f,0x9b05688c,0x1f83d9ab,0x5be0cd19
local function u32be(n)
return string.char(band(rshift(n,24),255), band(rshift(n,16),255), band(rshift(n,8),255), band(n,255))
end
local bitLen = #message * 8
message = message .. "\128"
while #message % 64 ~= 56 do message = message .. "\0" end
message = message .. u32be(math.floor(bitLen / U32)) .. u32be(bitLen % U32)
local w = {}
for chunk = 1, #message, 64 do
for i = 0, 15 do
local a,b,c,d = string.byte(message, chunk + i*4, chunk + i*4 + 3)
w[i+1] = ((a*256 + b)*256 + c)*256 + d
end
for i = 17, 64 do
local x, y = w[i-15], w[i-2]
local s0 = bxor(rrotate(x, 7), rrotate(x, 18), rshift(x, 3))
local s1 = bxor(rrotate(y, 17), rrotate(y, 19), rshift(y, 10))
w[i] = (w[i-16] + s0 + w[i-7] + s1) % U32
end
local a,b,c,d,e,f,g,h = h0,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6,h7
for i = 1, 64 do
local S1 = bxor(rrotate(e, 6), rrotate(e, 11), rrotate(e, 25))
local ch = bxor(band(e, f), band(bnot(e), g))
local t1 = (h + S1 + ch + K[i] + w[i]) % U32
local S0 = bxor(rrotate(a, 2), rrotate(a, 13), rrotate(a, 22))
local maj = bxor(band(a, b), band(a, c), band(b, c))
local t2 = (S0 + maj) % U32
h, g, f, e = g, f, e, (d + t1) % U32
d, c, b, a = c, b, a, (t1 + t2) % U32
end
h0=(h0+a)%U32 h1=(h1+b)%U32 h2=(h2+c)%U32 h3=(h3+d)%U32
h4=(h4+e)%U32 h5=(h5+f)%U32 h6=(h6+g)%U32 h7=(h7+h)%U32
end
return string.format("%08x%08x%08x%08x%08x%08x%08x%08x", h0,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6,h7)
end
local __eliteRemoteSecret = "36bc48de-ccc6-4666-af4b-7d3bd46d6aa7"
local function __eliteCipher(name, jobId)
local out, pos = {}, 0
for i = 1, #name do
local c = string.byte(name, i)
if c == 47 then
out[i] = "/"
else
pos = pos + 1
local k = string.byte(jobId, (pos - 1) % #jobId + 1)
out[i] = string.char(((c - 32 + k) % 95) + 32)
end
end
return table.concat(out)
end
local __eliteRemoteCache = {}
function _G.Elite_GetRemoteByHash(name)
local hit = __eliteRemoteCache[name]
if hit and hit.Parent then return hit end
local pkgs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Packages")
local Net = pkgs and pkgs:FindFirstChild("Net")
if not Net then return nil end
local jobId = game.JobId
if type(jobId) ~= "string" or jobId == "" then return nil end
local hash = __eliteSha256(__eliteCipher(name, jobId) .. __eliteRemoteSecret .. jobId)
local r = Net:FindFirstChild("RE/" .. hash)
or Net:FindFirstChild("RF/" .. hash)
or Net:FindFirstChild("URE/" .. hash)
if r then __eliteRemoteCache[name] = r end
return r
end
-- HOOK TP REMOVED: the grapple remote hash lookup is gone (always nil).
function _G.Elite_GrappleRemote()
return nil
end
do
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local function pin(a)
if a and a.Name == "Animate" then
pcall(function() a.Disabled = true end)
end
end
local function hook(char)
if not char then return end
pin(char:FindFirstChild("Animate"))
char.ChildAdded:Connect(pin)
end
local function onLp(lp)
if not lp then return end
if lp.Character then hook(lp.Character) end
lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(hook)
end
local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
if lp then
onLp(lp)
else
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(onLp)
end
end
do
local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local KEEP = {
CustomProximityPromptController = true,
InteractController = true,
PlotController = true,
AnimalController = true,
PetController = true,
WorldBrainrotController = true,
EquipBrainrotsController = true,
EquipBrainrotsRenderController = true,
SelectBrainrotsController = true,
BrainrotPreloadingController = true,
AssetStreamController = true,
AdminPanelController = true,
NotificationController = true,
}
local function wrap(fn)
if newcclosure then
return newcclosure(fn)
end
return fn
end
local function isCtrlFolder(x)
return typeof(x) == "Instance" and x.Name == "Controllers" and x.Parent == RS
end
local function callerName()
if not getcallingscript then
return "?"
end
local ok, s = pcall(getcallingscript)
if not ok or s == nil then
return "nil"
end
return tostring(s.Name)
end
local function fromLoader()
return callerName() == "ClientLoader"
end
local function filt(kids)
if type(kids) ~= "table" then
return kids
end
local out = {}
for i = 1, #kids do
local k = kids[i]
if k and KEEP[k.Name] then
out[#out + 1] = k
end
end
return out
end
local oldNc
local undone = false
local function unhook()
if undone then return end
undone = true
pcall(function()
if oldNc then
hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", oldNc)
end
end)
end
pcall(function()
oldNc = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", wrap(function(self, ...)
local method = getnamecallmethod()
if type(method) ~= "string" then
return oldNc(self, ...)
end
local ml = string.lower(method)
if ml ~= "getchildren" then
return oldNc(self, ...)
end
if not isCtrlFolder(self) then
return oldNc(self, ...)
end
if not fromLoader() then
return oldNc(self, ...)
end
return filt(oldNc(self, ...))
end))
end)
task.delay(6, unhook)
end
task.spawn(function()
local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local KILL = {
RNGMachineVFX = true,
LuckIcons = true,
GlossaryBackground = true,
AnimationGraphEditor = true,
ML = true,
PlayWaveEvents = true,
}
local function yeet(ch)
if not ch then return end
pcall(function()
ch:Destroy()
end)
end
local function dropKill(ch)
if ch and KILL[ch.Name] then yeet(ch) end
end
local kids = RS:GetChildren()
for i = 1, #kids do dropKill(kids[i]) end
RS.ChildAdded:Connect(dropKill)
end)
task.wait()
local function _eliteBusy()
    return _G.__Elite_VelFlying == true
end
local function _eliteIsProtectedWorld(inst)
    local x = inst
    for _ = 1, 24 do
        if not x then return false end
        local n = x.Name
        if n == "EventBoard" or n == "Shop" or n == "Robux Shop" or n == "RobuxShop" then
            return true
        end
        if x == workspace then return false end
        x = x.Parent
    end
    return false
end
_G.Elite_IsProtectedWorld = _eliteIsProtectedWorld
local function _eliteProtectPetVisual(inst)
local x = inst
while x and x ~= workspace do
if x.Name == "AnimalPodiums" and x ~= inst and _G.__Elite_AllowNukePods ~= true then return true end
if x:IsA("Model") and (x:FindFirstChild("RootPart") or x:FindFirstChild("FakeRootPart") or x:FindFirstChildOfClass("AnimationController")) then return true end
x = x.Parent
end
return false
end
function _G.Elite_Kill(inst)
    if not inst then return end
    if _G.__Elite_OptimizerOn ~= true then return end
    if _eliteIsProtectedWorld(inst) or _eliteProtectPetVisual(inst) then return end
    -- never destroy individual brainrot models (only full no-prio plot wipe)
    local looksPet = false
    pcall(function()
        if not inst:IsA("Model") then return end
        if inst:FindFirstChild("RootPart") or inst:FindFirstChild("FakeRootPart")
            or inst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("AnimationController", true) then
            looksPet = true
        end
    end)
    if looksPet then return end
    do
        local block = false
        pcall(function()
            local p = inst
            for _ = 1, 16 do
                if not p or p == workspace then break end
                if p.Name == "Laser" or p.Name == "LaserHitbox" then
                    block = true
                    return
                end
                if p.Name == "AnimalPodiums" then
                    if _G.__Elite_AllowNukePods ~= true then
                        block = true
                    end
                    return
                end
                p = p.Parent
            end
        end)
        if block then return end
    end
    local set = _G.Elite_PrioSet
    if type(set) == "table" and next(set) ~= nil then
        local _prioGuard = false
        pcall(function()
            if type(_G.Elite_IsPrioModel) == "function" and _G.Elite_IsPrioModel(inst) then
                _prioGuard = true
                return
            end
            local p = inst
            local underPods, plotRef = false, nil
            for _ = 1, 14 do
                if not p or p == workspace then break end
                if p.Name == "AnimalPodiums" then underPods = true end
                local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
                if plots and p.Parent == plots then
                    plotRef = p
                    break
                end
                if type(_G.Elite_IsPrioModel) == "function" and _G.Elite_IsPrioModel(p) then
                    _prioGuard = true
                    return
                end
                p = p.Parent
            end
            -- priority owner base: keep ALL brainrot models (pods + direct pets)
            if plotRef and type(_G.Elite_PlotHasPrio) == "function" and _G.Elite_PlotHasPrio(plotRef) then
                local looksPet = inst:IsA("Model") and (
                    inst:FindFirstChild("RootPart")
                    or inst:FindFirstChild("FakeRootPart")
                    or inst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("AnimationController", true)
                )
                if underPods or looksPet then
                    _prioGuard = true
                end
            end
        end)
        if _prioGuard then return end
    end
    if _eliteBusy() then
        local q = _G.__Elite_KillQ
        q[#q + 1] = inst
        return
    end
    pcall(function() inst:Destroy() end)
end
function _G.Elite_FlushKills()
    local q = _G.__Elite_KillQ
    _G.__Elite_KillQ = {}
    for i = 1, #q do
        local x = q[i]
        if not x or not x.Parent then continue end
        if _eliteIsProtectedWorld(x) or _eliteProtectPetVisual(x) then continue end
        if type(_G.Elite_PrioSet) == "table" and next(_G.Elite_PrioSet) ~= nil
            and type(_G.Elite_IsPrioModel) == "function" and _G.Elite_IsPrioModel(x) then
            continue
        end
        pcall(function() x:Destroy() end)
    end
end
do
    local MUT_WORDS = {
        gold = true, diamond = true, rainbow = true, cyber = true,
        bloodrot = true, lava = true, galaxy = true, cursed = true,
        divine = true, radioactive = true, candy = true, crystal = true,
        celestial = true, phantom = true, eclipse = true,
        ["yin yang"] = true, yinyang = true, ["yin-yang"] = true,
    }
    local function normPrioName(s)
        s = string.lower(tostring(s or ""))
        s = string.gsub(s, "%[.-%]", " ")
        s = string.gsub(s, "[^%w%s%-]", " ")
        s = string.gsub(s, "%s+", " ")
        s = string.gsub(s, "^%s+", "")
        s = string.gsub(s, "%s+$", "")
        return s
    end
    local function peelMutations(s)
        local changed = true
        while changed and s ~= "" do
            changed = false
            for word in pairs(MUT_WORDS) do
                local pref = word .. " "
                if string.sub(s, 1, #pref) == pref then
                    s = string.sub(s, #pref + 1)
                    changed = true
                    break
                end
            end
            if not changed then
                for word in pairs(MUT_WORDS) do
                    local suf = " " .. word
                    if #s > #suf and string.sub(s, -#suf) == suf then
                        s = string.sub(s, 1, #s - #suf)
                        changed = true
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return s
    end
    function _G.Elite_PrioBaseName(name)
        local set = _G.Elite_PrioSet
        local n = normPrioName(name)
        if n == "" then
            return nil
        end
        if type(set) == "table" and set[n] then
            return n
        end
        local peeled = peelMutations(n)
        if peeled ~= "" and type(set) == "table" and set[peeled] then
            return peeled
        end
        return nil
    end
    function _G.Elite_IsPrioName(name)
        local set = _G.Elite_PrioSet
        if type(set) ~= "table" or next(set) == nil then
            return true
        end
        return _G.Elite_PrioBaseName(name) ~= nil
    end
    function _G.Elite_IsPrioModel(inst)
        if not inst then return false end
        local set = _G.Elite_PrioSet
        -- empty priority list = no special protect (optimizer runs full)
        if type(set) ~= "table" or next(set) == nil then
            return false
        end
        if type(inst.Name) == "string" and _G.Elite_PrioBaseName(inst.Name) ~= nil then
            return true
        end
        local ok, desc = pcall(function() return inst:GetDescendants() end)
        if ok and type(desc) == "table" then
            for i = 1, #desc do
                local d = desc[i]
                if d and d:IsA("Model") and type(d.Name) == "string"
                    and _G.Elite_PrioBaseName(d.Name) ~= nil then
                    return true
                end
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    -- if a plot has ANY priority brainrot, keep ALL brainrot models on that plot
    function _G.Elite_PlotHasPrio(plot)
        if not plot then return false end
        local set = _G.Elite_PrioSet
        if type(set) ~= "table" or next(set) == nil then
            return false
        end
        local okc, ch = pcall(function()
            return _G.eliteSyncGet and _G.eliteSyncGet(plot.Name)
        end)
        if okc and ch then
            local list
            pcall(function()
                if type(channelGet) == "function" then
                    list = channelGet(ch, "AnimalList")
                end
            end)
            if type(list) ~= "table" then
                pcall(function()
                    list = ch.AnimalList or (ch.Get and ch:Get("AnimalList"))
                end)
            end
            if type(list) == "table" then
                for _, ad in pairs(list) do
                    if type(ad) == "table" and type(ad.Index) == "string"
                        and _G.Elite_PrioBaseName(ad.Index) ~= nil then
                        return true
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        for _, c in ipairs(plot:GetChildren()) do
            if c:IsA("Model") and _G.Elite_IsPrioModel(c) then
                return true
            end
            if c.Name == "AnimalPodiums" then
                for _, slot in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
                    if _G.Elite_IsPrioModel(slot) then
                        return true
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return false
    end
end
local function __eliteStrip()
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
    local LP = Players.LocalPlayer
    local function kill(inst)
        _G.Elite_Kill(inst)
    end
    pcall(function() workspace.Terrain.Decoration = false end)

    local WS_KILL = {
        FriendPanel = true, Cash = true, Unlock = true, Purchases = true,
        PlotSign = true, CashPad = true, InvisibleWalls = true, Multiplier = true,
        Slope = true, IncubatorMachine = true, FuseMachine = true,
        AdminBoard = true, SoccerScoreBoard = true, CrystalSpinWheel = true,
        Beehive = true, CraftingMachine = true, Road = true,
        TradePlazaPortal = true, LosTraders = true,
        RNGMachine = true, Present = true, StPatricksPot = true,
        ShopNPCRobuxIdle = true, RoadPanel = true, Honey = true,
        Trap = true, Events = true, Sounds = true, rods = true,
        Dev = true, ToolsAdds = true, __PROJECTILES = true, CrystalMap = true,
        SpawnLocation = true, UFO = true, Interacts = true,
    }
    local PLOT_KEEP = {
        Skin = true, Laser = true, AnimalPodiums = true, PlotSign = true,
        DeliveryHitbox = true, StealHitbox = true, AnimalTarget = true,
        MainRoot = true, Floor = true, Spawn = true, Door = true, Hitbox = true,
        Base = true, Wall = true, Roof = true, SecondFloor = true, ThirdFloor = true,
        ["structure base home"] = true, Unlock = true,
    }
    local PLOT_NUKE = {
        FriendPanel = true, Cash = true, Purchases = true,
        CashPad = true, InvisibleWalls = true, Multiplier = true,
        Events = true, Sounds = true,
        Slope = true,
    }
    local BODY_PART = {
        HumanoidRootPart = true, Head = true, Torso = true,
        UpperTorso = true, LowerTorso = true,
        LeftUpperArm = true, LeftLowerArm = true, LeftHand = true,
        RightUpperArm = true, RightLowerArm = true, RightHand = true,
        LeftUpperLeg = true, LeftLowerLeg = true, LeftFoot = true,
        RightUpperLeg = true, RightLowerLeg = true, RightFoot = true,
        ["Left Arm"] = true, ["Right Arm"] = true,
        ["Left Leg"] = true, ["Right Leg"] = true,
    }
    local CHAR_KEEP_CLASS = {
        Humanoid = true, Animator = true, Attachment = true,
        Motor6D = true, AnimationConstraint = true,
        BallSocketConstraint = true, Weld = true, WeldConstraint = true,
    }
    local MAP_KEEP = { Ground = true, Ground_Left = true, Ground_Right = true }
    local PART_CLASS = {
        Part = true, MeshPart = true, UnionOperation = true,
        WedgePart = true, CornerWedgePart = true, TrussPart = true,
        SpawnLocation = true, Seat = true, VehicleSeat = true,
    }

    local function underUnlock(d)
        local x = d
        for _ = 1, 12 do
            if not x then return false end
            if x.Name == "Unlock" then return true end
            if x == workspace then return false end
            x = x.Parent
        end
        return false
    end
    local function isMine(inst)
        if not (LP and inst) then return false end
        if inst == LP.Character or inst.Name == LP.Name then return true end
        return false
    end
    local function isVisualJunk(d)
        local cn = d.ClassName
        return cn == "Beam" or cn == "Trail" or cn == "ParticleEmitter"
            or cn == "PointLight" or cn == "SpotLight" or cn == "SurfaceLight"
            or cn == "Fire" or cn == "Smoke" or cn == "Sparkles"
            or cn == "BillboardGui" or cn == "SurfaceGui" or cn == "Highlight"
            or cn == "Decal" or cn == "Texture"
    end
    local function isSkinJunk(d)
        if not d then return false end
        if isVisualJunk(d) then return true end
        return PART_CLASS[d.ClassName] == true and not d.CanCollide
    end
    local function isPet(inst)
        if not inst or inst.ClassName ~= "Model" then return false end
        return inst:FindFirstChild("FakeRootPart") ~= nil
            or inst:FindFirstChild("RootPart") ~= nil
    end
    local function stripSkin(skin)
        if not (skin and skin.Parent) then return end
        local dd = skin:GetDescendants()
        local dump = {}
        for i = 1, #dd do
            if isSkinJunk(dd[i]) then dump[#dump + 1] = dd[i] end
        end
        for i = 1, #dump do kill(dump[i]) end
    end
    local function skipLaserStrip(plot)
        local keep = _G.__Elite_KeepPlot
        if not keep then
            return true
        end
        return plot and tostring(plot.Name) == tostring(keep)
    end
    local function skipTimerStrip(plot)
        return skipLaserStrip(plot)
    end
    local function plotOf(d)
        local x = d
        for _ = 1, 16 do
            if not x then return nil end
            local p = x.Parent
            if p and p.Name == "Plots" then return x end
            x = p
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function isRemainingTimeGui(d)
        if not d then return false end
        local x = d
        for _ = 1, 16 do
            if not x then break end
            if x.Name == "RemainingTime" then return true end
            x = x.Parent
        end
        local cn = d.ClassName
        if cn == "BillboardGui" or cn == "SurfaceGui" then
            local hit
            pcall(function()
                hit = d:FindFirstChild("RemainingTime", true)
            end)
            if hit then return true end
        end
        return false
    end
    local function stripPurchasesKeepTimer(purch)
        if not (purch and purch.Parent) then return end
        local kids = purch:GetChildren()
        for i = 1, #kids do
            local ch = kids[i]
            if ch.Name ~= "PlotBlock" then
                kill(ch)
            else
                local dd = ch:GetDescendants()
                for j = 1, #dd do
                    local d = dd[j]
                    local cn = d.ClassName
                    if (cn == "BillboardGui" or cn == "SurfaceGui") and not isRemainingTimeGui(d) then
                        kill(d)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local laserPend = {}
    local function stripLaser(laser)
        -- disabled: game owns laser collision (open base = pass, closed = block)
        return
    end
    local function queueStripLaser(laser)
        return
    end
    local function stripPetVfx(pet)
        return
    end
    _G.Elite_StripPetVfx = stripPetVfx

    local function isCharJunk(d)
        if not d then return false end
        local cn = d.ClassName
        if cn == "PathfindingModifier" then return false end
        if cn == "WrapTarget" or cn == "WrapLayer" then return true end
        if cn == "Shirt" or cn == "ShirtGraphic" then return true end
        if cn == "NoCollisionConstraint" then return true end
        local nm = d.Name
        if nm == "__HITBOX" or nm == "OriginalPosition" or nm == "OriginalSize" then return true end
        if CHAR_KEEP_CLASS[cn] then return false end
        if BODY_PART[nm] then return false end
        return true
    end
    local function isUuidName(n)
        return type(n) == "string" and #n == 36
            and string.match(n, "^%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x$") ~= nil
    end
    local function isOtherAvatar(char)
        if not (char and char.ClassName == "Model") then return false end
        if isMine(char) then return false end
        if isPet(char) then return false end
        local n = char.Name
        if type(n) == "string" and string.sub(n, -6) == "_Clone" then return false end
        if Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(char) then return true end
        if Players:FindFirstChild(n) then return true end
        return char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") ~= nil
    end
    local hookedChars = {}
    local function stripOtherChar(char)
        if _G.__Elite_VelFlying then return end
        if not (char and char.Parent) or isMine(char) or not isOtherAvatar(char) then return end
        local function sweep()
            local dd = char:GetDescendants()
            local dump = {}
            for i = 1, #dd do
                if isCharJunk(dd[i]) then dump[#dump + 1] = dd[i] end
            end
            for i = 1, #dump do kill(dump[i]) end
        end
        sweep()
        if hookedChars[char] then return end
        hookedChars[char] = true
        char.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
            if _G.__Elite_VelFlying then return end
            if d and d.Parent and not isMine(char) and isCharJunk(d) then kill(d) end
        end)
    end

    local strippedPlots = {}
    _G.__Elite_StrippedPlots = strippedPlots
    local function near(a, b)
        return math.abs(a - b) < 0.2
    end
    local function isPlotPadPart(d)
        if not d or PART_CLASS[d.ClassName] ~= true then return false end
        if d.Name == "structure base home" or d.Name == "Spawn" then return false end
        local s = d.Size
        if near(s.X, 4) and near(s.Y, 1) and near(s.Z, 4) then return true end
        if near(s.X, 2) and near(s.Y, 0.5) and near(s.Z, 2) then return true end
        return false
    end
    local function isPodiumPlate(d)
        if not d or PART_CLASS[d.ClassName] ~= true then return false end
        if d.Name == "Spawn" then return false end
        local s = d.Size
        if near(s.X, 6) and near(s.Y, 0.2) and near(s.Z, 6) then return true end
        if near(s.X, 4) and near(s.Y, 0.2) and near(s.Z, 4) then return true end
        return false
    end
    local function stripPlotDecor(dec)
        if not (dec and dec.Parent) then return end
        local dump = {}
        local function walk(x)
            local kids = x:GetChildren()
            for i = 1, #kids do
                local d = kids[i]
                local cn = d.ClassName
                local nm = d.Name
                if nm == "Spawn" or nm == "PromptAttachment" or cn == "ProximityPrompt" then
                elseif cn == "Model" and isPet(d) then
                elseif cn == "Beam" or cn == "PointLight" then
                    dump[#dump + 1] = d
                elseif isPlotPadPart(d) then
                    dump[#dump + 1] = d
                else
                    walk(d)
                end
            end
        end
        walk(dec)
        for i = 1, #dump do kill(dump[i]) end
    end
    local function stripPodiumPlates(pods)
        return
    end
    local function handlePlotChild(plot, ch)
        if not (ch and ch.Parent) then return end
        local n = ch.Name
        if n == "Skin" then stripSkin(ch); return end
        if n == "Laser" then
            return
        end
        if n == "AnimalPodiums" then
            return
        end
        if n == "Decorations" then stripPlotDecor(ch); return end
        if n == "Purchases" then
            if skipTimerStrip(plot) then
                stripPurchasesKeepTimer(ch)
            else
                kill(ch)
            end
            return
        end
        if PLOT_KEEP[n] then return end
        if PLOT_NUKE[n] then kill(ch); return end
        if isPet(ch) then
            -- never Destroy brainrot models; only strip VFX
            stripPetVfx(ch)
            return
        end
    end
    local function stripPlotInteriorFast(plot)
        if not (plot and plot.Parent) then return end
        if _G.__Elite_MyPlot and plot.Name == _G.__Elite_MyPlot then return end
        strippedPlots[plot.Name] = true
        local kids = plot:GetChildren()
        for i = 1, #kids do
            local ch = kids[i]
            if ch and ch.Parent then
                local n = ch.Name
                if n == "Skin" or n == "Laser" or n == "AnimalPodiums" or n == "Decorations" then
                elseif n == "Purchases" and skipTimerStrip(plot) then
                    stripPurchasesKeepTimer(ch)
                elseif PLOT_KEEP[n] then
                elseif PLOT_NUKE[n] then
                    kill(ch)
                elseif isPet(ch) then
                    -- never Destroy brainrot models here
                    stripPetVfx(ch)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local function stripPlotVisual(plot)
        if not (plot and plot.Parent) then return end
        if _G.__Elite_MyPlot and plot.Name == _G.__Elite_MyPlot then return end
        local skin = plot:FindFirstChild("Skin")
        if skin then stripSkin(skin) end
        -- Laser + AnimalPodiums left intact (game owns open/closed + brainrot models)
        local dec = plot:FindFirstChild("Decorations")
        if dec then stripPlotDecor(dec) end
        local kids = plot:GetChildren()
        for i = 1, #kids do
            local ch = kids[i]
            if isPet(ch) then stripPetVfx(ch) end
        end
    end
    local function stripPlotInterior(plot)
        stripPlotInteriorFast(plot)
        stripPlotVisual(plot)
    end
    local function connectPlot(plot)
        if not (plot and plot.Parent) then return end
        plot.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
            if _G.__Elite_VelFlying then return end
            handlePlotChild(plot, ch)
        end)
        plot.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
            if _G.__Elite_VelFlying then return end
            if not d then return end
            if underUnlock(d) then return end
            do
                local x = d
                for _ = 1, 16 do
                    if not x or x == plot then break end
                    if x.Name == "PlotSign" then return end
                    if x.Name == "AnimalPodiums" then return end
                    if isPet(x) then return end
                    x = x.Parent
                end
            end
            local cn = d.ClassName
            if cn == "BillboardGui" or cn == "SurfaceGui" then
                if skipTimerStrip(plot) and isRemainingTimeGui(d) then return end
                kill(d)
                return
            end
            if d.Name == "Spawn" or d.Name == "PromptAttachment" or cn == "ProximityPrompt" then
                return
            end
            if d.Name == "Decorations" and d.Parent == plot then
                stripPlotDecor(d)
                return
            end
            local p = d.Parent
            if not p then return end
            if p.Name == "LaserHitbox" then
                return
            end
            if PART_CLASS[d.ClassName] then
                if isPlotPadPart(d) then
                    local x = p
                    local inPlotDec, skip = false, false
                    while x and x ~= plot do
                        if isPet(x) or x.Name == "Spawn" or x.Name == "AnimalPodiums" then
                            skip = true; break
                        end
                        if x.Name == "Decorations" and x.Parent == plot then inPlotDec = true end
                        x = x.Parent
                    end
                    if inPlotDec and not skip then kill(d); return end
                elseif isPodiumPlate(d) then
                    local x = p
                    local inDec, skip = false, false
                    while x and x ~= plot do
                        if x.Name == "Spawn" then skip = true; break end
                        if x.Name == "Decorations" then inDec = true end
                        if x.Name == "AnimalPodiums" then
                            if inDec and not skip then kill(d) end
                            return
                        end
                        x = x.Parent
                    end
                end
            end
            local sk = p
            while sk and sk ~= plot do
                if sk.Name == "Skin" then
                    if isSkinJunk(d) then kill(d) end
                    return
                end
                sk = sk.Parent
            end
            local las = p
            while las and las ~= plot do
                if las.Name == "Laser" then
                    return
                end
                las = las.Parent
            end
        end)
    end
    local function plotIsMine(plot)
        if not plot then return false end
        if _G.__Elite_MyPlot and plot.Name == _G.__Elite_MyPlot then return true end
        local ch
        pcall(function()
            if _G.eliteSyncGet then ch = _G.eliteSyncGet(plot.Name) end
        end)
        if ch and _G.Elite_IsMyPlot and _G.Elite_IsMyPlot(ch) then
            _G.__Elite_MyPlot = plot.Name
            return true
        end
        return false
    end
    local function canDestroyPlot(plot)
        -- SHOW ALL BASES: never destroy a whole plot anymore
        return false
    end
    local hookedPlots
    local function hookPlot(plot)
        if plotIsMine(plot) then return end
        stripPlotInterior(plot)
        connectPlot(plot)
    end
    local function hookPlots(folder)
        if not folder or hookedPlots == folder then return end
        hookedPlots = folder
        local kids = folder:GetChildren()
        for i = 1, #kids do
            if not plotIsMine(kids[i]) then
                stripPlotInterior(kids[i])
                connectPlot(kids[i])
            end
        end
        folder.ChildAdded:Connect(function(plot)
            hookPlot(plot)
            if canDestroyPlot(plot) then
                kill(plot)
            end
        end)
    end
    local lastLockedPlot, lastLockTarget, lastLockSweep = nil, nil, -math.huge
    _G.Elite_LockPrioPlot = function(plotName)
        if not plotName then return end
        _G.__Elite_KeepPlot = tostring(plotName)
        local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not plots then return end
        local now = os.clock()
        if lastLockedPlot == plots and lastLockTarget == _G.__Elite_KeepPlot
            and now - lastLockSweep < 0.5 then return end
        lastLockedPlot, lastLockTarget, lastLockSweep = plots, _G.__Elite_KeepPlot, now
        local kids = plots:GetChildren()
        for i = 1, #kids do
            local plot = kids[i]
            if canDestroyPlot(plot) then
                kill(plot)
            end
        end
        local mine = _G.__Elite_MyPlot and plots:FindFirstChild(_G.__Elite_MyPlot)
        if mine and _G.Elite_NukePlotPodiums then
            pcall(_G.Elite_NukePlotPodiums, mine)
        end
        local keepPlot = _G.__Elite_KeepPlot and plots:FindFirstChild(_G.__Elite_KeepPlot)
        if keepPlot and _G.Elite_StripNonPrioPodiums then
            pcall(_G.Elite_StripNonPrioPodiums, keepPlot)
        end
    end

    local function stripMap(map)
        local function handle(x)
            if _G.__Elite_VelFlying then return end
            if x and not MAP_KEEP[x.Name] then kill(x) end
        end
        local kids = map:GetChildren()
        for i = 1, #kids do handle(kids[i]) end
        map.ChildAdded:Connect(handle)
    end
    local hookedRma
    local rmaBoxDone = false
    local function hookRma(rma)
        if not rma then return end
        if rmaBoxDone then
            kill(rma)
            return
        end
        if hookedRma == rma then return end
        hookedRma = rma
        local function nuke()
            if _G.__Elite_VelFlying then return end
            if rmaBoxDone then return end
            rmaBoxDone = true
            kill(rma)
        end
        if #rma:GetChildren() > 0 then
            nuke()
        else
            rma.ChildAdded:Connect(nuke)
        end
    end
    local function onWsChild(ch)
        if not ch then return end
        if _G.__Elite_VelFlying then return end
        local n = ch.Name
        if ch.ClassName == "Camera" or ch.ClassName == "Terrain" then return end
        if n == "Plots" then hookPlots(ch); return end
        if n == "RenderedMovingAnimals" then hookRma(ch); return end
        if n == "Map" then stripMap(ch); return end
        if isMine(ch) then return end
        if isUuidName(n) then kill(ch); return end
        if isOtherAvatar(ch) then stripOtherChar(ch); return end
        if ch.ClassName == "Model" then
            ch.ChildAdded:Connect(function()
                if isOtherAvatar(ch) then stripOtherChar(ch) end
            end)
        end
        if string.sub(n, 1, 7) == "BeeHive" or string.sub(n, 1, 7) == "Sentry_" then
            kill(ch)
            return
        end
        if n == "EventBoard" then
            return
        end
        if WS_KILL[n] or ch.ClassName == "Highlight" then kill(ch); return end
        if ch.ClassName == "Model" and string.find(n, "'s Rainbow", 1, true) then
            kill(ch)
        end
    end
    local wsch = workspace:GetChildren()
    local plotsFolder, mapFolder, rmaFolder
    local rest = {}
    for i = 1, #wsch do
        local ch = wsch[i]
        local n = ch.Name
        if n == "Plots" then plotsFolder = ch
        elseif n == "Map" then mapFolder = ch
        elseif n == "RenderedMovingAnimals" then rmaFolder = ch
        else rest[#rest + 1] = ch end
    end
    if mapFolder then stripMap(mapFolder) end
    if rmaFolder then hookRma(rmaFolder) end
    for i = 1, #rest do onWsChild(rest[i]) end
    if plotsFolder then hookPlots(plotsFolder) end

    local function _forceSolidWorldObstacle(inst)
        if not inst then return end
        local function solid(d)
            if d and d:IsA("BasePart") then
                pcall(function()
                    d.CanCollide = true
                    d.CanQuery = true
                end)
            end
        end
        solid(inst)
        for _, d in ipairs(inst:GetDescendants()) do
            solid(d)
        end
    end
    local function _forceShopCollide(root)
        if not root then return end
        local function apply(inst)
            if not inst then return end
            local n = inst.Name
            if n == "Shop" or n == "Robux Shop" or n == "RobuxShop"
                or n == "EventBoard" or n == "Decor" then
                _forceSolidWorldObstacle(inst)
            end
        end
        apply(root)
        for _, ch in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
            local n = ch.Name
            if n == "Shop" or n == "Robux Shop" or n == "RobuxShop" or n == "EventBoard" then
                apply(ch)
                if n == "EventBoard" then
                    local dec = ch:FindFirstChild("Decor")
                    if dec then apply(dec) end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    pcall(function() _forceShopCollide(workspace) end)
    workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
        local n = ch and ch.Name
        if n == "Shop" or n == "Robux Shop" or n == "RobuxShop" or n == "EventBoard" then
            task.defer(function() _forceShopCollide(ch) end)
        end
    end)
    -- keep EventBoard.Decor solid + visible forever
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            local eb = workspace:FindFirstChild("EventBoard")
            if eb then
                _forceSolidWorldObstacle(eb)
                local dec = eb:FindFirstChild("Decor")
                if dec then _forceSolidWorldObstacle(dec) end
            end
            for _, n in ipairs({ "Shop", "Robux Shop", "RobuxShop" }) do
                local s = workspace:FindFirstChild(n)
                if s then _forceSolidWorldObstacle(s) end
            end
            task.wait(2)
        end
    end)

    workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(onWsChild)

    local function hookPlrChar(plr)
        if not plr or plr == LP then return end
        if plr.Character then stripOtherChar(plr.Character) end
        plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(stripOtherChar)
    end
    local plrs = Players:GetPlayers()
    for i = 1, #plrs do hookPlrChar(plrs[i]) end
    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(hookPlrChar)

    local function onLit(x)
        if _G.__Elite_VelFlying then return end
        if not x then return end
        local cn = x.ClassName
        if cn == "Sky" or cn == "Atmosphere" or cn == "Clouds"
            or cn == "SunRaysEffect" or cn == "ColorCorrectionEffect" then
            kill(x)
        end
    end
    local lit = Lighting:GetChildren()
    for i = 1, #lit do onLit(lit[i]) end
    Lighting.ChildAdded:Connect(onLit)
    local function stripCamFx(x)
        if not x then return end
        local cn = x.ClassName
        if cn == "ColorCorrectionEffect" or cn == "BlurEffect" then kill(x) end
    end
    local hookedCam
    local function hookCam(cam)
        if not cam or hookedCam == cam then return end
        hookedCam = cam
        local ck = cam:GetChildren()
        for i = 1, #ck do stripCamFx(ck[i]) end
        cam.ChildAdded:Connect(stripCamFx)
    end
    hookCam(workspace.CurrentCamera)
    workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function()
        hookCam(workspace.CurrentCamera)
    end)
    workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
        if not d then return end
        if _G.__Elite_VelFlying then return end
        if _G.Elite_IsProtectedWorld and _G.Elite_IsProtectedWorld(d) then return end
        local cn = d.ClassName
        if cn == "BillboardGui" or cn == "SurfaceGui" or cn == "Decal" or cn == "Texture" then
            if underUnlock(d) then return end
            if (cn == "BillboardGui" or cn == "SurfaceGui")
                and skipTimerStrip(plotOf(d)) and isRemainingTimeGui(d) then
                return
            end
            kill(d)
        end
    end)
    if mapFolder and mapFolder.Parent then
        local md = mapFolder:GetDescendants()
        for i = 1, #md do
            local cn = md[i].ClassName
            if cn == "Decal" or cn == "Texture" then kill(md[i]) end
        end
    end
    do
        local ter = workspace.Terrain
        pcall(function() ter.Decoration = false end)
        pcall(function() ter:Clear() end)
        local kids = ter:GetChildren()
        for i = 1, #kids do kill(kids[i]) end
        ter.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
            if _G.__Elite_VelFlying then return end
            kill(ch)
        end)
        ter.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
            if _G.__Elite_VelFlying then return end
            if d then kill(d) end
        end)
    end
    _G.__Elite_StripDone = true
end
task.wait()
local SRC = [=[
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
do
local _xchan
local _synInst, _syncMod
local function _synNow()
if _synInst and _synInst.Parent then return _synInst end
local p = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Packages")
_synInst = p and p:FindFirstChild("Synchronizer")
return _synInst
end
local function _trySenv()
if _xchan then return true end
local syn = _synNow()
if not syn then return false end
if type(_syncMod) ~= "table" then
local req = (getrenv and getrenv().require) or require
local ok, mod = pcall(req, syn)
if ok and type(mod) == "table" then _syncMod = mod end
end
local getFn = type(_syncMod) == "table" and rawget(_syncMod, "Get")
local gf = getfenv or (debug and debug.getfenv)
if type(getFn) ~= "function" or type(gf) ~= "function" then return false end
local ok, env = pcall(gf, getFn)
if not ok or type(env) ~= "table" then return false end
local ch = rawget(env, "Channels")
if type(ch) ~= "table" then return false end
_xchan = ch
return true
end
_trySenv()
local function _chans()
if _xchan then return _xchan end
_trySenv()
return _xchan
end
_G.eliteSyncAll = function() return _chans() end
_G.eliteSyncGet = function(idx)
local t = _chans()
if not t or idx == nil then return nil end
local ok, cd = pcall(rawget, t, idx)
if ok and type(cd) == "table" then return cd end
local ok2, cd2 = pcall(function() return t[idx] end)
if ok2 and type(cd2) == "table" then return cd2 end
return nil
end
_G.Elite_IsPlotChanKey = function(k)
return type(k) == "string" and #k >= 32 and string.find(k, "-", 1, true) ~= nil
end
_G.elite_IsPlotChanKey = _G.Elite_IsPlotChanKey
_G.sProp = function(ch, key)
if type(ch) ~= "table" or key == nil then return nil end
local ct = rawget(ch, "CacheTable")
if type(ct) ~= "table" then
local okC, c2 = pcall(function() return ch.CacheTable end)
if okC and type(c2) == "table" then ct = c2 end
end
if type(ct) ~= "table" then return nil end
local v = rawget(ct, key)
if v ~= nil then return v end
local okV, v2 = pcall(function() return ct[key] end)
if okV then return v2 end
return nil
end
_G.EliteSyncAll = _G.eliteSyncAll
_G.EliteSyncGet = _G.eliteSyncGet
_G.eliteSyncAll = _G.eliteSyncAll
_G.eliteSyncGet = _G.eliteSyncGet
_G.Elite_GetPlotChannel = _G.eliteSyncGet
_G.EliteChanGet = _G.sProp
if _xchan then
_G.__Elite_SyncBoot = true
else
task.spawn(function()
local t0 = os.clock()
while os.clock() - t0 < 8 do
if _trySenv() then
_G.__Elite_SyncBoot = true
return
end
RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
end
end)
end
end
-- AYDN: auto entry velocity TP disabled
task.spawn(function()
-- no auto DoVelocityTP on join
end)
do
local okPre = pcall(function()
RunService.PreSimulation:Connect(function(dt)
local f = _G.__Elite_FlyPre
if f then
f(dt)
end
end)
end)
if not okPre then
RunService.Stepped:Connect(function(_, dt)
local f = _G.__Elite_FlyPre
if f then
f(dt)
end
end)
end
pcall(function()
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
local f = _G.__Elite_FlyHb
if f then
f(dt)
end
end)
end)
end
do
local function hookChar(char)
if not char then return end
task.defer(function()
local h = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 8)
if not (h and h:IsA("BasePart")) then return end
pcall(function()
h.Anchored = false
LocalPlayer.ReplicationFocus = h
end)
end)
end
if LocalPlayer.Character then hookChar(LocalPlayer.Character) end
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(hookChar)
end
do
local function unwalk(char)
local a = char and char:FindFirstChild("Animate")
if a then
pcall(function() a.Disabled = true end)
end
end
local function hook(char)
if not char then return end
unwalk(char)
char.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
if ch.Name == "Animate" then
pcall(function() ch.Disabled = true end)
end
end)
end
if LocalPlayer.Character then hook(LocalPlayer.Character) end
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(hook)
end

_G.Elite_PrioSet = {}
for _, n in ipairs(priorityList) do
if type(n) == "string" and n ~= "" then
_G.Elite_PrioSet[string.lower(n)] = true
end

task.defer(function()
local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
if not plots then return end
local set = _G.Elite_PrioSet
if type(set) ~= "table" then return end
for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
for _, ch in ipairs(plot:GetChildren()) do
if not ch:IsA("Model") then continue end
if not (ch:FindFirstChild("RootPart") or ch:FindFirstChild("FakeRootPart")
or ch:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("AnimationController", true)) then
continue
end
pcall(_G.Elite_StripPetVfx, ch)
end
end

Config = {
AutoStealEnabled = true,
AutoGrabRadius = 90,
AutoTPPriority = false, -- AYDN: no auto TP on priority
StealNearest = true, -- AYDN: auto arm nearest prio
StealMode = "Priority",
PriorityList = priorityList,
InfiniteJump = true,
WaveriderSpeedEnabled = false,
WaveriderSpeed = 140,
StealNearestKey = "T",
AutoClone = true,
InvSteal = false,
ProximityAP = true, -- AYDN: proximity AP (spam players that enter the circle)
ProximityRadius = 12, -- AYDN: circle size, slider in main GUI (max 30)
AydnSpeedValue = 31,
AydnSpeedActive = false,
AydnSpeedOnlySteal = true,
AydnTargetMode = "Priority", -- AYDN: STEAL TARGET list mode (Target = top N in server)
AydnTargetTopN = 3, -- AYDN: how many best-in-server targets the Target list shows
ShowBase30m = true, -- AYDN: 30M+ base filter restored (user rule) -- hide all bases except 30M+/priority; own base always visible; button toggles it
OptimizerOn = true, -- AYDN: lag-strip optimizer toggle (default ON)
InvStealAngle = 220,
InvStealDepth = 10,
TpSettings = {
Tool = "Waverider",
CloneDelayVal = 0.27,
LandingDelayVal = 0.35,
GrabbleTPSpeed = 480,
CloseBaseSpeed = 350,
AydnTPSpeed = 150,
AydnCloseSpeed = 40,
AydnCloseDist = 20,
AydnCloneDelay = 0,
AydnAutoGrab = true,
},
}
do
local _Http = game:GetService("HttpService")
local _CFG_FILE = "EliteHub.json"
local _UIS_FILE = "EliteUis.json"
local function _enc(v)
return _Http:JSONEncode(v)
end
local function _dec(s)
return _Http:JSONDecode(s)
end
local function _safeRead(path)
if type(readfile) ~= "function" then return nil end
local ok, body = pcall(readfile, path)
if not ok or type(body) ~= "string" or body == "" then return nil end
local ok2, data = pcall(_dec, body)
if ok2 and type(data) == "table" then return data end
return nil
end
local function _safeWrite(path, tbl)
if type(writefile) ~= "function" then return false end
local ok, enc = pcall(_enc, tbl)
if not ok or type(enc) ~= "string" then return false end
return pcall(writefile, path, enc)
end
local function _rebuildPrio(list)
if type(list) ~= "table" then return end
Config.PriorityList = list
priorityList = list
_G.Elite_PrioSet = {}
for _, n in ipairs(list) do
if type(n) == "string" and n ~= "" then
_G.Elite_PrioSet[string.lower(n)] = true
end
end
end
function _G.EliteSaveSettings()
local tp = Config.TpSettings
if type(tp) ~= "table" then tp = {} end
local keys = Config.Keybinds or _G.Elite_Keybinds or {}
local payload = {
AutoStealEnabled = Config.AutoStealEnabled ~= false,
AutoGrabRadius = tonumber(Config.AutoGrabRadius) or 90,
AutoTPPriority = Config.AutoTPPriority ~= false,
StealNearest = Config.StealNearest == true,
StealMode = tostring(Config.StealMode or "Priority"),
PriorityList = Config.PriorityList,
InfiniteJump = Config.InfiniteJump ~= false,
ProximityAP = Config.ProximityAP ~= false,
ProximityRadius = tonumber(Config.ProximityRadius) or 12,
WaveriderSpeedEnabled = Config.WaveriderSpeedEnabled == true,
WaveriderSpeed = tonumber(Config.WaveriderSpeed) or 140,
StealNearestKey = tostring(Config.StealNearestKey or "T"),
AutoClone = Config.AutoClone ~= false,
InvSteal = false, -- AYDN: InvSteal forced off
InvStealAngle = tonumber(Config.InvStealAngle) or 220,
InvStealDepth = tonumber(Config.InvStealDepth) or 10,
TpSettings = {
Tool = tostring(tp.Tool or "Waverider"),
CloneDelayVal = tonumber(tp.CloneDelayVal) or 0.27,
LandingDelayVal = tonumber(tp.LandingDelayVal) or 0.35,
GrabbleTPSpeed = tonumber(tp.GrabbleTPSpeed) or 480,
CloseBaseSpeed = tonumber(tp.CloseBaseSpeed) or 350,
AydnTPSpeed = tonumber(tp.AydnTPSpeed) or 150,
AydnCloseSpeed = tonumber(tp.AydnCloseSpeed) or 40,
AydnCloseDist = tonumber(tp.AydnCloseDist) or 20,
AydnCloneDelay = tonumber(tp.AydnCloneDelay) or 0,
AydnAutoGrab = tp.AydnAutoGrab ~= false,
},
Keybinds = keys,
CarpetTool = _G.Elite_CarpetTool,
InvisAuto = false,
AydnSpeedValue = tonumber(Config.AydnSpeedValue) or 31,
AydnSpeedActive = Config.AydnSpeedActive == true,
AydnSpeedOnlySteal = Config.AydnSpeedOnlySteal ~= false,
AydnTargetMode = tostring(Config.AydnTargetMode or "Priority"),
AydnTargetTopN = tonumber(Config.AydnTargetTopN) or 3,
ShowBase30m = Config.ShowBase30m == true,
OptimizerOn = Config.OptimizerOn ~= false,
}
return _safeWrite(_CFG_FILE, payload)
end
function _G.EliteLoadSettings()
local data = _safeRead(_CFG_FILE)
if type(data) ~= "table" then return false end
if type(data.AutoStealEnabled) == "boolean" then Config.AutoStealEnabled = data.AutoStealEnabled end
if data.AutoGrabRadius ~= nil then Config.AutoGrabRadius = tonumber(data.AutoGrabRadius) or Config.AutoGrabRadius end
if type(data.AutoTPPriority) == "boolean" then Config.AutoTPPriority = data.AutoTPPriority end
if type(data.StealNearest) == "boolean" then Config.StealNearest = data.StealNearest end
if type(data.StealMode) == "string" then Config.StealMode = data.StealMode end
if type(data.PriorityList) == "table" then
-- AYDN: the shipped 122-name priority list is authoritative. Only honor a
-- saved EliteHub.json list if it already IS the new list (marker check),
-- so an old saved config can never silently bring the old names back.
local _hasNewList = false
for _, n in ipairs(data.PriorityList) do
if type(n) == "string" and n:lower() == "pum pum pum" then _hasNewList = true break end
end
if _hasNewList then
_rebuildPrio(data.PriorityList)
end
end
if type(data.InfiniteJump) == "boolean" then Config.InfiniteJump = data.InfiniteJump end
if data.ProximityAP ~= nil then Config.ProximityAP = data.ProximityAP == true end
if data.ProximityRadius ~= nil then Config.ProximityRadius = tonumber(data.ProximityRadius) or 12 end
if type(data.WaveriderSpeedEnabled) == "boolean" then Config.WaveriderSpeedEnabled = data.WaveriderSpeedEnabled end
if data.WaveriderSpeed ~= nil then Config.WaveriderSpeed = tonumber(data.WaveriderSpeed) or Config.WaveriderSpeed end
if type(data.StealNearestKey) == "string" then Config.StealNearestKey = data.StealNearestKey end
if type(data.AutoClone) == "boolean" then Config.AutoClone = data.AutoClone end
if type(data.InvSteal) == "boolean" then Config.InvSteal = data.InvSteal end
if data.InvStealAngle ~= nil then Config.InvStealAngle = tonumber(data.InvStealAngle) or Config.InvStealAngle end
if data.InvStealDepth ~= nil then Config.InvStealDepth = tonumber(data.InvStealDepth) or Config.InvStealDepth end
if type(data.TpSettings) == "table" then
Config.TpSettings = Config.TpSettings or {}
if type(data.TpSettings.Tool) == "string" then Config.TpSettings.Tool = data.TpSettings.Tool end
if data.TpSettings.CloneDelayVal ~= nil then Config.TpSettings.CloneDelayVal = tonumber(data.TpSettings.CloneDelayVal) or Config.TpSettings.CloneDelayVal end
if data.TpSettings.LandingDelayVal ~= nil then Config.TpSettings.LandingDelayVal = tonumber(data.TpSettings.LandingDelayVal) or Config.TpSettings.LandingDelayVal end
if data.TpSettings.GrabbleTPSpeed ~= nil then Config.TpSettings.GrabbleTPSpeed = tonumber(data.TpSettings.GrabbleTPSpeed) or Config.TpSettings.GrabbleTPSpeed end
if data.TpSettings.CloseBaseSpeed ~= nil then Config.TpSettings.CloseBaseSpeed = tonumber(data.TpSettings.CloseBaseSpeed) or Config.TpSettings.CloseBaseSpeed end
if data.TpSettings.AydnTPSpeed ~= nil then Config.TpSettings.AydnTPSpeed = tonumber(data.TpSettings.AydnTPSpeed) or 150 end
if data.TpSettings.AydnCloseSpeed ~= nil then Config.TpSettings.AydnCloseSpeed = tonumber(data.TpSettings.AydnCloseSpeed) or 40 end
if data.TpSettings.AydnCloseDist ~= nil then Config.TpSettings.AydnCloseDist = tonumber(data.TpSettings.AydnCloseDist) or 20 end
if data.TpSettings.AydnCloneDelay ~= nil then Config.TpSettings.AydnCloneDelay = tonumber(data.TpSettings.AydnCloneDelay) or 0 end
if data.TpSettings.AydnAutoGrab ~= nil then Config.TpSettings.AydnAutoGrab = data.TpSettings.AydnAutoGrab == true end
end
if type(data.Keybinds) == "table" then
Config.Keybinds = data.Keybinds
_G.Elite_Keybinds = data.Keybinds
end
if type(data.CarpetTool) == "string" and data.CarpetTool ~= "" then
_G.Elite_CarpetTool = data.CarpetTool
end
if type(data.InvisAuto) == "boolean" then
_G.EliteInvisAuto = false
end
if data.AydnSpeedValue ~= nil then Config.AydnSpeedValue = tonumber(data.AydnSpeedValue) or 31 end
if type(data.AydnSpeedActive) == "boolean" then Config.AydnSpeedActive = data.AydnSpeedActive end
if type(data.AydnSpeedOnlySteal) == "boolean" then Config.AydnSpeedOnlySteal = data.AydnSpeedOnlySteal end
if data.AydnTargetMode == "Target" or data.AydnTargetMode == "Priority" then Config.AydnTargetMode = data.AydnTargetMode end
if data.AydnTargetTopN ~= nil then Config.AydnTargetTopN = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(data.AydnTargetTopN) or 3), 1, 20) end
if type(data.ShowBase30m) == "boolean" then Config.ShowBase30m = data.ShowBase30m end
if type(data.OptimizerOn) == "boolean" then Config.OptimizerOn = data.OptimizerOn end
return true
end
function _G.EliteSaveUis()
local pos = _G.Elite_UiPos
if type(pos) ~= "table" then pos = {} end
return _safeWrite(_UIS_FILE, pos)
end
function _G.EliteLoadUis()
local data = _safeRead(_UIS_FILE)
if type(data) == "table" then
_G.Elite_UiPos = data
return data
end
return _G.Elite_UiPos or {}
end
pcall(_G.EliteLoadSettings)
pcall(_G.EliteLoadUis)
_G.__Elite_OptimizerOn = Config.OptimizerOn ~= false
task.spawn(function()
while true do
task.wait(4)
pcall(_G.EliteSaveSettings)
pcall(_G.EliteSaveUis)
end
end)
end
function instantClone()
local c = LocalPlayer.Character
if not c then return end
local h = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not h then return end
local cl = LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Quantum Cloner") or c:FindFirstChild("Quantum Cloner")
if not cl then return end
pcall(function() h:UnequipTools() end)
task.wait()
if cl.Parent ~= c then h:EquipTool(cl); task.wait() end
local pg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
local tf = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("ToolsFrames")
local qc = tf and tf:FindFirstChild("QuantumCloner")
local tb = qc and qc:FindFirstChild("TeleportToClone")
if not tb then return end
_G.isCloning = true
cl:Activate()
task.wait(0.05)
tb.Visible = true
pcall(function() firesignal(tb.MouseButton1Click) end)
pcall(function() firesignal(tb.MouseButton1Up) end)
pcall(function() firesignal(tb.Activated) end)
task.delay(0.55, function() _G.isCloning = false end)
end
do
local LP = LocalPlayer
local RS = ReplicatedStorage
local CARPET_NAMES = {
"Waverider",
"Flying Carpet",
"Witch's Broom",
"Santa's Sleigh",
"Cupid's Wings",
"Skeleton Dragon",
}
_G.Elite_TpTools = {
"Waverider",
"Flying Carpet",
"Witch's Broom",
"Santa's Sleigh",
"Cupid's Wings",
"Skeleton Dragon",
}
local function findTool(name)
local char = LP.Character
local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
return (char and char:FindFirstChild(name)) or (bp and bp:FindFirstChild(name))
end
local _eqCarpetName
local function _carpetInChar(char)
if not char then return nil end
local prefer = (Config and Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.Tool) or _G.Elite_CarpetTool or _eqCarpetName
if type(prefer) == "string" then
local t = char:FindFirstChild(prefer)
if t then
_eqCarpetName = prefer
return prefer
end
end
if _eqCarpetName then
local t = char:FindFirstChild(_eqCarpetName)
if t then return _eqCarpetName end
_eqCarpetName = nil
end
for _, n in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do
local t = char:FindFirstChild(n)
if t then _eqCarpetName = n; return n end
end
return nil
end
local function equipCarpet()
if _G.isCloning then return nil end
local char = LP.Character
local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not hum then return nil end
local preferred = (Config and Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.Tool) or _G.Elite_CarpetTool
if type(preferred) == "string" then
local pt = findTool(preferred)
if pt and pt:IsA("Tool") then
if pt.Parent ~= char then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(pt) end) end
_eqCarpetName = preferred
return preferred
end
end
local held = _carpetInChar(char)
if held then return held end
for _, n in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do
local t = findTool(n)
if t and t:IsA("Tool") then
if t.Parent ~= char then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(t) end) end
_eqCarpetName = n
return n
end
end
return nil
end
-- HOOK TP REMOVED (user spec): no Grapple Hook equip / FireServer anywhere.
-- The engage is now: pull out the SELECTED flying gear and HOLD it (equip
-- only, never Activate -- the only activation is the 1s pulse at GO).
local function carpetEngage()
if _G.isCloning then return nil end
local char = LP.Character
local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not char or not hum then return nil end
local cn
local _tc = os.clock()
repeat
cn = equipCarpet()
local c = LP.Character
if cn and c and c:FindFirstChild(cn) then
break
end
RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
until os.clock() - _tc > 1.5
return cn
end
-- legacy entry point kept, but it is equip-only (hold) now
_G.Elite_PulseGrapple = function()
pcall(carpetEngage)
return true
end
local function setCarpetTool(name)
if type(name) ~= "string" or name == "" then return end
if not Config.TpSettings then
Config.TpSettings = {}
end
Config.TpSettings.Tool = name
if type(_G.EliteSaveSettings) == "function" then pcall(_G.EliteSaveSettings) end
_G.Elite_CarpetTool = name
_eqCarpetName = nil
for i = #CARPET_NAMES, 1, -1 do
if CARPET_NAMES[i] == name then
table.remove(CARPET_NAMES, i)
end
end
table.insert(CARPET_NAMES, 1, name)
_G.Elite_CarpetNames = CARPET_NAMES
if _G.Elite_OnCarpetTool then
pcall(_G.Elite_OnCarpetTool, name)
end
end
_G.Elite_SetCarpetTool = setCarpetTool
if type(_G.Elite_CarpetTool) ~= "string" or _G.Elite_CarpetTool == "" then
if type(_G.elite_CarpetTool) == "string" and _G.elite_CarpetTool ~= "" then
_G.Elite_CarpetTool = _G.elite_CarpetTool
end
end
if type(_G.Elite_CarpetTool) == "string" and _G.Elite_CarpetTool ~= "" then
setCarpetTool(_G.Elite_CarpetTool)
elseif Config and Config.TpSettings and type(Config.TpSettings.Tool) == "string" then
setCarpetTool(Config.TpSettings.Tool)
else
setCarpetTool("Waverider")
end
_G.Elite_CarpetEngage = carpetEngage
_G.Elite_EquipCarpet = equipCarpet
_G.Elite_CarpetNames = CARPET_NAMES
end
