end
local function stealBarHide()
if not _barOn and not (_barGui and _barGui.Enabled) then return end
_barOn = false
_barGen += 1
if _barTw then pcall(function() _barTw:Cancel() end); _barTw = nil end
if _barFill then _barFill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1) end
if _barGui then _barGui.Enabled = false end
end
-- AYDN (user rule): the auto grab LOOKS like it is grabbing the whole
-- time (top bar says AUTO GRABBING), but the grab itself only fires once
-- you are ~GRAB_FIRE_DIST studs in front of the pet.
local GRAB_FIRE_DIST = tonumber(_G.AYDN_GrabFireDistance) or 4
local _autoWaitOn = false
local function autoGrabWaitShow()
if _barOn or _autoWaitOn then return end
if not _barGui or not _barGui.Parent then return end
_autoWaitOn = true
if _G.Elite_StealBarName then _G.Elite_StealBarName.Text = "AUTO GRABBING" end
if _barFill then _barFill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1) end
_barGui.Enabled = true
end
local function autoGrabWaitHide()
if not _autoWaitOn then return end
_autoWaitOn = false
if not _barOn and _barGui and _barGui.Parent then _barGui.Enabled = false end
end
local function stealName()
local pet = _stealTarget or _G.Elite_StealTarget
if type(pet) == "table" then
local n = tostring(pet.name or pet.index or "Grab")
if type(pet.mutation) == "string" and pet.mutation ~= "" then
n = pet.mutation .. " " .. n
end
return n
end
if type(_G.Elite_HoldingName) == "string" and _G.Elite_HoldingName ~= "" then
return _G.Elite_HoldingName
end
return "Grab"
end
local function stealBarShow()
if _barOn or not _barFill then return end
_barOn = true
_barGen += 1
local gen = _barGen
if _G.Elite_StealBarName then
_G.Elite_StealBarName.Text = stealName()
end
_barFill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1)
_barGui.Enabled = true
local tw = TweenService:Create(_barFill, TweenInfo.new(STEAL_HOLD_DURATION, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1) })
_barTw = tw
tw:Play()
task.delay(STEAL_HOLD_DURATION, function()
if gen == _barGen then stealBarHide() end
end)
end
local function buildStealCallbacks(prompt)
if InternalStealCache[prompt] then return end
if not prompt or not prompt.Parent then return end
local data = { holdCallbacks = {}, triggerCallbacks = {}, holdEndCallbacks = {}, ready = true }
local function grab(sig, into)
local ok, conns = pcall(getconnections, sig)
if ok and type(conns) == "table" then
for _, c in ipairs(conns) do
if type(c.Function) == "function" then table.insert(into, c.Function) end
end
end
end
grab(prompt.PromptButtonHoldBegan, data.holdCallbacks)
grab(prompt.Triggered, data.triggerCallbacks)
grab(prompt.PromptButtonHoldEnded, data.holdEndCallbacks)
if #data.holdCallbacks > 0 or #data.triggerCallbacks > 0 or #data.holdEndCallbacks > 0 then
InternalStealCache[prompt] = data
end
end
local function executeStealAsync(prompt)
local data = InternalStealCache[prompt]
if not data or not data.ready then return false end
data.ready = false
_stealHoldStart = tick()
_stealHoldActive = true
stealBarShow()
task.spawn(function()
for _, fn in ipairs(data.holdCallbacks) do task.spawn(fn) end
pcall(function()
local _st = prompt:GetAttribute("State")
if _st ~= nil and _st ~= "Steal" then
-- EXACT COCOX RECHARGE SYSTEM: the prompt is not in the "Steal" state --
-- it is recharging. Resolve the game's own recharge remote (hashed Net key,
-- plain FindFirstChild) and fire it with a future timestamp (+124s) so the
-- recharge completes -- exactly as the CocoX engine does. A miss costs three
-- FindFirstChild calls and retries on the next steal instead of latching a
-- stub forever.
if not _G.__AYDN_RechargeRemote or not _G.__AYDN_RechargeRemote.Parent then
local _r = nil
pcall(function()
local _pkg = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Packages")
local _net = _pkg and _pkg:FindFirstChild("Net")
_r = _net and _net:FindFirstChild("f40f7d9e-2f0d-4167-b250-899273f46874")
end)
if _r and _r:IsA("RemoteEvent") then _G.__AYDN_RechargeRemote = _r end
end
local _rr = _G.__AYDN_RechargeRemote
if _rr then
local _t = workspace:GetServerTimeNow() + 124
_rr:FireServer(_t, "68c86eb7-eb7e-4b4d-96ae-cf7cd847c5b0")
_rr:FireServer(_t, "07b9cc25-2a1f-4a26-a0ec-f2fab578d8bd")
end
end
end)
-- EXACT COCOX: effective hold before firing the trigger that completes the
-- steal. Live-tunable via _G.AYDN_StealHoldDuration (default 1.3 = the game's
-- normal timing). Kept SEPARATE from STEAL_HOLD_DURATION so the bar/GUI is
-- unaffected by it.
local _holdDur = tonumber(_G.AYDN_StealHoldDuration) or STEAL_HOLD_DURATION
local remain = _holdDur - (tick() - _stealHoldStart)
if remain > 0 then task.wait(remain) end
if prompt and prompt.Parent then
for _, fn in ipairs(data.triggerCallbacks) do task.spawn(fn) end
end
for _, fn in ipairs(data.holdEndCallbacks) do task.spawn(fn) end
_stealHoldActive = false
stealBarHide()
task.wait(0.05)
data.ready = true
end)
return true
end
-- AYDN (user rule): FINISH THE PROMPT -- a real hold that runs ALL the way to
-- the end. If the game's bar is already partway (in the middle) this keeps the
-- prompt pressed instead of restarting it: InputHoldBegin -> hold until the
-- steal actually lands (Stealing flips true) or the window expires ->
-- InputHoldEnd. Shared by BOTH tabs -- this grab never looks at the mode.
local function holdPromptToFinish(prompt)
if not prompt or not prompt.Parent then return false end
if _stealHoldActive then return false end
if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then return false end
local hd = tonumber(prompt.HoldDuration) or 0
if hd <= 0 then
local fpp = fireproximityprompt or _G.fireproximityprompt
if fpp then pcall(fpp, prompt) end
pcall(function() prompt:InputHoldBegin() end)
task.wait(0.1)
pcall(function() prompt:InputHoldEnd() end)
return true
end
_stealHoldStart = tick()
_stealHoldActive = true
stealBarShow()
pcall(function() prompt:InputHoldBegin() end)
local t0 = os.clock()
local limit = math.max(hd + 1.5, 3)
while prompt.Parent and (os.clock() - t0) < limit do
if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then break end
RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
end
pcall(function() prompt:InputHoldEnd() end)
_stealHoldActive = false
stealBarHide()
return true
end

-- AYDN x TWILIGHT GRAB (ported from the twilight hub steal engine)
--   * resolves the game's own Steal Begin/Grab remotes from Packages/Net
--   * fires "Begin" with a BACKDATED timestamp (server thinks the hold
--     started 1.6s ago), waits GrabMin (0.55s), then fires "Grab" with the
--     completed timestamp -> instant legal-looking grab, no hold needed
--   * falls back to the prompt callback path (getconnections) when the
--     remotes are not present on this server
-- ===========================================================================
local TW_GRAB_MIN = 0.55
local TW_BEGIN_BACK = 1.6
local TW_GRAB_AHEAD = 2.1
local TW_STEAL_SETS = {
{ Begin = "b77dde61-dd74-4f18-b329-2ac7f7e48f0b", Grab = "0c1792e6-3df9-46fd-8b46-be6e4e6df08f",
BeginArgs = { { 79, "94fa9dee-cfa2-4cc2-ac2a-0659e5169fac" }, { 79, "173cd914-bfdd-4c77-80bc-86864bd1ecb4" } },
GrabArgs = { { 141, "859270da-f6e7-4110-af2e-4c58fa259c75" }, { 141, "b231811b-fa22-4265-81fe-424ea71ddb92" } } },
{ Begin = "f1a4d3a5-b97a-4622-8fd6-232663ff697e", Grab = "f5f26af9-cb1b-4588-bcc9-12fd78f64599",
BeginArgs = { { 50, "ba10cc43-8b93-4b7d-93b8-e1935730ebe3" }, { 50, "ed327974-0c39-423a-b9c7-467b8fdac46d" } },
GrabArgs = { { 16, "7aae3e74-d52e-433a-83b1-9d5a507d2e80" }, { 16, "c3eb316e-a4cc-4dc9-8b0e-1a770a7d99fd" } } },
}
local _twSet, _twBegin, _twGrab, _twTried = nil, nil, nil, 0
local function resolveStealRemotes(force)
if not force and _twBegin and _twBegin.Parent and _twGrab and _twGrab.Parent then
return _twSet, _twBegin, _twGrab
end
if not force and _twTried > 0 and os.clock() - _twTried < 5 then
return _twSet, _twBegin, _twGrab
end
_twTried = os.clock()
_twSet, _twBegin, _twGrab = nil, nil, nil
local ok = pcall(function()
local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local pkgs = rs:FindFirstChild("Packages")
local net = pkgs and pkgs:FindFirstChild("Net")
if not net then return end
for _, set in ipairs(TW_STEAL_SETS) do
local b = net:FindFirstChild("RE/" .. set.Begin)
local gr = net:FindFirstChild("RE/" .. set.Grab)
if b and gr and b:IsA("RemoteEvent") and gr:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
_twSet, _twBegin, _twGrab = set, b, gr
return
end
end
end)
if not ok then _twSet, _twBegin, _twGrab = nil, nil, nil end
return _twSet, _twBegin, _twGrab
end
local function twFire(rem, args, stamp, plotName, slotNum)
if not rem or not rem.Parent then return end
for _, pair in ipairs(args) do
pcall(function()
if plotName then
rem:FireServer(stamp + pair[1], pair[2], plotName, slotNum)
else
rem:FireServer(stamp + pair[1], pair[2])
end
end)
end
end
-- returns true when the twilight remote grab fired (Begin + Grab)
local function twilightFireSteal(pet)
local set, bRE, gRE = resolveStealRemotes()
if not (set and bRE and gRE) then return false end
local plotName = tostring((pet and pet.plot) or "")
local slotNum = tonumber(pet and pet.slot) or 0
if plotName == "" then return false end
local now = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
twFire(bRE, set.BeginArgs, now - TW_BEGIN_BACK)
local tw0 = os.clock()
while os.clock() - tw0 < TW_GRAB_MIN do
if not _G.Elite_StealTarget or LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then break end
RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
end
local stamp2 = workspace:GetServerTimeNow() - TW_BEGIN_BACK
twFire(gRE, set.GrabArgs, stamp2 + TW_GRAB_AHEAD, plotName, slotNum)
return true
end
local _promptCache, _promptPet = nil, nil
local function findStealPrompt(pet)
if not pet then return nil end
if _promptPet and _promptCache and _promptCache.Parent
and pet.plot == _promptPet.plot and pet.slot == _promptPet.slot then
return _promptCache
end
if _G.__Elite_VelFlying then
return _promptCache
end
local found = nil
if pet.plot and pet.slot then
local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
local plot = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(pet.plot)
local podiums = plot and plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
local podium = podiums and podiums:FindFirstChild(tostring(pet.slot))
if podium then
local base = podium:FindFirstChild("Base")
local spawn = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
local attach = spawn and spawn:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
if attach then
for _, p in ipairs(attach:GetChildren()) do
if p:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then found = p; break end
end
end
if not found then
for _, d in ipairs(podium:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then found = d; break end
end
end
end
end
_promptCache, _promptPet = found, pet
if found and not found:GetAttribute("AYDN_PromptHidden") then
pcall(function()
found:SetAttribute("AYDN_PromptHidden", true)
-- AYDN (user rule): grab WITHOUT seeing the prompt (UI hidden, fires fine)
found.Style = Enum.ProximityPromptStyle.Custom
end)
end
-- AYDN (user rule): a hold that stops halfway (bar sitting in the middle) gets
-- FINISHED -- the assist keeps the prompt pressed until the steal lands
if found and not found:GetAttribute("AYDN_HoldAssist") then
pcall(function()
found:SetAttribute("AYDN_HoldAssist", true)
found.PromptButtonHoldBegan:Connect(function()
if _stealHoldActive or LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then return end
task.spawn(function()
task.wait(0.2)
local pp = _promptCache
if pp and pp.Parent and pp:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
holdPromptToFinish(pp)
end
end)
end)
end)
end
return found
end
local function _promptWorldPos(prompt, pet)
return getPromptPosition(prompt) or (pet and pet.position) or nil
end
local STEAL_ARM_TIMEOUT = 25
local _stealTarget = nil
local _stealArmedAt = 0
local _stealBlocked = false
local function _refreshBlock()
_stealBlocked = not not (LP:GetAttribute("IsTrading")
or LP:GetAttribute("IsDuelSelecting")
or LP:GetAttribute("Web"))
end
_refreshBlock()
-- EXACT COCOX manual-target rule: a manually armed target is ABSOLUTE --
-- the auto re-picker never overrides it until the disarm clears it.
local _manualPick = false
do
local holdBb, holdLbl
local function holdingText()
local pet = _stealTarget or _G.Elite_StealTarget
local n
if type(pet) == "table" then
n = tostring(pet.name or pet.index or "")
if type(pet.mutation) == "string" and pet.mutation ~= "" then
n = pet.mutation .. " " .. n
end
end
if (not n or n == "") and type(_G.Elite_HoldingName) == "string" then
n = _G.Elite_HoldingName
end
if not n or n == "" then
n = "Brainrot"
end
return "Holding (" .. n .. ")"
end
local function ensureHold()
local char = LP.Character
local head = char and (char:FindFirstChild("Head") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
local on = LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
if not head or not on then
if holdBb then
holdBb.Enabled = false
end
return
end
if not holdBb or not holdBb.Parent then
holdBb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
holdBb.Name = "EliteHoldEsp"
holdBb.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(220, 26)
holdBb.StudsOffsetWorldSpace = Vector3.new(0, 3.15, 0)
holdBb.AlwaysOnTop = true
holdBb.LightInfluence = 0
holdBb.MaxDistance = 120
holdBb.ResetOnSpawn = false
local chip = Instance.new("Frame")
chip.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
chip.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 20)
chip.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
chip.BorderSizePixel = 0
chip.Parent = holdBb
local cc = Instance.new("UICorner")
cc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
cc.Parent = chip
local cs = Instance.new("UIStroke")
cs.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
cs.Transparency = 0.86
cs.Thickness = 1
cs.Parent = chip
holdLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
holdLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
holdLbl.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
holdLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
holdLbl.TextSize = 12
holdLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(244, 246, 248)
holdLbl.Parent = chip
local parent = _barGui and _barGui.Parent
holdBb.Parent = parent or char
end
holdBb.Adornee = head
holdBb.Enabled = true
if holdLbl then
holdLbl.Text = holdingText()
end
end
LP:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
_refreshBlock()
pcall(ensureHold)
end)
LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
task.defer(function()
pcall(ensureHold)
end)
end)
task.spawn(function()
while LP and LP.Parent do
pcall(ensureHold)
task.wait(0.25)
end
end)
pcall(ensureHold)
end
LP:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(_refreshBlock)
LP:GetAttributeChangedSignal("IsTrading"):Connect(_refreshBlock)
LP:GetAttributeChangedSignal("IsDuelSelecting"):Connect(_refreshBlock)
LP:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Web"):Connect(_refreshBlock)
-- EXACT COCOX timeUntilCanSteal: trading, dueling or webbed -> cannot steal.
-- AYDN CHANGE (user rule): carrying no longer blocks here -- while you are
-- stealing one brainrot home the engine keeps CHARGING the armed target so
-- the next grab lands the moment the delivery is done.
local function timeUntilCanSteal()
if _stealBlocked then return -1 end
return 0
end
-- EXACT COCOX _pickByMode (verbatim port, adapted to AYDN's record):
-- the scan is gen-sorted, so pets[1] is the priority pick; Nearest and
-- Highest walk the list exactly like CocoX's mode branches. hrp may be an
-- HRP instance OR a bare Vector3 (CocoX passes a Vector3 while airborne so
-- "nearest" is measured from where we are going, not where we are).
local function _pickByMode(pets, hrp)
if not pets or #pets == 0 then return nil end
-- AYDN (user rule): Target mode is selection-driven -- the re-picker plays
-- "Highest" there and never snaps back to the priority list.
local _mode = (_G.AYDN_TargetMode == "Target" and "Highest")
or ((Config and Config.StealMode) or "Priority")
if _mode == "Nearest" then
local rp
if typeof(hrp) == "Vector3" then
rp = hrp
elseif typeof(hrp) == "Instance" then
rp = hrp.Position
else
local _c = LP.Character
local _h = _c and _c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
rp = _h and _h.Position
end
if rp then
local best, bd = nil, math.huge
for _, p in ipairs(pets) do
if p.position then
local d = (rp - p.position).Magnitude
if d < bd then bd, best = d, p end
end
end
if best then return best end
end
return pets[1]
end
if _mode == "Highest" then
local best, bv
for _, p in ipairs(pets) do
local v = p.mps or 0
if not bv or v > bv then bv, best = v, p end
end
return best or pets[1]
end
-- Priority (and anything else): the biggest-earner fallback -- the scan is
-- gen-sorted, so pets[1] is it, exactly as CocoX's fallback resolves.
return pets[1]
end
local function nearestPaint(on)
local b = _G.Elite_NearestBtn
if b then
b.BackgroundColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245) or Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 28)
end
end
local _nearestOn = false
local _nearestCache
local _nearestAt
local _flyStealFired = false
-- AYDN HIGHEST-LOCK (user rule): the auto grab only steals THE highest, and
-- once locked it STAYS locked -- while the target is (Stolen) it must keep
-- charging it and never fall over to the next pet. Only a NEW highest (or
-- the pet really leaving the server) may switch it. _selKey is the latch
-- fingerprint captured at arm time (pri index / rank are both "1 = best").
local _selKey = nil
local function _selPri(p)
if not p then return nil end
return p._pri or p.rank
end
local function _selStealState(pet)
if not pet or not pet.plot or not pet.slot then return false end
local okch, channel = pcall(getPlotChannel, tostring(pet.plot))
if not okch or not channel then return false end
local okl, list = pcall(channelGet, channel, "AnimalList")
if not okl or type(list) ~= "table" then return false end
for slot, ad in pairs(list) do
if type(ad) == "table" and tostring(slot) == tostring(pet.slot) then
return ad.Steal ~= nil and ad.Steal ~= false
end
end
return false
end
local function _selBetter(a, key)
if not a then return false end
if not key then return true end
local ap, bp = _selPri(a), key.pri
if (ap ~= nil) ~= (bp ~= nil) then return ap ~= nil end
if ap and bp and ap ~= bp then return ap < bp end
if ap == nil then
local am, bm = tonumber(a.mps) or 0, key.mps or 0
if am ~= bm then return am > bm end
end
return false
end
-- may `best` replace the armed `cur`? false while cur is (Stolen) and no new
-- highest appeared; true when cur is really gone or best ranks above it.
-- `pets` (optional) is the already-scanned list to search cur in first.
local function _selShouldSwitch(best, cur, pets)
if not best then return false end
if not cur then return true end
if cur.plot == best.plot and tostring(cur.slot) == tostring(best.slot) then return false end
local function _inList(list)
if type(list) ~= "table" then return false end
for i = 1, #list do
local p = list[i]
if p and p.plot == cur.plot and tostring(p.slot) == tostring(cur.slot) then return true end
end
return false
end
local curLive = _inList(pets)
if not curLive and _G.Elite_ListAllPets then
local ok, all = pcall(_G.Elite_ListAllPets)
if ok then curLive = _inList(all) end
end
if not curLive and _G.Elite_ListPrioPets then
local ok, all = pcall(_G.Elite_ListPrioPets)
if ok then curLive = _inList(all) end
end
if curLive then
return _selBetter(best, _selKey) -- visible: only a new highest wins
end
if _selStealState(cur) then
return _selBetter(best, _selKey) -- (Stolen): hold the charge unless a new highest showed up
end
return true -- truly gone from the server
end
local function armSteal(pet)
if not pet then return end
_stealTarget = pet
_G.Elite_StealTarget = pet
_selKey = { pri = _selPri(pet), mps = tonumber(pet.mps) or 0 }
do
local n = tostring(pet.name or pet.index or "")
if type(pet.mutation) == "string" and pet.mutation ~= "" then
n = pet.mutation .. " " .. n
end
if n ~= "" then
_G.Elite_HoldingName = n
end
end
_stealArmedAt = os.clock()
_flyStealFired = false
_manualPick = true -- EXACT COCOX: an armed target is manual-absolute; the auto re-picker resets this right after its own pick
findStealPrompt(pet)
if _G.Elite_OnStealTarget then
pcall(_G.Elite_OnStealTarget, pet)
end
end
local function disarmSteal()
_stealTarget = nil
_G.Elite_StealTarget = nil
_selKey = nil
_manualPick = false
_stealHoldActive = false
stealBarHide()
autoGrabWaitHide()
_promptCache, _promptPet = nil, nil
nearestPaint(false)
_nearestOn = false
_nearestCache = nil
_nearestAt = nil
if _G.Elite_OnStealTarget then
pcall(_G.Elite_OnStealTarget, nil)
end
end
-- AYDN (user rule): one shared rule for BOTH tabs -- a manually armed target
-- (user click) is ABSOLUTE until it is re-clicked (back to auto) or it leaves
-- the prio list / the server.
_G.Elite_IsManualPick = function()
return _manualPick
end
local AUTO_STEAL = (Config and Config.AutoStealEnabled) and true or false
local STEAL_MODE = (Config and Config.StealMode) or "Priority"
local lastFire = {}
local FIRE_DEBOUNCE = 0.12
local FIRE_BURST = 4
local _hrpRef
local function stealHrp()
local h = _G.__Elite_FlyHrp
if h and h.Parent then return h end
h = _hrpRef
if h and h.Parent then return h end
local char = LP.Character
h = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
_hrpRef = h
return h
end
local function isPromptAvailable(prompt, hrpPos)
if not AUTO_STEAL then return false end
if not prompt or not prompt.Parent or not prompt.Enabled then return false end
local pos = getPromptPosition(prompt)
if not pos then return false end
return (pos - hrpPos).Magnitude <= STEAL_R
end
local function canFire(prompt, debounce)
local t = os.clock()
local last = lastFire[prompt]
if last and (t - last) < debounce then return false end
lastFire[prompt] = t
return true
end
local function firePrompt(prompt, burst, debounce)
if not prompt or not prompt.Parent or not prompt.Enabled or not canFire(prompt, debounce) then return end
pcall(function()
for _ = 1, burst do fireproximityprompt(prompt, 0) end
end)
end
task.spawn(function()
while task.wait(0.12) do
if not AUTO_STEAL or _stealHoldActive or _stealBlocked or not _stealTarget then
autoGrabWaitHide()
elseif _G.__Elite_VelFlying then
autoGrabWaitHide()
else
local pet = _stealTarget
local hrp = pet and stealHrp()
local prompt = pet and hrp and findStealPrompt(pet)
local firePos = hrp and (getPromptPosition(prompt) or (pet and pet.position))
local fireDist = (hrp and firePos) and (firePos - hrp.Position).Magnitude or math.huge
if fireDist > GRAB_FIRE_DIST then
-- AYDN (user rule): shows AUTO GRABBING while waiting; the grab only
-- really happens ~4 studs in front of the pet.
autoGrabWaitShow()
elseif hrp and prompt and isPromptAvailable(prompt, hrp.Position) then
autoGrabWaitHide()
-- AYDN: give the remote pair another shot every ~30s even after failures
if _twFails >= 4 and os.clock() - _twFailAt > 30 then
_twFails = 0
end
-- AYDN x TWILIGHT: remote grab first (never only-once: this loop keeps
-- firing every tick while we are inside the grab radius). If the remotes
-- keep failing (bar stuck halfway), stop hammering them and let the real
-- prompt hold FINISH the grab.
if not _twBusy and not LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") and _twFails < 4 then
local set = resolveStealRemotes()
if set then
_twBusy = true
task.spawn(function()
stealBarShow()
pcall(twilightFireSteal, pet)
if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then
_twFails = 0
else
_twFails += 1
if _twFails >= 4 then _twFailAt = os.clock() end
end
_twBusy = false
end)
continue
end
end
-- AYDN (user rule): prompt bar in the middle = FINISH it -- run the real
-- hold all the way to the end (or the game's own callbacks when they are
-- on the prompt) instead of a fire burst that leaves the bar half-done.
-- Same grab for BOTH tabs: this loop is mode-agnostic.
task.spawn(function()
buildStealCallbacks(prompt)
if InternalStealCache[prompt] then
executeStealAsync(prompt)
else
holdPromptToFinish(prompt)
end
end)
end
end
end
end)
local _stealLastScan = 0
local isTeleporting = false
local _tpStartedAt = 0
-- =====================================================================
-- EXACT COCOX AUTO GRAB ENGINE -- the Heartbeat re-picker (verbatim port).
-- Re-scans every 0.1s and re-arms the best target via _pickByMode, frozen
-- for the whole TP flight (the TP picks its destination ONCE and arms it),
-- retargeting only when the world actually changes, and never overriding a
-- manually armed target.
-- =====================================================================
local _autoLastScan = 0
local _autoCacheVer = -1
local _autoScanCache = nil
local _autoScanAt = 0
local function _cxSamePet(a, b)
if not a or not b then return false end
return a.plot == b.plot and tostring(a.slot) == tostring(b.slot)
end
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
local now = os.clock()
local _tpInFlight = (_G.__Elite_VelFlying == true)
or (_G.__Elite_TPStarted ~= nil)
or (_G.AYDN_TP_ACTIVE == true)
or (isTeleporting and (now - _tpStartedAt) < 30)
local _ver = tonumber(_G.Elite_CacheVersion) or 0
local _plotChanged = (_ver ~= _autoCacheVer)
if _plotChanged then _autoCacheVer = _ver end
-- AYDN (user rule): the re-picker stays live while carrying -- the best
-- target stays armed and charging even while you steal one home.
if AUTO_STEAL
and not _stealBlocked
and (_plotChanged or not _tpInFlight)
and (now - _autoLastScan) >= 0.1 then
_autoLastScan = now
-- EXACT COCOX: a manually armed target is ABSOLUTE -- hands off.
if _manualPick then return end
local char = LP.Character
local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if hrp then
-- CocoX keeps a scan cache (0.2s) behind the re-picker; same contract here:
-- ONE full-server scan per 0.5s, reused between ticks. A fresh full scan
-- every 0.1s is ~1000 GetBoundingBox calls a second -- that is what stalled
-- the flight and made the TP feel dead.
if (now - _autoScanAt) >= 0.5 or type(_autoScanCache) ~= "table" then
_autoScanAt = now
local ok, pets = pcall(_G.Elite_ListAllPets or scanAllPets)
_autoScanCache = (ok and type(pets) == "table") and pets or nil
end
local pets = _autoScanCache
if pets then
-- AYDN HIGHEST-LOCK: only the highest, latched -- keep charging the
-- current one while it is (Stolen); a switch happens only when the pet
-- really left the server or a NEW highest appeared.
local best = _pickByMode(pets, hrp)
if best and not _cxSamePet(best, _stealTarget) then
if _selShouldSwitch(best, _stealTarget, pets) then
armSteal(best)
_manualPick = false -- our own pick: the re-picker stays live
end
elseif not best and _stealTarget and not _stealHoldActive then
if not _selStealState(_stealTarget) then
disarmSteal()
end
end
end
end
end
end)

-- =====================================================================
-- EXACT COCOX AUTO-GRAB FINISHER + RECHARGE RE-ARM (verbatim port).
-- Re-arms the engine a short lead before a ragdoll recovery finishes, so
-- the steal engine is already armed and clean the instant you are back on
-- your feet. WHAT THIS MUST NOT DO: touch an in-flight hold -- a running
-- hold completes by itself and forcing it would stack coroutines (the
-- restart-loop bug CocoX documented). Only when nothing is in flight does
-- this re-arm.
-- =====================================================================
_G.AYDN_GrabFinisher = function()
-- Re-enable, but never turn on something the user switched off.
if Config and Config.AutoStealEnabled then AUTO_STEAL = true end
-- A hold is running: hands off. No status writes, no disarm.
if _stealHoldActive then return false end
-- Nothing in flight: drop the stale progress and force the re-picker to
-- re-evaluate on its next Heartbeat tick.
_autoLastScan = 0
return true
end
-- AYDN ragdoll signal: RagdollEndTime is SERVER time. Fire the finisher a
-- short lead (0.4s) before recovery finishes, and immediately when the
-- attribute clears early (back on our feet before the timer ran out).
LP:GetAttributeChangedSignal("RagdollEndTime"):Connect(function()
local et = LP:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime")
if et == nil then
pcall(_G.AYDN_GrabFinisher)
return
end
if type(et) ~= "number" then return end
local _wait = (et - Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()) - 0.4
if _wait > 0 then task.wait(_wait) end
if LP:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime") == nil then
pcall(_G.AYDN_GrabFinisher)
end
end)
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if _G.__Elite_VelFlying then
if AUTO_STEAL and not _stealHoldActive and not _stealBlocked then
local pet = _stealTarget
local hrp = pet and stealHrp()
local prompt = hrp and _promptCache
-- AYDN (user rule): same 4-stud fire gate while flying
local fpos = prompt and getPromptPosition(prompt)
local fdist = (hrp and fpos) and (fpos - hrp.Position).Magnitude or math.huge
if fdist > GRAB_FIRE_DIST then
autoGrabWaitShow()
else
autoGrabWaitHide()
if hrp and prompt and fdist <= GRAB_FIRE_DIST and isPromptAvailable(prompt, hrp.Position) then
firePrompt(prompt, 1, 0.25)
end
end
end
return
end
local now = os.clock()
local pet = _stealTarget
if not pet then return end
if now - _stealLastScan < 0.067 then return end
_stealLastScan = now
if _nearestOn and _nearestCache then
local hrpN = stealHrp()
if hrpN then
local p = hrpN.Position
local at = _nearestAt
if not at then
_nearestAt = p
else
local mx, my, mz = p.X - at.X, p.Y - at.Y, p.Z - at.Z
if mx * mx + my * my + mz * mz >= 4 then
_nearestAt = p
local best, bestD = nil, math.huge
for i = 1, #_nearestCache do
local e = _nearestCache[i]
local ep = e.pos
local dx, dy, dz = ep.X - p.X, ep.Y - p.Y, ep.Z - p.Z
local d = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz
if d < bestD then
bestD, best = d, e.pet
end
end
if best and (not _stealTarget or _stealTarget.plot ~= best.plot or _stealTarget.slot ~= best.slot) then
armSteal(best)
pcall(_G.Elite_LockPrioPlot, best.plot)
end
end
end
end
end
if timeUntilCanSteal() == -1 then
return
end
if _stealHoldActive then return end
-- AYDN (user rule): while actually carrying (Stealing attribute) the engine
-- still CHARGES the armed target -- the prompt path keeps firing so the next
-- grab lands the moment the delivery is done. Only the twilight remote pair
-- stays gated to the not-carrying state (firing it mid-carry used to break
-- the delivery).
local _carrying = LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
local hrp = stealHrp()
if not hrp then return end
local prompt = findStealPrompt(pet)
if not prompt or not prompt.Parent then return end
local ppPos = _promptWorldPos(prompt, pet)
if not ppPos then return end
if (hrp.Position - ppPos).Magnitude > STEAL_R then return end
buildStealCallbacks(prompt)
-- AYDN x TWILIGHT GRAB: remote Begin/Grab pair first (run in its own thread
-- so the heartbeat never blocks), prompt callbacks as fallback. While
-- carrying, the remote pair is skipped (mid-carry remote used to break the
-- delivery) and the prompt charge keeps running instead.
if not _twBusy and not _carrying and _twFails < 4 then
local set = resolveStealRemotes()
if set then
_twBusy = true
stealBarShow()
task.spawn(function()
pcall(twilightFireSteal, pet)
if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then
_twFails = 0
else
_twFails += 1
if _twFails >= 4 then _twFailAt = os.clock() end
end
_twBusy = false
end)
return
end
end
pcall(function()
local oldMax = prompt.MaxActivationDistance
prompt.MaxActivationDistance = math.huge
prompt.HoldDuration = STEAL_HOLD_DURATION
if InternalStealCache[prompt] then
executeStealAsync(prompt)
elseif hrp and ppPos and (hrp.Position - ppPos).Magnitude <= GRAB_FIRE_DIST then
-- AYDN (user rule): the real hold only starts ~4 studs in front of the
-- pet (same gate as the approach loop) and runs until it FINISHES
holdPromptToFinish(prompt)
elseif _twFails == 0 then
-- no twilight remotes and no callbacks on this server -> executor
-- fire from range stays available (old behavior preserved)
stealBarShow()
pcall(function() fireproximityprompt(prompt, STEAL_HOLD_DURATION) end)
end
if oldMax ~= nil then prompt.MaxActivationDistance = oldMax end
end)
end)
local function preFlyStrip(plotName)
if plotName then
pcall(_G.Elite_LockPrioPlot, plotName)
end
end
local function _doVelocityTPImpl(pre)
if isTeleporting and (os.clock() - _tpStartedAt) < 30 then
return
end
isTeleporting = true
_G.__Elite_TPStarted = true
_tpStartedAt = os.clock()
-- AYDN: full TP-busy flags so NOTHING (anti-ragdoll, speed boost,
-- optimizer) resets or interrupts us while the run is going
_G.EliteIsTeleporting = true
_G.M4rcIsTeleporting = true
local function _tpDone()
isTeleporting = false
_G.__Elite_TPStarted = nil
_G.EliteIsTeleporting = false
_G.M4rcIsTeleporting = false
end
local char = LP.Character
local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not hrp or not hum then
_tpDone()
return
end
local allPets
if type(pre) == "table" and (pre.plot or pre.name or pre.index) then
allPets = { pre }
elseif type(pre) == "table" and #pre > 0 then
allPets = pre
else
allPets = scanAllPets()
if #allPets == 0 then
local _t0 = os.clock()
while #allPets == 0 and (os.clock() - _t0) < 2 do
task.wait(0.1)
allPets = scanAllPets()
end
end
end
if #allPets == 0 then
_tpDone()
return
end
local pet = _pickByMode(allPets) or allPets[1]
armSteal(pet)
pcall(_ensurePetPos, pet)
local petPos = pet.position or pet.plotHint
if not petPos then
local _t0 = os.clock()
repeat
RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
pcall(_ensurePetPos, pet)
petPos = pet.position or pet.plotHint
if not petPos then
for i = 1, #allPets do
local cand = allPets[i]
pcall(_ensurePetPos, cand)
local pos = cand.position or cand.plotHint
if pos then
pet = cand
petPos = pos
armSteal(pet)
break
end
end
end
until petPos or (os.clock() - _t0) > 2
end
local _tpSpd = _G.D3GovClamp(Config and Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.GrabbleTPSpeed)
local _closeSpd = tonumber(Config and Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.CloseBaseSpeed) or 350
if _closeSpd < 120 then _closeSpd = 120 end
if _closeSpd > 550 then _closeSpd = 550 end
local petName = pet.name
if not petPos then
_tpDone()
return
end
if petPos.Y <= 8.9 and isPlotUnlocked(pet.plot) then
-- AYDN v5: ONLY the gear steps -> pull out flying gear, activate 0.5s,
-- re-pull it out. No grapple, no tween, no velocity fly.
pcall(function()
if type(_G.AYDN_GearPulse) == "function" then _G.AYDN_GearPulse()
elseif _G.Elite_EquipCarpet then _G.Elite_EquipCarpet() end
end)
local _to = Vector3.new(petPos.X, FRONT_Y_LOW, petPos.Z)
local _faceDir
do
local idx = getClosestBaseIdx(petPos)
local _, frontFace = buildFrontCandidate(idx, false, hrp.Position.Z)
_faceDir = frontFace
end
preFlyStrip(pet.plot)
local route = computeRoute(hrp.Position, _to, _faceDir)
if not route or #route == 0 then route = { _to } end
-- AYDN v5: walk the route with YOUR walking velocity (no reset)
walkMoveThrough(hrp, route)
pcall(function() if hrp and hrp.Parent then hrp.Anchored = false end end)
if hrp and hrp.Parent then
hrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero
hrp.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
end
_tpDone()
return
end
-- AYDN v5: ONLY the gear steps -> pull out flying gear, activate 0.5s,
-- re-pull it out. No grapple, no tween, no velocity fly.
pcall(function()
if type(_G.AYDN_GearPulse) == "function" then _G.AYDN_GearPulse()
elseif _G.Elite_EquipCarpet then _G.Elite_EquipCarpet() end
end)
local destPos, facingDir = resolveCloneDest(hrp.Position, petPos, petName)
if facingDir and facingDir.Magnitude > 0.1 then
destPos = wallFrontPos(destPos, facingDir)
end
preFlyStrip(pet.plot)
_G.__Elite_VelFlying = true
local _route = computeRoute(hrp.Position, destPos, facingDir)
if not _route or #_route == 0 then _route = { destPos } end
local _stepped = {}
do
local startY = hrp.Position.Y
local destY = destPos.Y
local prev = hrp.Position
local totalFlat = 0
for _, wp in ipairs(_route) do
totalFlat = totalFlat + Vector3.new(wp.X - prev.X, 0, wp.Z - prev.Z).Magnitude
prev = wp
end
if totalFlat < 0.01 then totalFlat = 0.01 end
prev = hrp.Position
local travelled = 0
for _, wp in ipairs(_route) do
travelled = travelled + Vector3.new(wp.X - prev.X, 0, wp.Z - prev.Z).Magnitude
local rampY = startY + (destY - startY) * (travelled / totalFlat)
_stepped[#_stepped + 1] = Vector3.new(wp.X, rampY, wp.Z)
prev = wp
end
if #_stepped > 0 then _stepped[#_stepped] = destPos end
end
-- AYDN v5: walk the route with YOUR walking velocity (no reset, no tween)
walkMoveThrough(hrp, _stepped)
pcall(function()
if hrp and hrp.Parent then
hrp.Anchored = false
hrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero
hrp.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
end
end)
local look = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
if facingDir and facingDir.Magnitude > 0.1 then
look = Vector3.new(facingDir.X, 0, facingDir.Z)
if look.Magnitude < 0.1 then look = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) else look = look.Unit end
end
local hitName
destPos, hitName = wallFrontPos(destPos, facingDir)
do
local into = 0.45
local ln = string.lower(hitName or "")
if string.find(ln, "laser", 1, true) then into = 0 end
destPos = destPos + look * into
end
hrp.Anchored = false
local function _faceLook()
if look.Magnitude > 0.1 and hrp and hrp.Parent then
hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + look)
end
end
_faceLook()
vZero(hrp)
local _stopFall = _antiFall(hrp, destPos)
armSteal(pet)
pcall(function() if hrp and hrp.Parent then hrp.Anchored = false end end)
-- AYDN (user rule): clone delay 0.00 = INSTANT -- the lower of the two
-- delay sliders wins, so a 0.00 Clone Delay never waits before the cloner.
local _landDelay = math.min(
tonumber(Config and Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.LandingDelayVal) or 0,
tonumber(Config and Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.CloneDelayVal) or 0)
if _landDelay > 0 then
task.wait(_landDelay)
end
pcall(function() if hrp and hrp.Parent then hrp.Anchored = false end end)
local _cloneOk = doClone()
if _stopFall then pcall(_stopFall, false) end
if _cloneOk then
_G.isCloning = false
-- AYDN: wait till the clone is actually IN (character swap settles)
task.wait(0.3)
-- AYDN: clone in -> pull the flying gear back out, then go to brainrot
pcall(function()
if type(_G.AYDN_GearPulse) == "function" then _G.AYDN_GearPulse()
elseif _G.Elite_EquipCarpet then _G.Elite_EquipCarpet() end
end)
goToBrainrot(petPos)
end
_tpDone()
end
function doVelocityTP(pre)
pcall(_doVelocityTPImpl, pre)
end
_G.Elite_DoVelocityTP = function(pre)
pcall(_doVelocityTPImpl, pre)
end
-- original kept as _G.Elite_DoVelocityTP_Orig if needed later
_G.Elite_DoVelocityTP_Orig = doVelocityTP
-- AYDN: STEAL NEAREST = steal the NEAREST BRAINROT (any brainrot on any
-- other base, not just priority names). Cache is kept so the heartbeat
-- keeps re-arming the closest one as you move.
local function stealNearestPrio()
local char = LP.Character
local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not hrp then return end
local origin = hrp.Position
local cache = {}
local best, bestD = nil, math.huge
local isKey = _G.Elite_IsPlotChanKey
local reg = _G.eliteSyncAll and _G.eliteSyncAll()
if type(reg) ~= "table" then return end
for name, ch in next, reg do
if not (isKey and isKey(name)) then continue end
if not ch or isMyPlot(ch) then continue end
local list = channelGet(ch, "AnimalList")
if type(list) ~= "table" then continue end
for slot, animalData in pairs(list) do
if type(animalData) ~= "table" then continue end
local animalName = animalData.Index
if type(animalName) ~= "string" then continue end
if _eliteIsFusing(animalData) then continue end
-- stolen/locked animals can never be grabbed -> skip them
if animalData.Steal ~= nil and animalData.Steal ~= false then continue end
local pet = {
name = animalName, index = animalName,
mutation = _adMut(animalData),
plot = tostring(name), slot = tostring(slot),
}
_ensurePetPos(pet)
local pos = pet.position or pet.plotHint
if not pos then continue end
cache[#cache + 1] = { pet = pet, pos = pos }
local d = (pos - origin).Magnitude
if d < bestD then
bestD, best = d, pet
end
end
end
if not best then
return
end
_nearestCache = cache
_nearestAt = origin
_nearestOn = true
armSteal(best)
pcall(_G.Elite_LockPrioPlot, best.plot)
nearestPaint(true)
end
_G.Elite_StealNearestPrio = stealNearestPrio
-- AYDN LAG FIX: one shared 0.5s cache. The Targets GUI loop (every 0.4s) and
-- the PRIORITY INSTANT LOCK loop (every 0.5s) each walked the whole sync
-- registry before -- now both read this one cached list, so the walk runs at
-- most twice a second no matter how many priorities are on the server. A
-- brand-new priority is still picked up within the same 0.5s as before.
local _prioListCache, _prioListAt = nil, 0
local function listAllPrioPets()
local _nowC = os.clock()
if _prioListCache and (_nowC - _prioListAt) < 0.5 then
return _prioListCache
end
local out = {}
local isKey = _G.Elite_IsPlotChanKey
local reg = _G.eliteSyncAll and _G.eliteSyncAll()
if type(reg) ~= "table" then return out end
for name, ch in next, reg do
if not (isKey and isKey(name)) then continue end
if not ch or isMyPlot(ch) then continue end
local list = channelGet(ch, "AnimalList")
if type(list) ~= "table" then continue end
for slot, animalData in pairs(list) do
if type(animalData) ~= "table" then continue end
local animalName = animalData.Index
if type(animalName) ~= "string" then continue end
local rank = _prioRank[_nameKey(animalName)]
if not rank then continue end
if _eliteIsFusing(animalData) then continue end
local mut = _adMut(animalData)
local pet = {
name = animalName,
index = animalName,
mutation = mut,
plot = tostring(name),
slot = tostring(slot),
rank = rank,
mutRank = _mutRank(mut),
}
_ensurePetPos(pet)
out[#out + 1] = pet
end
end
table.sort(out, function(a, b)
if a.rank ~= b.rank then return a.rank < b.rank end
if a.mutRank ~= b.mutRank then return a.mutRank < b.mutRank end
return tostring(a.slot) < tostring(b.slot)
end)
_prioListCache, _prioListAt = out, os.clock()
return out
end
_G.Elite_ListPrioPets = listAllPrioPets
local SWITCH_TWEEN_SPEED = 350
local _switchGen = 0
local _switchTw
local _switchHold
local function _petDest(pet, hrp)
if not (pet and hrp) then return nil end
pcall(_ensurePetPos, pet)
local petPos = pet.position or pet.plotHint
if not petPos then return nil end
local h = petPos.Y
local targetY = hrp.Position.Y
if h > 23.15 then targetY = 21
elseif h >= 11 and h <= 23.15 then targetY = 14.5
elseif h >= -6.9 and h <= 8.9 then targetY = -4 end
return Vector3.new(petPos.X, targetY, petPos.Z), petPos
end
local function _stopSwitchMove()
if _switchHold then
pcall(function() _switchHold:Disconnect() end)
_switchHold = nil
end
if _switchTw then
pcall(function() _switchTw:Cancel() end)
_switchTw = nil
end
local char = LP.Character
local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if hrp then
pcall(function()
hrp.Anchored = false
hrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero
hrp.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
end)
end
end
local function switchStealTarget(pet)
if not pet then return end
-- AYDN LAG FIX: already armed on THIS exact pet -> do nothing. The priority
-- instant lock and the auto re-picker used to re-arm the same pet over and
-- over; every re-arm ran findStealPrompt + the FULL Targets GUI rebuild, so
-- each priority that joined made that per-tick rebuild heavier. A real
-- switch (different plot+slot) still re-arms instantly as before.
do
local cur = _G.Elite_StealTarget
if cur and cur.plot == pet.plot and tostring(cur.slot) == tostring(pet.slot) then
return
end
end
armSteal(pet)
-- AYDN: selecting a new target cancels STEAL NEAREST mode so the
-- heartbeat can never fight the new lock
_nearestOn = false
_nearestCache = nil
_nearestAt = nil
pcall(_G.Elite_LockPrioPlot, pet.plot)
-- AYDN: no auto tween/TP on target select; only arms steal for that one pet
return
end
-- AYDN (user rule): re-clicking the ALREADY-SELECTED brainrot drops the
-- manual pick and goes back to AUTO -- arm the mode's best (Target = highest
-- in server, Priority = top prio) and leave the re-picker live again.
_G.Elite_ArmHighest = function()
local ok, pets = pcall(_G.Elite_ListAllPets or scanAllPets)
if not ok or type(pets) ~= "table" or #pets == 0 then return nil end
local best = _pickByMode(pets, nil)
if not best then return nil end
pcall(_ensurePetPos, best)
armSteal(best)
_manualPick = false -- our own pick: the re-picker stays live (auto mode)
return best
end
_G.Elite_SwitchStealTarget = switchStealTarget
_G.Elite_GoToBrainrot = goToBrainrot
end

}

-- sync from the MAIN GUI settings (Config.TpSettings, saved by EliteSaveSettings)
local function syncConfig()
    local c = (type(Config) == "table" and Config) or _G.Config
    local tp = c and c.TpSettings
    if type(tp) ~= "table" then return end
    CONFIG.TPSpeed = tonumber(tp.AydnTPSpeed) or CONFIG.TPSpeed
    CONFIG.CloseSpeed = tonumber(tp.AydnCloseSpeed) or CONFIG.CloseSpeed
    CONFIG.CloseDistance = tonumber(tp.AydnCloseDist) or CONFIG.CloseDistance
    CONFIG.CloneDelay = tonumber(tp.AydnCloneDelay) or CONFIG.CloneDelay
    CONFIG.AutoGrab = (tp.AydnAutoGrab ~= false)
end

local running = false

local function clamp(v, lo, hi) return math.max(lo, math.min(hi, v)) end
local function ping() return clamp(LOCAL_PLAYER:GetNetworkPing(), 0.03, 0.4) end

-- ===========================================================================
-- MPS PHRASES: short number formatting for display (6000 -> "6k", 1.5e6 -> "1.5m")
-- ===========================================================================
local function mpsFmt(v)
    v = tonumber(v) or 0
    local sign = (v < 0) and "-" or ""
    local n = math.abs(v)
    local function trim(s)
        s = s:gsub("0+$", "")
        s = s:gsub("%.$", "")
        return s
    end
    if n >= 1e15 then
        return sign .. trim(string.format("%.2f", n / 1e15)) .. "q"
    elseif n >= 1e12 then
        return sign .. trim(string.format("%.2f", n / 1e12)) .. "t"
    elseif n >= 1e9 then
        return sign .. trim(string.format("%.2f", n / 1e9)) .. "b"
    elseif n >= 1e6 then
        return sign .. trim(string.format("%.2f", n / 1e6)) .. "m"
    elseif n >= 1e3 then
        return sign .. trim(string.format("%.2f", n / 1e3)) .. "k"
    end
    return sign .. trim(string.format("%.1f", n))
end

-- ===========================================================================
-- Character helpers
