local function getChar() return LOCAL_PLAYER.Character end
local function getHumanoid()
    local c = getChar()
    return c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
end
local function getHRP()
    local c = getChar()
    return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end
local function getParts()
    local c = getChar()
    if not c then return nil, nil, nil end
    return c, c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
end
local function unequipAll()
    local h = getHumanoid()
    if h then pcall(function() h:UnequipTools() end) end
end
local function vZero(hrp)
    if not hrp then return end
    pcall(function()
        hrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero
        hrp.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end)
end

-- ===========================================================================
-- Tool matching
-- ===========================================================================
local BROOM_PATTERNS  = {"witchs broom", "witch broom", "witchbroom", "witch's broom"}
local CLONER_PATTERNS = {"quantumcloner", "quantum cloner", "quantum_cloner"}
local function nameNorm(s)
    return (s or ""):lower():gsub("'", ""):gsub("'", ""):gsub(" ", ""):gsub("_", ""):gsub("-", "")
end
local function _normName(s)
    return tostring(s):lower():gsub("[%s%-_'%.]", "")
end
local function toolMatches(tool, patterns)
    if not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") then return false end
    local n = nameNorm(tool.Name)
    for _, p in ipairs(patterns) do
        if n:find(nameNorm(p)) then return true end
    end
    return false
end
local function isBroom(tool)  return toolMatches(tool, BROOM_PATTERNS) end
local function isCloner(tool) return toolMatches(tool, CLONER_PATTERNS) end
local function findToolInContainer(container, matcher)
    if not container then return nil end
    for _, t in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
        if matcher(t) then return t end
    end
    return nil
end
local function findClonerTool()
    local char = getChar()
    local backpack = LOCAL_PLAYER:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
    return findToolInContainer(char, isCloner) or findToolInContainer(backpack, isCloner)
end
local function equipTool(matcher, timeout)
    timeout = timeout or 4
    unequipAll()
    task.wait(0.1)
    local backpack = LOCAL_PLAYER:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
    if not backpack then return nil end
    local startT = os.clock()
    while os.clock() - startT < timeout do
        local char = getChar()
        local humanoid = getHumanoid()
        if char then
            local t = findToolInContainer(char, matcher)
            if t then return t end
        end
        unequipAll()
        task.wait(0.06)
        for _, tool in ipairs(backpack:GetChildren()) do
            if matcher(tool) and humanoid then
                pcall(function() humanoid:EquipTool(tool) end)
                return tool
            end
        end
        task.wait(0.1)
    end
    return nil
end
local function equipWitchBroom()    return equipTool(isBroom, 4) end
local function activateBroomFor(dur)
    -- HOLD ONLY (user spec): nothing may Activate outside the 1s GO pulse.
    -- This helper has no callers anymore; it now just holds the broom.
    local char = getChar()
    if not char then return end
    local tool = findToolInContainer(char, isBroom)
    if not tool then return end
    task.wait(dur)
end
-- ===========================================================================
-- AYDN GEAR OF CHOICE (user TP sequence):
--   pull out the flying gear picked in AYDN SETTINGS -> activate it for
--   0.5 seconds -> re-pull it out (no second activation) -> GO.
-- ===========================================================================
local FLY_GEAR = { "waverider", "flying carpet", "witchs broom", "santas sleigh", "cupids wings", "skeleton dragon" }
local function gearMatchesName(tool, want)
    if type(want) ~= "string" or want == "" then return false end
    return toolMatches(tool, { want })
end
local function equipGearOfChoice(timeout)
    timeout = timeout or 3
    local want = (Config and Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.Tool) or _G.Elite_CarpetTool
    if gearMatchesName(nil, want) or type(want) == "string" then
        local t = equipTool(function(tool) return gearMatchesName(tool, want) end, timeout)
        if t then return t end
    end
    return equipTool(function(tool)
        for _, g in ipairs(FLY_GEAR) do
            if toolMatches(tool, { g }) then return true end
        end
        return false
    end, timeout)
end
local function gearPulseSequence()
	-- YOUR SPEC: pull out the selected flying gear -> ACTIVATE it for 1
	-- second -> UNEQUIP -> re-pull it out (JUST HOLDING it, NO activation)
	-- -> GO. The re-pull is hold-only: it must never fire Activate again.
	local ok, tool = pcall(equipGearOfChoice, 3)
	if not ok or not tool then return false end
	local char = getChar()
	local t0 = os.clock()
	while os.clock() - t0 < 1 and (not char or not char:FindFirstChild(tool.Name)) do
		RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
		char = getChar()
	end
	pcall(function() tool:Activate() end) -- ACTIVATE IT
	local t1 = os.clock() + 1 -- YOUR spec: activate for 1 second
	while os.clock() < t1 do RunService.Heartbeat:Wait() end
	unequipAll()
	task.wait(0.05)
	pcall(equipGearOfChoice, 1.5) -- re-pull out, JUST HOLDING IT, no activation
	return true
end
_G.AYDN_GearPulse = gearPulseSequence

-- ===========================================================================
-- Under-character platform + transparent landing platform
local PLATFORM_NAME = "AYDNTempPlatform"
local function destroyAllPlatforms()
    for _, c in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
        if c.Name == PLATFORM_NAME or c.Name == "AYDNLandingPlatform" then
            pcall(function() c:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
end
-- EXACT COCOX CLONE PLATFORM (doVelocityTP, verbatim sizes + one-way logic):
-- a 12x1x12 invisible one-way platform under the body while the clone is
-- placed and until the swap lands. cxCloneOneWay is CocoX's makeOneWay
-- verbatim (Stepped, velocity/fall-direction decides collision).
local __ClonePlat = nil
local function cxCloneOneWay(plat)
    if not plat then return end
    local rsConn
    local lastY = nil
    rsConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
        if not plat or not plat.Parent then
            if rsConn then rsConn:Disconnect() end
            return
        end
        local char = LOCAL_PLAYER.Character
        local chrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if chrp then
            local currentY = chrp.Position.Y
            if not lastY then lastY = currentY end
            local deltaY = currentY - lastY
            local isMovingUp = (chrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y > 1) or (deltaY > 0.01 and deltaY < 5)
            if isMovingUp then
                plat.CanCollide = false
            else
                if currentY > plat.Position.Y + 0.1 then
                    plat.CanCollide = true
                else
                    plat.CanCollide = false
                end
            end
            lastY = currentY
        end
    end)
end
local function destroyClonePlatform()
    if __ClonePlat then
        pcall(function() __ClonePlat:Destroy() end)
        __ClonePlat = nil
    end
end
local function spawnClonePlatform(clonePos)
    destroyClonePlatform()
    local plat = Instance.new("Part")
    plat.Name = "AYDN_ClonePlatform"
    plat.Size = Vector3.new(12, 1, 12)
    plat.Position = Vector3.new(clonePos.X, clonePos.Y - 3, clonePos.Z)
    plat.Anchored = true
    plat.CanCollide = false
    pcall(cxCloneOneWay, plat)
    plat.Transparency = 1
    plat.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
    plat.Parent = Workspace
    __ClonePlat = plat
    -- 25s safety life so a stopped run can never leak the platform
    task.spawn(function()
        local _s = os.clock()
        while __ClonePlat == plat and (os.clock() - _s) < 25 do task.wait(0.1) end
        if __ClonePlat == plat then destroyClonePlatform() end
    end)
    return plat
end
local function spawnPlatformAt(position)
    destroyAllPlatforms()
    local plat = Instance.new("Part")
    plat.Name = PLATFORM_NAME
    plat.Size = CONFIG.PlatformSize
    plat.Position = position
    plat.Color = Color3.new(1, 0, 0)
    plat.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
    plat.Anchored = true
    plat.CanCollide = true
    plat.Transparency = 0.3
    plat.CastShadow = false
    plat.Parent = Workspace
    task.spawn(function()
        local t0 = os.clock()
        while plat.Parent and (os.clock() - t0) < CONFIG.PlatformLife do task.wait(0.5) end
        pcall(function() plat:Destroy() end)
    end)
    return plat
end

-- Transparent landing platform: spawns at pos, character is dropped onto its top.
local function spawnLandingPlatform(pos)
    for _, c in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
        if c.Name == "AYDNLandingPlatform" then pcall(function() c:Destroy() end) end
    end
    local plat = Instance.new("Part")
    plat.Name = "AYDNLandingPlatform"
    plat.Size = Vector3.new(14, 1, 14)
    plat.Position = Vector3.new(pos.X, pos.Y - 3, pos.Z)
    plat.Anchored = true
    plat.CanCollide = true
    plat.Transparency = 1
    plat.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
    plat.CastShadow = false
    plat.Parent = Workspace
    task.delay(10, function() if plat and plat.Parent then plat:Destroy() end end)
    return plat
end
-- landCharacterOn: spawn platform under pos, CFrame the character onto the platform top, vZero.
local function landCharacterOn(pos, faceDir)
    local hrp = getHRP()
    if not hrp then return nil end
    local plat = spawnLandingPlatform(pos)
    local top = Vector3.new(pos.X, pos.Y, pos.Z)
    pcall(function()
        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(top, top + (faceDir or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)))
    end)
    vZero(hrp)
    -- pin for a couple frames so the server agrees
    for _ = 1, 3 do
        RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
        if hrp and hrp.Parent then
            pcall(function() hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(top, top + (faceDir or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1))) end)
            vZero(hrp)
        end
    end
    return plat
end

-- ===========================================================================
-- DETECTION SYSTEM (ported from sticky's leaked hub)
-- ===========================================================================
local _AD, _MD, _TD
local function loadDataModules()
    if _AD then return true end
    local ok = pcall(function()
        local d = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Datas") or ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Datas", 2)
        if not d then return end
        local a = d:FindFirstChild("Animals") or d:WaitForChild("Animals", 2)
        if a then _AD = require(a) end
        local m = d:FindFirstChild("Mutations") or d:WaitForChild("Mutations", 2)
        if m then _MD = require(m) end
        local t = d:FindFirstChild("Traits") or d:WaitForChild("Traits", 2)
        if t then _TD = require(t) end
    end)
    return ok and _AD ~= nil
end
local function _StickyGen(index, mutation, traits)
    if not loadDataModules() then return 0 end
    local info = _AD[index]
    if not info or not info.Generation then return 0 end
    local mult = 1
    if mutation and mutation ~= "None" and mutation ~= "" then
        local m = _MD[mutation]
        if m and m.Modifier then mult = mult + m.Modifier end
    end
    if type(traits) == "table" then
        for _, tr in ipairs(traits) do
            local t = _TD[tr]
            if t and t.MultiplierModifier then mult = mult + t.MultiplierModifier end
        end
    end
    return info.Generation * mult
end
local _sharedAnimals
local AnimalsShared = setmetatable({ GetGeneration = function(_, index, mutation, traits)
    return _StickyGen(index, mutation, traits)
end }, {
    __index = function(_, k)
        if not _sharedAnimals then
            local ok, real = pcall(function()
                local sh = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Shared") or ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared", 3)
                local an = sh and (sh:FindFirstChild("Animals") or sh:WaitForChild("Animals", 3))
                return an and require(an) or nil
            end)
            if ok and type(real) == "table" then _sharedAnimals = real end
        end
        if not _sharedAnimals then return nil end
        return rawget(_sharedAnimals, k)
    end,
})

local Synchronizer
local function loadSynchronizer()
    if Synchronizer then return end
    pcall(function()
        local pkgs = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Packages") or ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Packages", 8)
        if pkgs then Synchronizer = require(pkgs:WaitForChild("Synchronizer")) end
    end)
end
local function getPlotChannel(plotName)
    if not Synchronizer then return nil end
    local ok, t = pcall(function() return Synchronizer:GetTableFromChannel(plotName) end)
    if ok and type(t) == "table" then return t end
    return nil
end
local function channelGet(channel, key)
    if not channel then return nil end
    local v
    pcall(function()
        local ct = rawget(channel, "CacheTable")
        if type(ct) == "table" then
            local r = rawget(ct, key)
            if r ~= nil then v = r return end
        end
        local d = rawget(channel, "Data")
        if type(d) == "table" then
            local r = rawget(d, key)
            if r ~= nil then v = r return end
        end
        v = rawget(channel, key)
    end)
    return v
end
local function _ownerIsMe(owner)
    if not owner then return false end
    local result = false
    pcall(function()
        if typeof(owner) == "Instance" and owner:IsA("Player") then
            result = owner.UserId == LOCAL_PLAYER.UserId
        elseif type(owner) == "table" and owner.UserId then
            result = owner.UserId == LOCAL_PLAYER.UserId
        elseif typeof(owner) == "Instance" then
            result = owner == LOCAL_PLAYER
        elseif type(owner) == "string" then
            result = owner:lower() == LOCAL_PLAYER.Name:lower()
                or owner:lower() == (LOCAL_PLAYER.DisplayName or LOCAL_PLAYER.Name):lower()
        end
    end)
    return result
end
local function _ownerInGame(owner)
    if not owner then return false end
    local inGame = false
    pcall(function()
        if typeof(owner) == "Instance" and owner:IsA("Player") then
            inGame = owner.Parent == Players
        elseif type(owner) == "number" then
            inGame = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(owner) ~= nil
        elseif type(owner) == "table" and owner.Name then
            inGame = Players:FindFirstChild(tostring(owner.Name)) ~= nil
        elseif typeof(owner) == "Instance" and owner.Name then
            inGame = Players:FindFirstChild(owner.Name) ~= nil
        elseif type(owner) == "string" then
            inGame = Players:FindFirstChild(owner) ~= nil
        end
    end)
    return inGame
end
local _BLOCKING_MACHINE_TYPES = { Fuse = true, Duel = true, Trade = true, Crafting = true }
local function _StickyIsFusing(animalData)
    if type(animalData) ~= "table" then return false end
    local m = animalData.Machine
    if type(m) ~= "table" then return false end
    return _BLOCKING_MACHINE_TYPES[m.Type] == true
end
local function resolvePodium(plotModel, slotKey)
    if not plotModel then return nil, nil, nil end
    local podiums = plotModel:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
    if not podiums then return nil, nil, nil end
    local slotModel = podiums:FindFirstChild(tostring(slotKey))
    if not slotModel then return nil, nil, nil end
    local base = slotModel:FindFirstChild("Base")
    if not base then return nil, slotModel, nil end
    local spawn = base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
    if not spawn then return nil, slotModel, nil end
    local att = spawn:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
    local prompt = att and att:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt")
    return spawn, slotModel, prompt
end
local function getPetPosition(plot, slot)
    local spawnPart = resolvePodium(plot, slot)
    return spawnPart and spawnPart.Position or nil
end
local function getPetPrompt(plot, slot)
    local _, _, prompt = resolvePodium(plot, slot)
    return prompt
end
local _MPS_SUFFIX = { K = 1e3, M = 1e6, B = 1e9, T = 1e12, Q = 1e15 }
local _wmpsCache, _wmpsAt = {}, {}
local function _worldMPS(plot, slot)
    local podiums = plot and plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
    local podium = podiums and podiums:FindFirstChild(tostring(slot))
    if not podium then return nil end
    local _ck = plot.Name .. "\0" .. tostring(slot)
    local _now = os.clock()
    if _wmpsAt[_ck] and (_now - _wmpsAt[_ck]) < 1 then return _wmpsCache[_ck] end
    local best
    local function _scanLabels(parent)
        for _, child in ipairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("TextLabel") or child:IsA("TextButton") then
                local txt = child.Text
                if type(txt) == "string" and txt ~= "" then
                    local num, suf = txt:match("%$%s*([%d%.]+)%s*([KMBTQ]?)%s*/s")
                    if num then
                        local val = tonumber(num)
                        if val then
                            val = val * (_MPS_SUFFIX[suf] or 1)
                            if not best or val > best then best = val end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            _scanLabels(child)
        end
    end
    pcall(_scanLabels, podium)
    _wmpsCache[_ck], _wmpsAt[_ck] = best, _now
    return best
end

-- priority lists (user-configurable globals; fallback to mps-only)
local _priCacheVer, _priCache = -1, {}
local _mutPriCacheVer, _mutPriCache = -1, {}
local _traitPriCacheVer, _traitPriCache = -1, {}
local function _priLookup(listName)
    local ver = _G.StickyPriVersion or 0
    if listName == "mutation" then
        if _mutPriCacheVer ~= ver then
            _mutPriCache = {}
            local plist = _G.SHARED_MUTATION_PRIORITY
            if type(plist) == "table" then
                for i = #plist, 1, -1 do _mutPriCache[_normName(plist[i])] = i end
            end
            _mutPriCacheVer = ver
        end
        return _mutPriCache
    elseif listName == "trait" then
        if _traitPriCacheVer ~= ver then
            _traitPriCache = {}
            local plist = _G.SHARED_TRAIT_PRIORITY
            if type(plist) == "table" then
                for i = #plist, 1, -1 do _traitPriCache[_normName(plist[i])] = i end
            end
            _traitPriCacheVer = ver
        end
        return _traitPriCache
    else
        if _priCacheVer ~= ver then
            _priCache = {}
            local plist = _G.SHARED_PRIORITY_ITEMS
            if type(plist) == "table" then
                for i = #plist, 1, -1 do _priCache[_normName(plist[i])] = i end
            end
            _priCacheVer = ver
        end
        return _priCache
    end
end
local function _priIndexOf(name)
    if not name then return nil end
    return _priLookup()[_normName(name)]
end
local function _priIndexOfMut(name)
    if not name then return nil end
    return _priLookup("mutation")[_normName(name)]
end
local function _priIndexOfTrait(name)
    if not name then return nil end
    return _priLookup("trait")[_normName(name)]
end
local function getMutPrio(m)
    if not m or m == "" or m == "None" then return 0 end
    local n = tostring(m)
    local idx = _priIndexOfMut(n) or _priIndexOfMut(n:lower():gsub("[%s%-_]", ""))
    if idx then return 999 - idx end
    return 0
end
local function getTraitPrio(t)
    if not t or t == "" then return 0 end
    local name = type(t) == "table" and t.name or tostring(t)
    local idx = _priIndexOfTrait(name) or _priIndexOfTrait(name:lower():gsub("[%s%-_]", ""))
    if idx then return 999 - idx end
    return 0
end
local function _bestTraitPrio(traits)
    if type(traits) ~= "table" then return 0 end
    local best = 0
    for _, t in ipairs(traits) do
        local p = getTraitPrio(t)
        if p > best then best = p end
    end
    return best
end

local _genCache = {}
-- AYDN LAG FIX: ONE shared 0.5s scan cache. The podium-visuals loop (0.6s),
-- the auto-grab re-picker (0.5s) and the target engine (5s) each demanded a
-- FULL server scan on their own -- together that was several whole-map walks
-- per second, and every priority that joined made each walk heavier. They all
-- read this one cached result now; pass force=true when you truly need fresh
-- data (the GO press re-resolves its target with a fresh scan).
local _scanCache, _scanCacheAt = nil, 0
local function scanAllPets(force)
    if not force then
        local _nowC = os.clock()
        if _scanCache and (_nowC - _scanCacheAt) < 0.5 then
            return _scanCache
        end
    end
    local pets = {}
    loadDataModules()
    local Plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not Plots then return pets end
    for _, plot in ipairs(Plots:GetChildren()) do
        if plot:IsA("Model") then
            local channel = getPlotChannel(plot.Name)
            if channel then
                local owner = channelGet(channel, "Owner")
                if not _ownerIsMe(owner) then
                    if owner == nil or _ownerInGame(owner) then
                        local animalList = channelGet(channel, "AnimalList")
                        if type(animalList) == "table" then
                            for slot, animalData in pairs(animalList) do
                                if type(animalData) == "table" and type(animalData.Index) == "string" then
                                    local animalName = animalData.Index
                                    local animalInfo = _AD and _AD[animalName]
                                    if not _StickyIsFusing(animalData) then
                                        local mutation = animalData.Mutation or "None"
                                        local traits = animalData.Traits
                                        if type(traits) ~= "table" then traits = {} end
                                        local genValue
                                        local _gk = plot.Name .. "\0" .. tostring(slot)
                                        local _gc = _genCache[_gk]
                                        if _gc and _gc.n == animalName and _gc.m == animalData.Mutation and _gc.t == animalData.Traits then
                                            genValue = _gc.g
                                        else
                                            genValue = 0
                                            pcall(function()
                                                genValue = AnimalsShared:GetGeneration(animalName, animalData.Mutation, animalData.Traits, nil)
                                            end)
                                            if genValue and genValue > 0 then
                                                _genCache[_gk] = { n = animalName, m = animalData.Mutation, t = animalData.Traits, g = genValue }
                                            end
                                        end
                                        if (not genValue or genValue <= 0) then
                                            local wm = _worldMPS(plot, slot)
                                            if wm and wm > 0 then genValue = wm end
                                        end
                                        local displayName = (animalInfo and animalInfo.DisplayName) or animalName
                                        local pos = getPetPosition(plot, slot)
                                        if pos and (genValue or 0) >= (tonumber(CONFIG.MinMPS) or 0) then
                                            local stealBlocked = (animalData.Steal ~= nil and animalData.Steal ~= false)
                                            if not stealBlocked then
                                                local prompt = getPetPrompt(plot, slot)
                                                table.insert(pets, {
                                                    name = displayName, index = animalName,
                                                    mps = genValue or 0, mutation = mutation, traits = traits,
                                                    position = pos, plot = plot.Name, slot = tostring(slot),
                                                    uid = plot.Name .. "_" .. tostring(slot),
                                                    owner = owner,
                                                    prompt = prompt,
                                                })
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    for _, p in ipairs(pets) do
        p._pri = _priIndexOf(p.name) or (p.index and _priIndexOf(p.index)) or nil
        p._mp = tonumber(p.mps) or 0
        p._mut = getMutPrio(p.mutation)
        p._trait = _bestTraitPrio(p.traits)
    end
    local function _petOrder(a, b)
        local ia, ib = a._pri, b._pri
        if (ia ~= nil) ~= (ib ~= nil) then return ia ~= nil end
        if ia and ib and ia ~= ib then return ia < ib end
        if ia == nil then
            if a._mp ~= b._mp then return a._mp > b._mp end
        end
        if a._mut ~= b._mut then return a._mut > b._mut end
        if a._trait ~= b._trait then return a._trait > b._trait end
        if a._mp ~= b._mp then return a._mp > b._mp end
        local pa, pb = tostring(a.plot), tostring(b.plot)
        if pa ~= pb then return pa < pb end
        return (tonumber(a.slot) or 0) < (tonumber(b.slot) or 0)
    end
    table.sort(pets, _petOrder)
    _scanCache, _scanCacheAt = pets, os.clock()
    return pets
end

local function findTargetIn(list)
    -- target selection deleted: highest-priority target only (sort order in scanAllPets)
    if #list == 0 then return nil end
    return list[1]
end
local function findTarget()
    return findTargetIn(scanAllPets())
end
-- AYDN: expose the full scan (priority pets first, then highest value first)
-- so the GUI / highlight engine can read the same ranking the TP engine uses.
_G.Elite_ListAllPets = scanAllPets

-- ===========================================================================
-- EXACT TP: sky-base coord tables + destination compute + snap (ported)
-- ===========================================================================
local MK_UPPER = {
    B = {{coord=Vector3.new(-487.921448,16.850713,-75.768013),facing="NORTH"},{coord=Vector3.new(-332.379730,16.850722,-75.762100),facing="NORTH"},{coord=Vector3.new(-487.134918,16.850713,-18.094154),facing="SOUTH"},{coord=Vector3.new(-316.300171,16.850713,-17.845898),facing="SOUTH"}},
    C = {{coord=Vector3.new(-330.765381,16.850713,31.424425),facing="NORTH"},{coord=Vector3.new(-502.989349,16.850713,31.172430),facing="NORTH"},{coord=Vector3.new(-489.077087,16.850713,89.010147),facing="SOUTH"},{coord=Vector3.new(-330.908936,16.850713,88.930145),facing="SOUTH"}},
    D = {{coord=Vector3.new(-331.264893,16.850713,138.209167),facing="NORTH"},{coord=Vector3.new(-487.935181,16.850713,138.026321),facing="NORTH"},{coord=Vector3.new(-487.774933,16.850713,195.882538),facing="SOUTH"},{coord=Vector3.new(-330.799133,16.850575,196.022354),facing="SOUTH"}},
}
local MK_LOWER = {
    B = {{coord=Vector3.new(-335.725586,-3.048217,-74.984589),facing="NORTH"},{coord=Vector3.new(-503.214233,-3.048217,-75.043137),facing="NORTH"},{coord=Vector3.new(-483.619385,-3.718430,-18.844337),facing="SOUTH"},{coord=Vector3.new(-316.147095,-3.048218,-18.818844),facing="SOUTH"}},
    C = {{coord=Vector3.new(-335.985413,-3.048218,32.051426),facing="NORTH"},{coord=Vector3.new(-503.277008,-3.048217,31.956175),facing="NORTH"},{coord=Vector3.new(-483.749390,-3.048218,88.147003),facing="SOUTH"},{coord=Vector3.new(-315.793823,-3.048217,88.163979),facing="SOUTH"}},
    D = {{coord=Vector3.new(-335.476654,-3.048218,139.001083),facing="NORTH"},{coord=Vector3.new(-503.710083,-3.048218,138.989883),facing="NORTH"},{coord=Vector3.new(-315.654938,-3.048218,195.302444),facing="SOUTH"},{coord=Vector3.new(-483.859253,-3.048218,195.269043),facing="SOUTH"}},
}
local TALL_PETS = { ["La Secret Combinasion"] = true, ["La Jolly Grande"] = true }
local TALL_OFFSET = 3
local UPPER_Y_THRESHOLD = 7
local BASES_LOW = {
    [1] = Vector3.new(-476.52, -2, 220.94090270996094),
    [2] = Vector3.new(-476.52, -2, 113.77315521240234),
    [3] = Vector3.new(-476.52, -2, 6.178487777709961),
    [4] = Vector3.new(-476.52, -2, -101.07275390625),
    [5] = Vector3.new(-342.66, -2, 221.44737243652344),
    [6] = Vector3.new(-342.66, -2, 113.41409301757812),
    [7] = Vector3.new(-342.66, -2, 6.249461650848389),
    [8] = Vector3.new(-342.66, -2, -99.73458862304688),
}
local BASES_HIGH = {
    [1] = Vector3.new(-479.51, 18, 220.94090270996094),
    [2] = Vector3.new(-479.51, 18, 113.77315521240234),
    [3] = Vector3.new(-479.51, 18, 6.178487777709961),
    [4] = Vector3.new(-479.51, 18, -101.07275390625),
    [5] = Vector3.new(-339.48, 18, 221.44737243652344),
    [6] = Vector3.new(-339.48, 18, 113.41409301757812),
    [7] = Vector3.new(-339.48, 18, 6.249461650848389),
    [8] = Vector3.new(-339.48, 18, -99.73458862304688),
}
local FRONT_Y_LOW  = -3.048217
local FRONT_Y_HIGH = 16.850713
local COLUMN_SPLIT_X = -410
local FRONT_Z_CLAMP = 18
local SIDE_NEAR_Z   = 45

local function getClosestBaseIdx(pos)
    local closest, dist = 1, math.huge
    for i = 1, 8 do
        local b = BASES_LOW[i]
        local d = (pos.X - b.X)^2 + (pos.Z - b.Z)^2
        if d < dist then dist = d; closest = i end
    end
    return closest
end
local function buildFrontCandidate(idx, isUpper, playerZ)
    local base = isUpper and BASES_HIGH[idx] or BASES_LOW[idx]
    local frontY = isUpper and FRONT_Y_HIGH or FRONT_Y_LOW
    local frontZ = math.clamp(playerZ - base.Z, -FRONT_Z_CLAMP, FRONT_Z_CLAMP) + base.Z
    local coord = Vector3.new(base.X, frontY, frontZ)
    local faceDir = (idx <= 4) and Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0) or Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
    return coord, faceDir
end
local function plotSides(coordTable, idx)
    local base = BASES_LOW[idx]
    local isWest = idx <= 4
    local out = {}
    for _, coords in pairs(coordTable) do
        for _, data in ipairs(coords) do
            if ((data.coord.X < COLUMN_SPLIT_X) == isWest)
               and math.abs(data.coord.Z - base.Z) < SIDE_NEAR_Z then
                out[#out + 1] = data
            end
        end
    end
    return out
end
local function isPlotUnlocked(plotName)
    local ok, res = pcall(function()
        local channel = getPlotChannel(plotName)
        if not channel then return false end
        if channelGet(channel, "BlockEndTimeFirstFloor") ~= nil then return false end
        return true
    end)
    return ok and (res == true)
end
local function findClosest(petPos, coordTable)
    local best, bestKey, bestDist = nil, nil, math.huge
    for skyKey, coords in pairs(coordTable) do
        for _, data in ipairs(coords) do
            local c = data.coord
            local d = math.sqrt((petPos.X - c.X)^2 + (petPos.Z - c.Z)^2)
            if d < bestDist then bestDist = d; best = data; bestKey = skyKey end
        end
    end
    return best, bestKey
end

-- computeDestination -> destPos, facingDir, isUpperFloor, dropBelow
local function computeDestination(pet)
    -- EXACT COCOX doVelocityTP destination (idkkkkbro.lua.txt, verbatim
    -- port): the sky-grid coordinate tables; UPPER when the pet sits above
    -- UPPER_Y_THRESHOLD (7), else LOWER; TALL pets lower the lookup Y by
    -- TALL_OFFSET; then the front/side candidate refinement, verbatim. This
    -- coordinate is what the clone drops on -- it is INSIDE the target base.
    local petPos = pet.position
    -- ground path (CocoX ground branch, verbatim): pet at street level on an
    -- unlocked plot -> straight into the base interior at Y = -4
    if petPos.Y <= 8.9 and isPlotUnlocked(pet.plot) then
        return Vector3.new(petPos.X, -4, petPos.Z), Vector3.new(0, 0, -1), false, true
    end
    local petName = pet.name
    local adjY = petPos.Y
    if TALL_PETS[petName] then adjY = petPos.Y - TALL_OFFSET end
    local coordTable = adjY > UPPER_Y_THRESHOLD and MK_UPPER or MK_LOWER
    local closestData = findClosest(petPos, coordTable)
    if not closestData then
        return petPos, Vector3.new(0, 0, -1), (coordTable == MK_UPPER), false
    end
    local destPos = closestData.coord
    local facingDir = closestData.facing == "NORTH" and Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
    -- front/side candidate refinement (doVelocityTP, verbatim)
    local isUpper = (coordTable == MK_UPPER)
    local idx = getClosestBaseIdx(petPos)
    local hrp = getHRP()
    local refZ = hrp and hrp.Position.Z or petPos.Z
    local frontCoord, frontFace = buildFrontCandidate(idx, isUpper, refZ)
    local bestCoord, bestFace = frontCoord, frontFace
    local bestDist = hrp and (hrp.Position - frontCoord).Magnitude or (petPos - frontCoord).Magnitude
    for _, d in ipairs(plotSides(coordTable, idx)) do
        local dd = hrp and (hrp.Position - d.coord).Magnitude or (petPos - d.coord).Magnitude
        if dd < bestDist then
            bestDist = dd
            bestCoord = d.coord
            bestFace = d.facing == "NORTH" and Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
        end
    end
    destPos = bestCoord
    facingDir = bestFace
    return destPos, facingDir, isUpper, false
end

-- forward declarations: the transit no-collide helpers are defined further
-- below, but snapToDest's velocity docking needs them too
local transitCollideOff, transitCollideRestore

-- ===========================================================================
-- AYDN VELOCITY ANTI-VOID (added): the original V5 void guards below are
-- untouched, but they recover with a CFrame pull -- and a CFrame pull is
-- exactly the kind of move this server resets you for. This watchdog is armed
-- the whole time a GO run is in transit (running == true) and recovers with
-- VELOCITY ONLY: the frame we dip below the void line, kill the fall and fly
-- straight back up to the last spot we really stood on, pushed by plain
-- .Velocity the whole way. No CFrame, no anchor, per-frame (so it reacts
-- long before the 0.35s background guard ever sees the void).
-- ===========================================================================
local _AV_VOID_Y = -55
local _avLastSafe = nil
local _avRecovering = false
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if not running then _avLastSafe = nil _avRecovering = false return end
    local hrp = getHRP()
    if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return end
    local p = hrp.Position
    if not _avRecovering then
        if p.Y >= _AV_VOID_Y then
            -- above the void line: remember the last spot we are standing on
            local hum = getHumanoid()
            if hum and hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then
                _avLastSafe = p
            end
            return
        end
        -- dipped below the void line mid-transit -> start the climb-out
        if not (_avLastSafe and _avLastSafe.Y > -40) then return end
        _avRecovering = true
        print("[AYDN] velocity anti-void: climbing back to last safe spot")
    end
    -- recovering: climb back to the last safe spot, VELOCITY ONLY
    local back = _avLastSafe
    if not back or back.Y <= -40 then _avRecovering = false return end
    local target = back + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0)
    if p.Y > math.max(_AV_VOID_Y, back.Y + 3) and (target - p).Magnitude <= 6 then
        -- back in business: hand control back to normal physics
        _avRecovering = false
        pcall(function() hrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero end)
        return
    end
    pcall(function()
        hrp.Anchored = false
        local toT = target - p
        if toT.Magnitude > 0.1 then
            hrp.Velocity = toT.Unit * 120
        end
    end)
end)

-- snapToDest: final docking (velocity docking + soft hold + stable wait)
-- AYDN NO-RESET v6: ZERO position CFrames. The character is moved to the
-- exact spot with Velocity ONLY (pure physics -- the server
-- sees a normally flung character, never a teleport, so no reset). CFrame is
-- used ONLY for facing (position untouched) and the soft hold fires only once
-- we are <= 2 studs away, where it is a zero/tiny-delta hold, not a move.
local function snapToDest(destPos, facingDir, isUpperFloor)
    local hrp = getHRP()
    local hum = getHumanoid()
    if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return false end
    local _needPin = isUpperFloor or (hum and hum.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air)
    pcall(function()
        hrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero
        hrp.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
        hrp.Anchored = false
    end)
    -- AYDN RESET FIX: no CFrame to the destination at all -- dock there with
    -- Velocity (velocity = replicated physics, no reset). The
    -- upper body goes no-collide during the dock so walls/props cannot block
    -- the push; collision is restored the moment docking ends.
    transitCollideOff()
    local dockStart = os.clock()
    while not stopRequested and os.clock() - dockStart < 2.5 do
        local cur = getHRP()
        if not cur or not cur.Parent then break end
        local d = (cur.Position - destPos).Magnitude
        if d <= 1.2 then break end
        local dir = (destPos - cur.Position).Unit
        -- ease the speed with distance: quick when far, gentle up close
        local dockSpeed = math.clamp(24 + 28 * d, 24, 80)
        pcall(function()
            cur.Velocity = dir * dockSpeed
            cur.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)
        RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
    end
    local hrp2 = getHRP()
    if hrp2 and hrp2.Parent then
        pcall(function()
            hrp2.Velocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp2.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
            -- facing only: position stays exactly where physics put us
            hrp2.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp2.Position, hrp2.Position + facingDir)
        end)
    end
    transitCollideRestore()
    -- AYDN hold: zero/tiny-delta soft hold ONLY (<= 2 studs). This never
    -- carries the character to the destination -- the velocity dock did that.
    local framesLeft = CONFIG.SyncFrames + (_needPin and 25 or 0)
    local conn
    conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then conn:Disconnect(); return end
        framesLeft = framesLeft - 1
        pcall(function()
            if (hrp.Position - destPos).Magnitude <= 2 then
                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(destPos, destPos + facingDir)
            end
        end)
        vZero(hrp)
        if framesLeft <= 0 then conn:Disconnect() end
    end)
    while conn.Connected do task.wait(0.03) end
    local stable, st0 = 0, os.clock()
    while os.clock() - st0 < (tonumber(CONFIG.StableWait) or 0.1) do
        if stopRequested then break end
        local _hrp = getHRP()
        if not _hrp or not _hrp.Parent then break end
        local flat = (Vector3.new(_hrp.Position.X, 0, _hrp.Position.Z) - Vector3.new(destPos.X, 0, destPos.Z)).Magnitude
        if flat <= 3.5 and math.abs(_hrp.Position.Y - destPos.Y) <= 4 then
            stable = stable + 1
            if stable >= 2 then break end
        else
            stable = 0
            -- drifted off: push back with velocity, never CFrame
            vZero(_hrp)
            local dd = (_hrp.Position - destPos).Magnitude
            if dd > 1.2 and dd < 16 then
                pcall(function()
                    _hrp.Velocity = (destPos - _hrp.Position).Unit * math.clamp(24 + 28 * dd, 24, 80)
                end)
            end
        end
        RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
    end
    return true
end

-- ===========================================================================
-- TW TRAVEL -- twilight's EXACT travel executor (module a.y), clone/swap
-- functions excluded, everything else byte-for-byte the same values/logic.
--   Kept: Root/Ping/Still/JumpIfClimbing/InWalls/Trace, Pad, Carpet, Snug,
--         LevelOf/UnderHeight, GoCFrame (hop), GoVelocity (glide + landing
--         pad + 8-frame pin), GoTo (hop -> lagback check -> glide), the sky
--         base coordinate tables, ClosestBase/Aligned/Approach/Bases,
--         GroundPetY, AwaitSwap/InRing.
--   Excluded (the clone): SwapLeft/Hold/Tool/Left/SwapButton/CloneAt/
--         FireSwap/SwapLanded/CloneModel/WaitPlaced/Instant/AtClone/Arm/
--         SwapIn/Stow/HoldPose/Settle/Through/Respawn/Lead.
-- ===========================================================================
TW_CARPET = (function()
    -- twilight module a.i (flying carpet gear), exact
    local PLR = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    local ac = {
        Flying = table.freeze({ 'Flying Carpet', "Cupid's Wings", "Witch's Broom",
            'Waverider', "Santa's Sleigh", 'Skeleton Dragon', 'Flying Bee' }),
        Prefer = nil, Hold = false,
    }
    local function Owned(ad, ae)
        local af = ad:FindFirstChild(ae)
        return if af and af:IsA('Tool') then af else nil
    end
    function ac.Held(ad)
        if not ad then return nil end
        for ae, af in ac.Flying do
            local ag = Owned(ad, af)
            if ag then return ag end
        end
        return nil
    end
    function ac.Equip()
        local ad = PLR.Character
        local ae = ad and ad:FindFirstChildOfClass'Humanoid'
        local af = PLR:FindFirstChildOfClass'Backpack'
        if ac.Hold or not ae or not af or ac.Held(ad) then return false end
        local ag = if ac.Prefer then Owned(af, ac.Prefer) else nil
        for ah, ai in ac.Flying do
            ag = ag or Owned(af, ai)
        end
        if not ag then return false end
        ae:EquipTool(ag)
        return true
    end
    return ac
end)()

TW_CHAR = (function()
    -- twilight module a.h: only the pieces the travel executor needs,
    -- Character() and Feet(), exact same math
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local PLR = Players.LocalPlayer
    local ah = { Posing = false }
    function ah.Character()
        local an = PLR.Character
        local ao = an and an:FindFirstChild'HumanoidRootPart'
        local ap = an and an:FindFirstChildOfClass'Humanoid'
        if not ao or not ap or ap.Health <= 0 then return nil, nil, nil end
        return an, ao, ap
    end
    function ah.Feet()
        local an, ao, ap = ah.Character()
        if not an or not ao or not ap then return 3 end
        local aq = math.huge
        for ar, as in an:GetChildren() do
            if not as:IsA'BasePart' or as == ao then continue end
            local at, au = as.CFrame, as.Size
            local av = (math.abs(at.RightVector.Y) * au.X
                + math.abs(at.UpVector.Y) * au.Y
                + math.abs(at.LookVector.Y) * au.Z) * 0.5
            aq = math.min(aq, at.Position.Y - av)
        end
        local ar = ap.HipHeight + ao.Size.Y * 0.5
        return if aq < math.huge then math.max(ar, ao.Position.Y - aq) else ar
    end
    return ah
end)()

TW_TRAVEL = (function()
    -- twilight module a.y travel executor, exact values
    local PLR = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    local RS = game:GetService("RunService")
    local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local ab = TW_CHAR      -- Character() / Feet()
    local ac = TW_CARPET    -- carpet Held / Equip
    -- twilight's exact constants (aB..aX region; clone-only ones dropped)
    local ag, ah, ai, aj = math.clamp, math.max, math.min, math.abs
    local ak = task.spawn
    local al = task.wait
    local am = RS
    local ap = workspace
    local aq = Players
    local ar = PLR
    local at = 6            -- (clone) unused now
    local au, av, aw = 0.6, 6, 6
    local aD = 10           -- JumpIfClimbing height threshold
    local aE = -4           -- ground floor Y
    local aF = Vector3.new(4, 0.4, 4)      -- pad size
    local aG = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 46)  -- pad color
    local aH = 10           -- pad watch radius
    local aI = 20           -- pad lifetime
    local aJ = 0.5          -- pad collide offset
    local aK = 0.3          -- snug margin
    local aL = 8            -- snug ray length
    local aM, aN, aO = 150, 400, 0.03      -- glide ramp start / speed / time
    local aP, aQ = 2.5, 3   -- glide timeout / arrive distance
    local aR = 8            -- glide pin frames
    local aS, aT = 40, 0.1  -- hop step / hop wait
    local aU = 6            -- lagback-after-hop threshold
    local aV, aW = 6, 7.5   -- UnderHeight drops
    local aX = 8.9          -- GroundPetY
    local aY = { Delay = 0.3 }
    local function Root()
        local aZ = ar.Character
        return aZ and aZ:FindFirstChild'HumanoidRootPart'
    end
    local function Ping()
        return ar:GetNetworkPing()
    end
    local function Still(aZ)
        aZ.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        aZ.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end
    local function Name()
        return (string.gsub(string.lower(HttpService:GenerateGUID(false)), '%-', ''))
    end
    local function JumpIfClimbing(aZ, a_)
        if aj(a_ - aZ.Position.Y) <= aD then return end
        local a0 = aZ.Parent and aZ.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass'Humanoid'
        if a0 then
            a0:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
            a0.Jump = true
        end
    end
    function TW_TRAVEL_InWalls(aZ, a_)
        local a0 = aZ:FindFirstChild'StealHitbox'
        if not a0 or not a0:IsA'BasePart' then return false end
        local a2 = a0.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(a_)
        local a3 = a0.Size * 0.5
        return aj(a2.X) <= a3.X and aj(a2.Y) <= a3.Y and aj(a2.Z) <= a3.Z
    end
    local Log = {}
    local function Trace(a3)
        local a4 = Root()
        local a5 = a4 and a4.Parent and a4.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass'Humanoid'
        local a6 = ''
        if a4 then
            local a7 = a4.CFrame.LookVector
            a6 = string.format(' @(%.0f,%.0f,%.0f) yaw %.0f %s%s',
                a4.Position.X, a4.Position.Y, a4.Position.Z,
                math.deg(math.atan2(-a7.X, -a7.Z)),
                if a5 then a5:GetState().Name else '',
                if ar:GetAttribute'GiantPotion' ~= nil then ' giant' else '')
        end
        Log[#Log + 1] = string.format('%.2f %s%s', os.clock(), a3, a6)
    end
    local function InRing(a5, a6)
        local a7 = a5:GetPivot().Position
        return aj(a6.X - a7.X) < 26 and aj(a6.Z - a7.Z) < 26
    end
    local function AwaitSwap(a5, a6)
        local a7 = os.clock() + a6
        repeat
            local a8 = Root()
            if a8 and (TW_TRAVEL_InWalls(a5, a8.Position) or InRing(a5, a8.Position)) then
                return true
            end
            am.Heartbeat:Wait()
        until os.clock() > a7
        local a8 = Root()
        return a8 ~= nil and InRing(a5, a8.Position)
    end
    local ClearPad
    local a5
    ClearPad = function()
        if a5 then a5:Destroy() a5 = nil end
    end
    local function Pad(a6, a7, a8)
        ClearPad()
        local a9 = Instance.new'Part'
        a9.Name = Name()
        a9.Size = aF
        a9.Position = Vector3.new(a6.X, a7 - aF.Y * 0.5, a6.Z)
        a9.Anchored = true
        a9.CanCollide = not a8
        a9.CanTouch = false
        a9.CanQuery = false
        a9.Transparency = 0.55
        a9.Color = aG
        a9.CastShadow = false
        a9.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
        a9.Parent = ap
        a5 = a9
        local b = ab.Feet()
        local ba = if a8 then am.Stepped:Connect(function()
            local ba = Root()
            if ba then a9.CanCollide = ba.Position.Y - b >= a7 - aJ end
        end) else nil
        ak(function()
            local bb = os.clock()
            while a9.Parent and os.clock() - bb < aI do
                local bc = Root()
                if not bc or ((bc.Position - a9.Position) * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)).Magnitude > aH then break end
                al(0.15)
            end
            if ba then ba:Disconnect() end
            if a5 == a9 then ClearPad() end
        end)
    end
    local function Carpet(a6)
        local a7 = os.clock()
        repeat
            if ac.Held(ar.Character) then al(a6) return end
            ac.Equip()
            am.Heartbeat:Wait()
        until os.clock() - a7 > 1.5
    end
    local function LevelOf(a6)
        return if a6 > 23.15 then 3 elseif a6 >= 11 then 2 else 1
    end
    local function GoCFrame(a6, a7, a8, a9)
        if not a9 then Carpet(0.2) end
        local b = Root()
        if not b then return false, nil end
        b.Anchored = false
        if a8 and a7 and not AwaitSwap(a7, 1.5) then
            Trace'not inside the base after the swap'
            return false, nil
        end
        local ba = a6.Y
        local bb = b.Position.Y
        local bc = LevelOf(bb) == LevelOf(ba)
        if bc then
            Trace"already on the podium's floor"
        elseif ba > 23.15 then
            bb = 21
        elseif ba >= 11 then
            bb = 14.5
        elseif ba >= -6.9 and ba <= 8.9 then
            bb = aE
        end
        local c = Vector3.new(a6.X, bb, a6.Z)
        Trace(string.format('hop to (%.0f,%.0f,%.0f)', c.X, c.Y, c.Z))
        JumpIfClimbing(b, c.Y)
        local e = b.Position
        local f = math.max(1, math.ceil((c - e).Magnitude / aS))
        for g = 1, f do
            if not b.Parent then return false, nil end
            b.CFrame = CFrame.new(e:Lerp(c, g / f))
            Still(b)
            if g < f then al(aT) end
        end
        if not bc then Pad(c, c.Y - ab.Feet(), false) end
        task.delay(0.5, Trace, 'half a second after the hop')
        return true, c
    end
    local function UnderHeight(a6, a7)
        if (a7 and 19 <= a7) or (not a7 and a6 > 23.15) then return a6 - aW end
        if (a7 and a7 >= 11) or (not a7 and a6 >= 11) then return a6 - aV end
        if (a7 and a7 >= 1) or (not a7 and a6 >= -6.9 and a6 <= 8.9) then return aE end
        return a6 - aV
    end
    local function GoVelocity(a6, a7, a8, a9, b, ba)
        if not ba then Carpet(0.03) end
        local bb = Root()
        if not bb then return false end
        bb.Anchored = false
        if a9 and a8 and not AwaitSwap(a8, ag(Ping() * 4, 0.4, 1.5)) then
            Trace'not inside the base after the swap'
            return false
        end
        Trace'glide start'
        Still(bb)
        local bc = LevelOf(bb.Position.Y) == LevelOf(a6.Y)
        local c = Vector3.new(a6.X, if bc then bb.Position.Y else UnderHeight(a6.Y, a7), a6.Z)
        local e = bb.Parent and bb.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass'Humanoid'
        local function States(f)
            if e then
                e:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running, f)
                e:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Climbing, f)
            end
        end
        States(false)
        local f = os.clock()
        while os.clock() - f < aP do
            bb = Root()
            if not bb or not bb.Parent or ar:GetAttribute'Stealing' then break end
            local g = c - bb.Position
            if g.Magnitude <= aQ then break end
            local h = g.Unit * (if os.clock() - f < aO then ai(aM, b or aN) else b or aN)
            if bb.Position.Y >= c.Y - 0.5 and h.Y > 0 then h = Vector3.new(h.X, 0, h.Z) end
            bb.AssemblyLinearVelocity = h
            am.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
        bb = Root()
        if not bb or not bb.Parent then States(true) return false end
        local g = CFrame.new(c) * (bb.CFrame - bb.CFrame.Position)
        JumpIfClimbing(bb, c.Y)
        Still(bb)
        if not bc then Pad(a6, c.Y - ab.Feet(), true) end
        bb.CFrame = g
        for h = 1, aR do
            am.Heartbeat:Wait()
            if not bb.Parent then break end
            if (bb.Position - c).Magnitude > 4 then bb.CFrame = g end
        end
        States(true)
        Trace'glide done'
        task.delay(0.5, Trace, 'half a second after the glide')
        return true
    end
    -- twilight's exact sky-base coordinate tables
    local a6 = {
        { Vector3.new(-487.921448, 16.850713, -75.768013), 'N' },
        { Vector3.new(-332.37973, 16.850722, -75.7621), 'N' },
        { Vector3.new(-487.134918, 16.850713, -18.094154), 'S' },
        { Vector3.new(-316.300171, 16.850713, -17.845898), 'S' },
        { Vector3.new(-330.765381, 16.850713, 31.424425), 'N' },
        { Vector3.new(-502.989349000175, 16.850713, 31.17243), 'N' },
        { Vector3.new(-489.077087, 16.850713, 89.010147), 'S' },
        { Vector3.new(-330.908936, 16.850713, 88.930145), 'S' },
        { Vector3.new(-331.264893, 16.850713, 138.209167), 'N' },
        { Vector3.new(-487.935181, 16.850713, 138.026321), 'N' },
        { Vector3.new(-487.774933, 16.850713, 195.882538), 'S' },
        { Vector3.new(-330.799133, 16.850575, 196.022354), 'S' },
    }
    local a7 = {
        { Vector3.new(-335.725586, -3.048217, -74.984589), 'N' },
        { Vector3.new(-503.214233, -3.048217, -75.043137), 'N' },
        { Vector3.new(-483.619385, -3.71843, -18.844337), 'S' },
        { Vector3.new(-316.147095, -3.048218, -18.818844), 'S' },
        { Vector3.new(-335.985413, -3.048218, 32.051426), 'N' },
        { Vector3.new(-503.277008, -3.048217, 31.956175), 'N' },
        { Vector3.new(-483.74939, -3.048218, 88.147003), 'S' },
        { Vector3.new(-315.793823, -3.048217, 88.163979), 'S' },
        { Vector3.new(-335.476654, -3.048218, 139.001083), 'N' },
        { Vector3.new(-503.710083, -3.048218, 138.989883), 'N' },
        { Vector3.new(-315.654938, -3.048218, 195.302444), 'S' },
        { Vector3.new(-483.859253, -3.048218, 195.269043), 'S' },
    }
    local a8 = { -476.52, -476.52, -476.52, -476.52, -342.66, -342.66, -342.66, -342.66 }
    local a9 = { -479.51, -479.51, -479.51, -479.51, -339.48, -339.48, -339.48, -339.48 }
    local b = { 220.94090270996094, 113.77315521240234, 6.178487777709961, -101.07275390625,
        221.44737243652344, 113.41409301757813, 6.249461650848389, -99.73458862304688 }
    local ba = { ['La Secret Combinasion'] = true, ['La Jolly Grande'] = true }
    local bb, bc = 7, 3
    local c, e = -3.048217, 16.850713
    local f, g, h, i = -410, 18, 45, 2
    local function ClosestBase(j)
        local k, l = 1, math.huge
        for m = 1, 8 do
            local n = (j.X - a8[m]) ^ 2 + (j.Z - b[m]) ^ 2
            if l > n then k, l = m, n end
        end
        return k
    end
    local function Aligned(j, k, l)
        local m = Vector3.new(l, j.Y, j.Z)
        if aj(m.X - j.X) < 1 then return nil end
        local n = {}
        for o, p in aq:GetPlayers() do
            if p.Character then table.insert(n, p.Character) end
        end
        local o = ap:FindFirstChild'Ledge'
        if o then table.insert(n, o) end
        local p = RaycastParams.new()
        p.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        p.FilterDescendantsInstances = n
        p.RespectCanCollide = true
        local q = ap:Raycast(m, 6 * k, p)
        if not q then return nil end
        local r = OverlapParams.new()
        r.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        r.FilterDescendantsInstances = n
        r.RespectCanCollide = true
        if #ap:GetPartBoundsInBox(CFrame.new(m), Vector3.new(3, 5, 3), r) > 0 then return nil end
        if #ap:GetPartBoundsInBox(CFrame.new(q.Position + k * 3), Vector3.new(3, 5, 3), r) > 0 then return nil end
        return m
    end
    local function Approach(j, k, l, m)
        local n = j.Y - (if l and ba[l] then bc else 0)
        local o = bb < n
        local p = ClosestBase(j)
        local q = ag(k.Z - b[p], -g, g) + b[p]
        local r = Vector3.new(if o then a9[p] else a8[p], if o then e else c, q)
        local s = if p <= 4 then Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0) else Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
        local t = 'front'
        if m ~= 'Front' then
            local u = p <= 4
            local v = if m == 'Side' then math.huge else (k - r).Magnitude
            local w = if o then a6 else a7
            for x, y in w do
                local z = y[1]
                if (z.X < f) ~= u or aj(z.Z - b[p]) >= h then continue end
                local A = if y[2] == 'N' then Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) else Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
                z = Aligned(z, A, j.X) or z
                local B = z - A * i
                local C = (k - B).Magnitude
                if v > C then r, s, v, t = B, A, C, 'side' end
            end
        end
        if o then r += Vector3.new(0, 2, 0) end
        return r, s, t
    end
    local function Bases()
        local j = {}
        for k = 1, 8 do
            j[k] = Vector3.new(a8[k], -2, b[k])
        end
        return j
    end
    local function Snug(a5, a6)
        local a7, a8 = ab.Character()
        local a9 = Vector3.new(a6.X, 0, a6.Z)
        if not a7 or not a8 or a9.Magnitude < 1e-4 then return a5 end
        a9 = a9.Unit
        local b = RaycastParams.new()
        b.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        b.FilterDescendantsInstances = { a7 }
        b.RespectCanCollide = true
        local ba
        for bb, bc in { 1.5, 0, -1.5 } do
            local c = ap:Raycast(a5 + Vector3.new(0, bb, 0), a9 * aL, b)
            if c and (not ba or c.Distance < ba) then ba = c.Distance end
        end
        if not ba then return a5 end
        local bb = ba - a8.Size.Z * 0.5 - aK
        return if bb > 0 then a5 + a9 * bb else a5
    end
    return {
        Log = Log,
        Root = Root,
        Ping = Ping,
        Still = Still,
        Trace = Trace,
        InWalls = TW_TRAVEL_InWalls,
        Pad = Pad,
        ClearPad = ClearPad,
        Carpet = Carpet,
        LevelOf = LevelOf,
        GoCFrame = GoCFrame,
        UnderHeight = UnderHeight,
        GoVelocity = GoVelocity,
        GoTo = function(a6, a7, a8, a9, b, ba, bb)
            if a6 == 'Velocity' then return GoVelocity(a7, a8, a9, b, ba, bb) end
            local bc, c = GoCFrame(a7, a9, b, bb)
            if not bc or not c then return bc end
            al(ag(Ping() * 2, 0.2, 0.6))
            local e = Root()
            if not e or ((e.Position - c) * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)).Magnitude <= aU then return bc end
            Trace(string.format('lagged back %.0f studs after the hop, gliding instead',
                ((e.Position - c) * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)).Magnitude))
            return GoVelocity(a7, a8, a9, false, ba, bb)
        end,
        Approach = Approach,
        ClosestBase = ClosestBase,
        Bases = Bases,
        Snug = Snug,
        AwaitSwap = AwaitSwap,
        GroundPetY = aX,
        Feet = ab.Feet,
        Character = ab.Character,
    }
end)()
print("[AYDN] TW travel executor ready (twilight a.y exact, clone excluded)")

-- ===========================================================================
-- TW PATH -- twilight's EXACT movement engine (module a.z), verbatim:
-- speed governor (auto-caps on lagback snaps), resampler, waypoint follower
-- (drive / turn-slow at corners / jump assist / stuck bail), VIS visibility
-- planner + full grid A*-family pathfinder, the 3D ground router (RouteD3)
-- and Fly (plan -> move -> re-route retries). Only the module plumbing
-- (services / player / carpet) is swapped for direct Roblox calls.
-- ===========================================================================
