    return ai
end)()
print("[AYDN] TW pathfinding ready (twilight a.z exact)")


-- ===========================================================================
-- TRAVEL: broom 0.5s + cruise + tween (velocity) -- TOP-TIER PATHFINDING
--   * dual-agent computes (slim retry when tight spots fail)
--   * line-of-sight corner cutting -> smooth diagonals, never hugs walls
--   * hops knee-high blockers BEFORE getting stuck (real humanoid jump)
--   * tall walls reroute instantly (early recompute, not just stuck timeout)
--   * stuck ladder: hop -> side-step -> forward nudge, recompute every time
--   * CLOSE SPEED inside CLOSE DISTANCE studs: glides in, no overshoot
-- ===========================================================================
-- ===========================================================================
-- TRANSIT NO-COLLIDE ("NEVER HIT ANYTHING"): while the engine drives us, the
-- whole upper body goes non-collidable -- walls, podiums, players and props
-- simply cannot snag or body-block us anymore. Legs/feet keep their collision
-- so walking, jumping and floor support stay 100% intact. Restored instantly
-- when the cruise/tween ends.
-- ===========================================================================
local _NC_KEEP_COLLIDE = {
    ["Left Leg"] = true, ["Right Leg"] = true, -- R6 legs (ground support)
    ["LeftFoot"] = true, ["RightFoot"] = true, -- R15 feet
    ["LeftLowerLeg"] = true, ["RightLowerLeg"] = true, -- R15 lower legs
}
local _transitSaved = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
transitCollideOff = function()
    local char = LOCAL_PLAYER.Character
    if not char then return end
    for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("BasePart") and not _NC_KEEP_COLLIDE[d.Name] then
            if _transitSaved[d] == nil then _transitSaved[d] = d.CanCollide end
            d.CanCollide = false
        end
    end
end
transitCollideRestore = function()
    for p, v in pairs(_transitSaved) do
        if p and p.Parent then pcall(function() p.CanCollide = v end) end
    end
    table.clear(_transitSaved)
end

local function createBestPath(startPos, endPos)
    local path = PathfindingService:CreatePath({
        AgentRadius = 2, AgentHeight = 5,
        AgentCanJump = true, AgentCanClimb = true,
        AgentJumpHeight = 12, AgentMaxSlope = 60,
        Costs = { Water = 20, Danger = math.huge },
    })
    local success = pcall(function() path:ComputeAsync(startPos, endPos) end)
    if success and path.Status == Enum.PathStatus.Success then return path end
    -- slim retry: crowded bases / tight gaps fail with the default agent
    local slim = PathfindingService:CreatePath({
        AgentRadius = 1, AgentHeight = 4.5,
        AgentCanJump = true, AgentCanClimb = true,
        AgentJumpHeight = 12, AgentMaxSlope = 75,
        Costs = { Water = 20, Danger = math.huge },
    })
    local okSlim = pcall(function() slim:ComputeAsync(startPos, endPos) end)
    if okSlim and slim.Status == Enum.PathStatus.Success then return slim end
    return nil
end

-- small forward nudge when stuck: face dest, then a pure Velocity
-- push forward -- NO position CFrame (a CFrame jump is exactly what the server
-- resets you for; a velocity fling looks like normal physics)
local function stuckNudge(hrp, destination, studs)
    studs = studs or 6
    if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return end
    local dir = (destination - hrp.Position)
    local flat = Vector3.new(dir.X, 0, dir.Z)
    if flat.Magnitude < 0.1 then return end
    local unit = flat.Unit
    pcall(function()
        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + unit) -- facing only, no move
    end)
    vZero(hrp)
    -- Velocity burst: pushed forward + slight hop, physics-live
    pcall(function()
        hrp.Velocity = unit * 60 + Vector3.new(0, 18, 0)
    end)
end

-- line-of-sight: knee ray + head ray must BOTH be clear (our own char excluded)
local losParams = RaycastParams.new()
losParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
losParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {}
losParams.IgnoreWater = true

local function losClear(a, b)
    local delta = b - a
    if delta.Magnitude < 0.5 then return true end
    local ok, clear = pcall(function()
        if Workspace:Raycast(a + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0), delta + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0), losParams) then
            return false
        end
        if Workspace:Raycast(a + Vector3.new(0, 3.4, 0), delta + Vector3.new(0, 3.4, 0), losParams) then
            return false
        end
        return true
    end)
    if not ok then return false end
    return clear == true
end

-- probe straight ahead: "clear" / "step" (knee-high -> hop it) / "wall" (reroute)
local function obstacleAhead(pos, dir)
    local ok, res = pcall(function()
        local body = Workspace:Raycast(pos + Vector3.new(0, 2.6, 0), dir * 7, losParams)
        if not body then return "clear" end
        local head = Workspace:Raycast(pos + Vector3.new(0, 4.4, 0), dir * 7, losParams)
        return head and "wall" or "step"
    end)
    if not ok then return "clear" end
    return res
end

-- ===========================================================================
-- COCOX TP CORE (ported 1:1 from the CocoX engine)
--   * raycast route solving: clearance probing + bounded smoothing
--     (cxBlocks / cxBlockRay / cxPullWide / cxPushOffWalls -> cxComputeRoute)
--   * cxVelMoveThrough: Heartbeat velocity follower with stall bail
--   * cxCarpetGlideTo: full-3D-speed final approach on the equipped gear
--   * GRAPPLE / HOOK TP REMOVED: no Grapple Hook equip, no remote replay --
--     the flying gear you selected IS the movement.
--   * GEAR RULE (user spec): the selected flying tool is pulled out and
--     Activate()d for 1s at GO, then UNEQUIPPED and re-pulled out as a
--     plain HOLD (no activation). The flight holds it, never activates.
--   * WALL RULE (user spec): run into a wall FACING STRAIGHT -> clone right
--     there; end up SIDEWAYS against the wall -> the character turns
--     straight IN PLACE (rotation only, position untouched) where the player
--     was sideways, then the clone proceeds.
-- ===========================================================================
-- ===========================================================================
-- AYDN: TP VISUAL TRAIL REMOVED (user rule) -- the neon path dots/lines
-- (cxVizLine/cxVizDot/cxVizPath) spawned real parts along the flight path and
-- lagged the client. No TP path is drawn anymore.
-- ===========================================================================

-- COCOX GEAR: pull out the SELECTED flying tool (AYDN SETTINGS -> TOOL).
local CX_FLY_NAMES = { "Flying Carpet", "Carpet", "Cloud", "Witch's Broom", "Cupid's Wings", "Santa's Sleigh", "Waverider", "Skeleton Dragon" }
local function cxFindTool(name)
	local char = LOCAL_PLAYER.Character
	local bp = LOCAL_PLAYER:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
	return (char and char:FindFirstChild(name)) or (bp and bp:FindFirstChild(name))
end
-- returns toolName, freshEquipped. fresh=true only when we JUST equipped it.
local function cxEquipFlying()
	local char = LOCAL_PLAYER.Character
	local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not hum then return nil, false end
	local preferred = (Config and Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.Tool) or _G.Elite_CarpetTool
	if preferred and preferred ~= "" then
		local pt = cxFindTool(preferred)
		if pt and pt:IsA("Tool") then
			if pt.Parent ~= char then
				pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(pt) end)
				return preferred, true
			end
			return preferred, false
		end
	end
	for _, n in ipairs(CX_FLY_NAMES) do
		local t = cxFindTool(n)
		if t and t:IsA("Tool") then
			if t.Parent ~= char then
				pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(t) end)
				return n, true
			end
			return n, false
		end
	end
	return nil, false
end
-- per-Heartbeat ensure during the flight: keeps the gear out as a plain HOLD.
-- NEVER activates (user spec: the re-pull is hold-only; the only Activate in
-- the whole run is the 1s pulse at GO).
local function cxFlyingEnsure()
	local name = cxEquipFlying()
	return name
end

-- MANUAL TP TARGET (CocoX-style): the Targets panel arms a pet; the next GO
-- flies to IT instead of the auto-picked highest-priority pet.
local TP_MANUAL_PET = nil
_G.AYDN_TP_SelectPet = function(pet)
	-- AYDN (user rule): whatever brainrot is selected in the target panel
	-- is the one the TP flies to; uid is built from plot+slot if missing.
	if type(pet) == "table" and (pet.uid or (pet.plot and pet.slot)) then
		if not pet.uid then pet.uid = tostring(pet.plot) .. "_" .. tostring(pet.slot) end
		TP_MANUAL_PET = pet
	else
		TP_MANUAL_PET = nil
	end
	return TP_MANUAL_PET ~= nil
end
_G.AYDN_TP_ManualUID = function()
	return (TP_MANUAL_PET and TP_MANUAL_PET.uid) or nil
end

-- ===== Routing (CocoX, verbatim logic) =====
local CX_DIRS = { Vector3.new(1,0,0), Vector3.new(-1,0,0), Vector3.new(0,0,1), Vector3.new(0,0,-1) }
local CX_STRUCT = { ["structure base home"]=true, ["Wall"]=true, ["Floor"]=true, ["Roof"]=true }
local CX_SKIP_NAME = { ["DeliveryHitbox"]=true, ["StealHitbox"]=true, ["LaserHitbox"]=true,
	["AnimalTarget"]=true, ["Multiplier"]=true, ["Laser"]=true, ["Hitbox"]=true,
	["Spawn"]=true, ["MainRoot"]=true, ["SecondFloor"]=true, ["ThirdFloor"]=true, ["Slope"]=true }
local function cxBlocks(inst)
	if not inst then return false end
	if CX_SKIP_NAME[inst.Name] then return false end
	if inst.CanCollide then return true end
	if CX_STRUCT[inst.Name] then return true end
	local s = inst.Size
	if s and math.max(s.X * s.Y, s.X * s.Z, s.Y * s.Z) > 150 then return true end
	return false
end
local function cxBlocksWide(inst)
	if not inst then return false end
	if CX_SKIP_NAME[inst.Name] then return false end
	if inst.CanCollide then return true end
	if CX_STRUCT[inst.Name] then return true end
	local s = inst.Size
	if s and math.max(s.X * s.Y, s.X * s.Z, s.Y * s.Z) > 30 then return true end
	return false
end
-- ONE RaycastParams, ONE cached player-skip list, AddToFilter instead of a
-- whole-array reassignment per bounce (CocoX hot-path fix).
local _cxRp = RaycastParams.new()
_cxRp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
_cxRp.IgnoreWater = true
local _cxCharSkip, _cxCharSkipAt = {}, 0
local function cxCharList()
	if _cxCharSkipAt ~= 0 and (os.clock() - _cxCharSkipAt) < 1 then return _cxCharSkip end
	local t = {}
	for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if pl.Character then t[#t + 1] = pl.Character end end
	_cxCharSkip, _cxCharSkipAt = t, os.clock()
	return t
end
local _cxHasAddToFilter = pcall(function() return RaycastParams.new().AddToFilter end)
local function cxBlockRay(origin, target, blockFn)
	blockFn = blockFn or cxBlocks
	local skip = cxCharList()
	_cxRp.FilterDescendantsInstances = skip
	local grown = nil
	local o = origin
	for _ = 1, 16 do
		local d = target - o; if d.Magnitude < 0.05 then return nil end
		local res = workspace:Raycast(o, d, _cxRp)
		if not res then return nil end
		if blockFn(res.Instance) then return res end
		if _cxHasAddToFilter then
			_cxRp:AddToFilter(res.Instance)
		else
			if not grown then
				grown = table.create and table.create(#skip + 4) or {}
				table.move(skip, 1, #skip, 1, grown)
			end
			grown[#grown + 1] = res.Instance
			_cxRp.FilterDescendantsInstances = grown
		end
		o = res.Position + d.Unit * 0.3
	end
	return nil
end
local function cxClear(a, b) return cxBlockRay(a, b) == nil end
local function cxClearWideRay(a, b) return cxBlockRay(a, b, cxBlocksWide) == nil end
local function cxClearWide(a, b)
	if not cxClear(a, b) then return false end
	local d = Vector3.new(b.X - a.X, 0, b.Z - a.Z)
	if d.Magnitude < 0.1 then return true end
	local _CLEARANCE = 10
	local perp = Vector3.new(-d.Z, 0, d.X).Unit * _CLEARANCE
	local up = Vector3.new(0, _CLEARANCE, 0)
	return cxClearWideRay(a + perp, b + perp) and cxClearWideRay(a - perp, b - perp)
		and cxClearWideRay(a + up, b + up) and cxClearWideRay(a - up, b - up)
end
-- BOUNDED SMOOTHING (CocoX): lookahead cap + hard deadline instead of O(N^2)
local _CX_PULL_LOOKAHEAD = 6
local function cxPullWide(pts, deadline)
	if #pts <= 2 then return pts end
	local out = { pts[1] }; local i = 1
	while i < #pts do
		if deadline and os.clock() > deadline then
			for k = i + 1, #pts do out[#out + 1] = pts[k] end
			return out
		end
		local j = math.min(#pts, i + _CX_PULL_LOOKAHEAD)
		while j > i + 1 and not cxClearWide(out[#out], pts[j]) do j = j - 1 end
		out[#out + 1] = pts[j]; i = j
	end
	return out
end
local function cxPushOffWalls(pts, deadline)
	if #pts <= 2 then return pts end
	local MARGIN = 10; local MAX_PUSH = 14
	local out = { pts[1] }
	for i = 2, #pts - 1 do
		if deadline and os.clock() > deadline then
			for k = i, #pts do out[#out + 1] = pts[k] end
			return out
		end
		local p = pts[i]; local shift = Vector3.zero
		for _, dr in ipairs(CX_DIRS) do
			local res = cxBlockRay(p, p + dr * MARGIN, cxBlocks)
			if res then
				local dist = (res.Position - p).Magnitude
				if dist < MARGIN then shift = shift - dr * (MARGIN - dist) end
			end
		end
		if shift.Magnitude > 0.1 then
			if shift.Magnitude > MAX_PUSH then shift = shift.Unit * MAX_PUSH end
			local moved = p + shift
			if cxClear(out[#out], moved) then out[#out + 1] = moved else out[#out + 1] = p end
		else
			out[#out + 1] = p
		end
	end
	out[#out + 1] = pts[#pts]
	return out
end
local function cxComputeRoute(fromPos, toPos, facingDir)
	if cxClear(fromPos, toPos) then return { toPos } end
	local entry = facingDir and (toPos - facingDir * 14) or toPos
	local groundTo = Vector3.new(entry.X, fromPos.Y, entry.Z)
	local path = PathfindingService:CreatePath({
		AgentRadius = 12, AgentHeight = 5, AgentCanJump = true, AgentJumpHeight = 10, AgentMaxSlope = 89,
	})
	local FLOAT = 3
	local nav = { fromPos }
	local ok = pcall(function()
		path:ComputeAsync(Vector3.new(fromPos.X, fromPos.Y, fromPos.Z), groundTo)
	end)
	if ok and path.Status == Enum.PathStatus.Success then
		local last = fromPos
		for _, wp in ipairs(path:GetWaypoints()) do
			if (wp.Position - last).Magnitude >= 8 then
				nav[#nav + 1] = wp.Position + Vector3.new(0, FLOAT, 0); last = wp.Position
			end
		end
	end
	nav[#nav + 1] = entry + Vector3.new(0, FLOAT, 0)
	local deadline = os.clock() + (tonumber(_G.AYDN_RouteBudget) or 0.03)
	nav = cxPushOffWalls(nav, deadline)
	local route = cxPullWide(nav, deadline)
	route[#route + 1] = toPos
	return route
end

-- ===== Movement (CocoX velMoveThrough, verbatim logic) =====
local CX_SPEED, CX_ARRIVE, CX_MAX_CLIMB = 200, 3, 60
local cxAbort = false
-- WALL RULE state: set when a flight phase stalls against a wall (no progress
-- for ~0.3s of Heartbeats). runSequence reads these so the snap + soft pin
-- keep the straight facing until the clone drops.
local cxHitWall = false
local cxWallDir = nil
local function cxWallFaceStraight(hrp, pushDir)
	cxHitWall = true
	cxWallDir = nil
	if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return end
	local straight = pushDir and Vector3.new(pushDir.X, 0, pushDir.Z) or nil
	if not straight or straight.Magnitude < 0.01 then return end
	straight = straight.Unit
	cxWallDir = straight
	pcall(function()
		local look = hrp.CFrame.LookVector * XZ
		if look.Magnitude > 0.01 then look = look.Unit end
		if look:Dot(straight) < 0.7 then
			-- user spec: hit the wall on the SIDE of the character -> turn
			-- straight RIGHT HERE where the player was sideways (rotation
			-- in place, position untouched -- no reset risk)
			hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + straight)
			print("[AYDN] wall: character was sideways -> turned straight in place")
		else
			-- already facing straight into the wall -> clone as normal
			print("[AYDN] wall: facing straight -> clone as normal")
		end
	end)
end
local function cxVelMoveThrough(hrp, waypoints, speedOverride, allowJump, quickStart)
	if not hrp or not hrp.Parent or #waypoints == 0 then return false end
	local _runSpeed = speedOverride or CX_SPEED
	local wpIdx = 1
	local done = false
	local okDone = false
	local conn
	local function finish(finished)
		if done then return end
		done = true
		okDone = finished == true
		if hrp and hrp.Parent then
			-- AYDN NO-RESET: velocity zero only. The old CocoX finish snapped a
			-- position CFrame onto the last waypoint -- that position teleport
			-- could drop you inside geometry / off the landing anchor.
			hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
		end
		if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
	end
	local lastDist, stall = math.huge, 0
	if quickStart then
		local _hp = RaycastParams.new()
		_hp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
		_hp.IgnoreWater = true
		local _skip = {}
		for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if pl.Character then _skip[#_skip + 1] = pl.Character end
		end
		_hp.FilterDescendantsInstances = _skip
		for _ = 1, 3 do
			local target = waypoints[wpIdx]
			if not target then break end
			local flat = Vector3.new(target.X - hrp.Position.X, 0, target.Z - hrp.Position.Z)
			local mag = flat.Magnitude
			if mag < 1 then break end
			local nextPos = hrp.Position + flat.Unit * math.min(20, mag)
			local _hit = workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, nextPos - hrp.Position, _hp)
			if _hit and _hit.Instance and _hit.Instance.CanCollide then break end
			hrp.CFrame = (hrp.CFrame - hrp.CFrame.Position) + nextPos
			hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return false end
		end
	end
	conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if not hrp or not hrp.Parent or done or cxAbort then
			if conn then conn:Disconnect() end; return
		end
		cxFlyingEnsure() -- keep the selected flying gear OUT for the whole flight
		local target = waypoints[wpIdx]
		local diff = target - hrp.Position
		local mag = diff.Magnitude
		if mag < CX_ARRIVE then
			wpIdx = wpIdx + 1
			if wpIdx > #waypoints then finish(true); return end
			lastDist, stall = math.huge, 0
			target = waypoints[wpIdx]
			diff = target - hrp.Position
			mag = diff.Magnitude
		end
		if mag > lastDist - 0.05 then stall = stall + 1 else stall = 0 end
		lastDist = mag
		if stall >= 18 then
			-- WALL RULE: no progress for ~0.3s == pressed against a wall
			cxWallFaceStraight(hrp, diff)
			finish(true); return
		end
		if mag >= 0.1 then
			local dir = diff.Unit
			if allowJump and diff.Y > 5 and wpIdx < #waypoints then
				local hum = hrp.Parent and hrp.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if hum then
					local st = hum:GetState()
					if st ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping and st ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall then
						pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end)
						pcall(function() hum.Jump = true end)
					end
				end
			end
			local _vy = dir.Y * _runSpeed
			if _vy > CX_MAX_CLIMB then _vy = CX_MAX_CLIMB end
			hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(dir.X * _runSpeed, _vy, dir.Z * _runSpeed)
		end
	end)
	local totalDist = 0
	local prev = hrp.Position
	for _, wp in ipairs(waypoints) do totalDist = totalDist + (prev - wp).Magnitude; prev = wp end
	local timeout = totalDist / math.min(CX_SPEED, _runSpeed) + 2
	local elapsed = 0
	while not done and elapsed < timeout do
		if cxAbort then break end
		task.wait(0.05); elapsed = elapsed + 0.05
	end
	finish(not cxAbort)
	vZero(hrp)
	return okDone
end

-- CARPET GLIDE (CocoX): fly straight to the point on the equipped gear at full
-- 3D speed. Final approach after the route flight.
local function cxCarpetGlideTo(targetPos, speedOverride)
	if not targetPos then return false end
	local char = LOCAL_PLAYER.Character
	local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not hrp then return false end
	pcall(function() hrp.Anchored = false end)
	cxFlyingEnsure()
	local SPEED2 = speedOverride
		or (Config and Config.TpSettings and (tonumber(Config.TpSettings.GrabbleTPSpeed) or tonumber(Config.TpSettings.TPSpeed)))
		or 190
	-- ANCHOR FIX: goal is EXACTLY the dock point. The old CocoX glide dove the
	-- goal 4 studs down whenever Y > 20, which is BELOW the landing platform
	-- top on upper floors -- that is what put the anchor in the wrong place.
	local goal = targetPos
	local t0 = os.clock()
	local lastDist, stall = math.huge, 0
	local reached = false
	while hrp.Parent and (os.clock() - t0) < 8 do
		if LOCAL_PLAYER:GetAttribute("Stealing") then break end
		cxFlyingEnsure()
		local diff = goal - hrp.Position
		local mag = diff.Magnitude
		if mag < 3 then reached = true; break end
		if mag > lastDist - 0.05 then stall = stall + 1 else stall = 0 end
		lastDist = mag
		if stall >= 30 then
			cxWallFaceStraight(hrp, diff) -- WALL RULE: straighten if sideways
			break
		end
		hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = diff.Unit * SPEED2
		RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
	end
	if hrp and hrp.Parent then
		hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
	end
	return reached
end

local function flyToDestination(destination, speed, stopRange, maxTime)
	-- COCOX CORE: cxComputeRoute + cxVelMoveThrough. Hook tp is GONE. Gear is
	-- already handled by the pulse at GO (activate 1s -> re-pull as HOLD);
	-- the flight just keeps it held (cxFlyingEnsure, never activates).
	-- AYDN guards stay: transit no-collide, void clamp, stop request, max time.
	local hrp = getHRP()
	local humanoid = getHumanoid()
	if not hrp or not humanoid then return false end
	speed = speed or CONFIG.TPSpeed
	stopRange = stopRange or CONFIG.CruiseStopRange
	maxTime = maxTime or CONFIG.MaxTime
	local startTime = os.clock()
	local startPos = hrp.Position
	transitCollideOff() -- "never hit anything" during the whole flight
	cxAbort = false
	cxHitWall = false -- WALL RULE: fresh state for this flight
	cxWallDir = nil
	local monitor
	monitor = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		local cur = getHRP()
		if not cur or not cur.Parent then return end
		if stopRequested or os.clock() - startTime > (maxTime or 30) then
			cxAbort = true
		end
		if cur.Position.Y < -55 then
			vZero(cur)
			pcall(function() cur.CFrame = CFrame.new(startPos + Vector3.new(0, 8, 0)) end)
		end
	end)
	local facing = (destination - hrp.Position) * XZ
	if facing.Magnitude > 0.01 then facing = facing.Unit end
	local route = cxComputeRoute(hrp.Position, destination, facing)
	local pts = (route and #route > 0) and route or { destination }
	local okFly = false
	pcall(function()
		okFly = cxVelMoveThrough(hrp, pts, speed, false, true) == true
	end)
	pcall(function() monitor:Disconnect() end)
	cxAbort = false
	pcall(transitCollideRestore)
	hrp = getHRP()
	if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return false end
	vZero(hrp)
	local arrived = (hrp.Position - destination).Magnitude <= ((stopRange or 8) + 3)
	print(("[AYDN] CX fly %s (waypoints %s)"):format(
		okFly and "OK" or "FAIL", tostring(pts and #pts or "direct")))
	return arrived or okFly
end

local function tweenToPosition(destination, stopRange, maxTime)
	-- COCOX final approach: cxCarpetGlideTo (full 3D speed on the gear).
	stopRange = stopRange or CONFIG.TweenStopRange
	local hrp = getHRP()
	if not hrp then return false end
	local speed = CONFIG.TPSpeed or 400
	local ok = false
	pcall(function() ok = cxCarpetGlideTo(destination, speed) == true end)
	local cur = getHRP()
	if cur and cur.Parent then vZero(cur) end
	print(("[AYDN] CX glide hop %s"):format(ok and "OK" or "FAIL"))
	return ok == true
end

local function walkTravel(destination, stopRange, maxTime)
    local hrp = getHRP()
    local humanoid = getHumanoid()
    if not hrp or not humanoid then return false end
    stopRange = stopRange or CONFIG.TweenStopRange
    maxTime = maxTime or CONFIG.MaxTime
    local startTime = os.clock()
    local currentPath, waypoints, waypointIndex = nil, {}, 1
    local lastPathCompute = 0
    local lastComputeTry = 0
    local PATH_RECOMPUTE_INTERVAL = 1.5
    local lastPos = hrp.Position
    local lastProgressTime = os.clock()
    local stuckRecovers = 0
    local lastRisePulse = 0
    local risePulses = 0
    transitCollideOff() -- legs keep collision: walking + floor support stay real
    local ch = LOCAL_PLAYER.Character
    losParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = (ch and ch.Parent) and { ch } or {}

    local function computePath()
        local t = os.clock()
        if t - lastComputeTry < 0.15 then return false end
        lastComputeTry = t
        local path = createBestPath(hrp.Position, destination)
        if path then
            currentPath = path
            waypoints = path:GetWaypoints()
            waypointIndex = 1
            lastPathCompute = os.clock()
            return true
        end
        currentPath = nil
        waypoints = {}
        return false
    end

    -- SIMPLE ROUTE: if we can already see the target, walk the straight line
    -- and skip the pathfinder completely
    if losClear(hrp.Position, destination) then
        currentPath = nil
        waypoints = {}
        lastPathCompute = os.clock()
        print("[AYDN] walk: simple route (straight line)")
    else
        computePath()
    end

    local arrived = false
    local _ncFrames = 0
    local connection
    connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        hrp = getHRP()
        humanoid = getHumanoid()
        if not hrp or not hrp.Parent or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
            connection:Disconnect()
            return
        end
        local now = os.clock()
        if stopRequested then connection:Disconnect() return end
        if now - startTime > maxTime then connection:Disconnect() return end
        -- keep the transit no-collide fresh (tools/accessories can spawn mid-walk)
        _ncFrames = _ncFrames + 1
        if _ncFrames % 30 == 0 then transitCollideOff() end
        local pos = hrp.Position
        local distToTarget = (destination - pos).Magnitude
        if distToTarget <= stopRange then
            connection:Disconnect()
            arrived = true
            return
        end

        -- progress bookkeeping
        if (pos - lastPos).Magnitude > 0.35 then
            lastProgressTime = now
        end
        lastPos = pos

        -- stuck: real humanoid hop + fresh path, then bail. NO CFrame nudges,
        -- NO velocity pops -- that is exactly what was resetting you.
        if now - lastProgressTime > 1.8 then
            stuckRecovers = stuckRecovers + 1
            if stuckRecovers > 8 then
                print("[AYDN] walk stuck too long, bailing")
                connection:Disconnect()
                return
            end
            pcall(function() humanoid.Jump = true end)
            computePath()
            lastProgressTime = now
            return
        end

        -- tall wall ahead (and no fresh path) -> reroute
        if (now - lastPathCompute > PATH_RECOMPUTE_INTERVAL or #waypoints == 0)
            and not losClear(pos, destination) then
            computePath()
        end

        -- pick the current waypoint, skipping the ones we already passed
        local targetPos = destination
        if currentPath and #waypoints > 0 then
            while waypointIndex <= #waypoints do
                local wp = waypoints[waypointIndex]
                local distToWp = (Vector3.new(wp.Position.X, pos.Y, wp.Position.Z) - pos).Magnitude
                if distToWp < 5 then
                    waypointIndex = waypointIndex + 1
                else
                    targetPos = wp.Position
                    break
                end
            end
            if waypointIndex > #waypoints then targetPos = destination end
            -- corner cut: next waypoint visible -> skip straight to it
            for _skipTry = 1, 2 do
                local nxt = waypointIndex + 1
                if nxt > #waypoints then break end
                local wpPos = waypoints[nxt].Position
                if losClear(pos, wpPos) then
                    waypointIndex = nxt
                    targetPos = wpPos
                else
                    break
                end
            end
            -- the path itself says jump -> jump
            if waypoints[waypointIndex]
                and waypoints[waypointIndex].Label == Enum.PathWaypointAction.Jump then
                pcall(function() humanoid.Jump = true end)
            end
        end

        local flat = (targetPos - pos) * XZ
        -- RISE: destination is above us and we are basically under it -> hop.
        -- Still too low -> one more gear pulse (SAME steps: pull out ->
        -- activate 0.1s -> re-pull out) lifts us back up, never a velocity
        -- shove and never a CFrame drag. Capped at 3 pulses per walk.
        if (targetPos.Y - pos.Y) > 3 and flat.Magnitude < 6 then
            pcall(function() humanoid.Jump = true end)
            if risePulses < 3 and now - lastRisePulse > 2.5 then
                lastRisePulse = now
                risePulses = risePulses + 1
                print("[AYDN] walk: gear pulse to rise (" .. risePulses .. "/3)")
                task.spawn(function()
                    pcall(function()
                        if type(_G.AYDN_GearPulse) == "function" then _G.AYDN_GearPulse() end
                    end)
                end)
            end
        end
        -- knee-high blocker straight ahead -> hop it before we get stuck
        if flat.Magnitude > 0.1 then
            local ahead = obstacleAhead(pos, flat.Unit)
            if ahead == "step" then
                pcall(function() humanoid.Jump = true end)
            end
        end

        -- WALK: your own walking velocity. No tween, no velocity, no CFrame.
        local dir = Vector3.zero
        if flat.Magnitude > 0.1 then
            dir = flat.Unit
        end
        pcall(function() humanoid:Move(dir, false) end)
    end)

    while connection.Connected do task.wait(0.03) end
    transitCollideRestore()
    local hum2 = getHumanoid()
    if hum2 then pcall(function() hum2:Move(Vector3.zero, false) end) end
    return arrived
end

-- ===========================================================================
-- TWILIGHT CLONE-IN (SwapLeft via debug.getupvalue)
-- ===========================================================================
local SWAP_KEY = "ItemUse/QuantumClonerTeleport/" .. LOCAL_PLAYER.Name
local debounceFn, debounceTable
local function SwapLeft()
    if not debounceTable then
        pcall(function()
            local pkgs = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Packages")
            local debounce = pkgs and pkgs:FindFirstChild("Debounce")
            local ok, fn = pcall(require, debounce)
            if ok and type(fn) == "function" then debounceFn = fn end
            if debounceFn and debug and debug.getupvalue then
                local ok2, t = pcall(function() return debug.getupvalue(debounceFn, 1) end)
                if ok2 and type(t) == "table" then debounceTable = t end
            end
        end)
        debounceTable = debounceTable or {}
    end
    local v = rawget(debounceTable, SWAP_KEY)
    if type(v) == "number" then return math.max(0, v - os.clock()) end
    return 0
end
local function HoldCooldown(cooldownFn, maxWait)
    maxWait = maxWait or 6
    local deadline = os.clock() + maxWait
    while cooldownFn() > 0 and os.clock() < deadline do task.wait(0.05) end
    return cooldownFn() <= 0
end
local function cloneModel()
    local m = Workspace:FindFirstChild(LOCAL_PLAYER.UserId .. "_Clone")
    return (m and m:IsA("PVInstance")) and m or nil
end
local function waitPlaced(originPos, faceDir, prevModel, prevPos)
    local deadline = os.clock() + clamp(ping() * 3 + 0.4, 0.6, 2.0)
    repeat
        task.wait()
        local m = cloneModel()
        local p = m and m:GetPivot().Position
        if m and p and (m ~= prevModel or not prevPos or ((p - prevPos) * XZ).Magnitude > 0.5) then
            -- EASY ACCEPT: a fresh/moved clone anywhere near our spot = placed
            -- (the old check demanded >=1.5 studs FORWARD -> false "missed clone")
            local d = (p - originPos) * XZ
            if d.Magnitude < 8 then return true end
        end
    until os.clock() > deadline
    -- last resort: a clone model standing near us at all = placed, never "missed"
    local m = cloneModel()
    local p = m and m:GetPivot().Position
    if m and p and (p - originPos).Magnitude < 30 then return true end
    return false
end
local function instantCloner(faceDir)
    local char, hrp, humanoid = getParts()
    local cloner = findClonerTool()
    if not char or not hrp or not humanoid then return false, "no character" end
    if not cloner then return false, "no Quantum Cloner" end
    if not HoldCooldown(SwapLeft) then return false, "cloner on cooldown" end
    humanoid:UnequipTools()
    task.wait()
    if cloner.Parent ~= char then
        pcall(function() humanoid:EquipTool(cloner) end)
        local t0 = os.clock()
        while cloner.Parent ~= char and os.clock() - t0 < 1 do RunService.Heartbeat:Wait() end
        if cloner.Parent ~= char then return false, "cloner equip failed" end
        task.wait(clamp(ping() * 2, 0.1, 0.4))
    end
    local look = hrp.CFrame.LookVector * XZ
    if faceDir and (look.Magnitude < 1e-4 or look.Unit:Dot(faceDir) < 0.99) then
        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + faceDir)
        vZero(hrp)
        task.wait(clamp(ping() * 2, 0.1, 0.4))
    end
    local origin = hrp.Position
    local face = hrp.CFrame.LookVector * XZ
    face = (face.Magnitude > 1e-4) and face.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
    local prevModel = cloneModel()
    local prevPos = prevModel and prevModel:GetPivot().Position
    pcall(function() cloner:Activate() end)
    if not waitPlaced(origin, face, prevModel, prevPos) then
        pcall(function() cloner:Activate() end)
        if not waitPlaced(origin, face, prevModel, prevPos) then return false, "clone was not placed" end
    end
    return true
end
-- ===========================================================================
-- AUTO-GRAB: fire the brainrot's ProximityPrompt
-- ===========================================================================
local function autoGrab(target)
    if not CONFIG.AutoGrab then return false, "auto-grab off" end
    if not target then return false, "no target" end
    -- re-resolve prompt (it may have changed)
    local prompt = target.prompt
    if not prompt or not prompt.Parent then
        local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        local plot = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(target.plot)
        prompt = plot and getPetPrompt(plot, target.slot)
        target.prompt = prompt
    end
    if not prompt then return false, "no prompt" end

    -- preferred: executor fireproximityprompt
    local fpp = fireproximityprompt or _G.fireproximityprompt
    if fpp then
        print("[AYDN] auto-grab via fireproximityprompt")
        pcall(fpp, prompt)
        task.wait(0.1)
        pcall(fpp, prompt)  -- second tap in case HoldDuration>0
        return true
    end
    -- fallback: drive the prompt hold manually
    print("[AYDN] auto-grab via InputHoldBegin/End")
    pcall(function()
        local hd = prompt.HoldDuration
        if hd and hd > 0 then prompt.HoldDuration = 0 end
    end)
    pcall(function() prompt:InputHoldBegin() end)
    task.wait(0.1)
    pcall(function() prompt:InputHoldEnd() end)
    return true
end

-- ===========================================================================
-- AUTO-GO TO BRAINROT: after landing in the base, travel to the brainrot podium
-- using the SAME cruise + tween method we already use, then leave the grab to autoGrab.
-- ===========================================================================
local function autoGoToBrainrot(target)
    if not target or not target.position then return false, "no target pos" end
    local hrp = getHRP()
    local dest = target.position
    -- stop ~2 studs short of the podium so we land close enough
    if hrp then
        local flat = (dest - hrp.Position) * XZ
        if flat.Magnitude > 10 then
            dest = dest - flat.Unit * 2
        end
    end
    setStatus("auto-go to brainrot...")
    print("[AYDN] auto-go to brainrot")
    flyToDestination(dest, CONFIG.TPSpeed, 4, 25)
    tweenToPosition(dest, 2, 5)
    hrp = getHRP()
    if hrp then vZero(hrp) end
    return true
end

-- ===========================================================================
-- MAIN SEQUENCE
--   GO toggles: click GO (or press "\") to start, click GO again to turn OFF.
--   0.1s dead time on every step EXCEPT cruising + cloning.
--   KEEP CLONER OUT: the Quantum Cloner is never unequipped.
-- ===========================================================================
-- ===========================================================================
-- VOID GUARD (the "teleported to the void on the brainrot" fix, part 2):
-- a background watchdog. Anchors are now gone from the whole steal sequence,
-- and if ANYTHING still drops us below the map (anti-cheat, bad snap, moving
-- podium), we unanchor + pull back to the last spot we were really standing
-- on. You can never get lost in the void anymore.
-- ===========================================================================
do
    local lastSafe = nil
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(0.35)
            local hrp = getHRP()
            local hum = getHumanoid()
            if hrp and hrp.Parent then
                local p = hrp.Position
                if p.Y > -15 and hum and hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then
                    lastSafe = p
                elseif p.Y < -55 and lastSafe then
                    pcall(function()
                        hrp.Anchored = false
                        vZero(hrp)
                        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(lastSafe + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0))
                    end)
                    lastSafe = nil
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function stopRun()
    stopRequested = true
    local hrp = getHRP()
    if hrp then
        pcall(function() hrp.Anchored = false end)
        vZero(hrp)
    end
    destroyAllPlatforms()
    setStatus("off")
end

local function runSequence(forced)
    if running then return end
    running = true
    stopRequested = false
    syncConfig()

    local target = forced or nil
    if not target and TP_MANUAL_PET then
        -- manual pick from the Targets panel: re-resolve the SAME pet live so
        -- position/prompt are current; gone -> clear pick, fall back to auto.
        -- AYDN LAG FIX: force=true -> GO never reads the 0.5s shared cache.
        local list = scanAllPets(true)
        for _, p in ipairs(list) do
            if p.uid == TP_MANUAL_PET.uid then target = p break end
        end
        if not target then
            TP_MANUAL_PET = nil
            warn("[AYDN] manual target gone from the server -> auto pick")
        end
    end
    if not target then target = findTargetIn(scanAllPets(true)) end
    if not target then
        warn("[AYDN] No target found")
        setStatus("no target")
        running = false
        return
    end
    setStatus("target: " .. tostring(target.name) .. " (" .. mpsFmt(target.mps) .. " mps)")
    -- arm the same pet in the main script so its steal bar / prompt firing follow us
    pcall(function()
        if _G.Elite_SwitchStealTarget then _G.Elite_SwitchStealTarget(target) end
    end)
    print(("[AYDN] GO | %s | %s (mps=%s) mut=%s plot=%s slot=%s"):format(
        CONFIG.Mode, target.name, mpsFmt(target.mps),
        tostring(target.mutation), tostring(target.plot), tostring(target.slot)))

    local destPos, facingDir, isUpperFloor, dropBelow = computeDestination(target)
    print(("[AYDN] Dest %s | facing %s | upper=%s drop=%s"):format(
        tostring(destPos), tostring(facingDir), tostring(isUpperFloor), tostring(dropBelow)))

    -- AYDN gear steps (YOUR spec, BEFORE ANYTHING MOVES): pull out the flying
    -- gear chosen in AYDN SETTINGS -> ACTIVATE it for 1 second -> UNEQUIP
    -- -> re-pull it out (JUST HOLDING it, no activation) -> GO.
    pcall(function() _G.AYDN_GearPulse() end)
    task.wait(0.1)
    print("[AYDN] Gear ready (activated 1s, re-pulled as HOLD) -> GO")
    setStatus("flying to dest...")
    if stopRequested then running = false stopRequested = false return end

    local hrp = getHRP()
    -- EXACT COCOX: turn to the grid facing BEFORE the flight (rotation only,
    -- position untouched). doVelocityTP does this right after picking the
    -- destination, so route + arrival snap share one facing. No pre-flight
    -- platform -- CocoX spawns the clone platform after landing instead.
    if hrp and facingDir and facingDir.Magnitude > 0.1 then
        pcall(function()
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + facingDir.Unit)
        end)
    end

    -- cruise (TP SPEED, CLOSE SPEED inside CloseDistance studs) + tween
    local okCruise = flyToDestination(destPos, CONFIG.TPSpeed, CONFIG.CruiseStopRange, CONFIG.MaxTime)
    print(("[AYDN] Cruise %s"):format(okCruise and "OK" or "FAIL/BAIL"))
    setStatus("cruise " .. (okCruise and "ok" or "bail"))
    if stopRequested then destroyAllPlatforms() running = false stopRequested = false return end
    local okTween = tweenToPosition(destPos, CONFIG.TweenStopRange, CONFIG.TweenMaxTime)
    print(("[AYDN] Tween %s"):format(okTween and "OK" or "FAIL"))
    if stopRequested then destroyAllPlatforms() running = false stopRequested = false return end

    -- EXACT COCOX SKY CLONE-TP ARRIVAL (doVelocityTP, verbatim): hard CFrame
    -- ONTO the grid coordinate + facing -- this is what lands the clone
    -- INSIDE the base -- then 5 sync frames holding that exact CFrame, then
    -- wait until the body is grounded. No velocity-dock-and-give-up: the
    -- snap IS the arrival.
    hrp = getHRP()
    if hrp and hrp.Parent then
        pcall(function()
            hrp.Anchored = false
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(destPos, destPos + facingDir)
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)
        local _syncLeft = 5
        local _syncConn
        _syncConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local cur = getHRP()
            if not cur or not cur.Parent or stopRequested then
                if _syncConn then _syncConn:Disconnect() end
                return
            end
            _syncLeft = _syncLeft - 1
            pcall(function()
                cur.CFrame = CFrame.new(destPos, destPos + facingDir)
                cur.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                cur.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end)
            if _syncLeft <= 0 and _syncConn then _syncConn:Disconnect() end
        end)
        while _syncConn.Connected do task.wait(0.03) end
        -- grounded wait (CocoX: 20 x 0.05s, break once FloorMaterial ~= Air)
        for _ = 1, 20 do
            if stopRequested then break end
            task.wait(0.05)
            local _hum = getHumanoid()
            if _hum and _hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then break end
        end
        print("[AYDN] EXACT CocoX snap: on grid coord, grounded")
    end
    setStatus("snapped - pinning...")
    if stopRequested then destroyAllPlatforms() running = false stopRequested = false return end

    -- EXACT COCOX: invisible one-way clone platform under the body while the
    -- clone is placed and until the swap lands (doVelocityTP, verbatim sizes).
    spawnClonePlatform((hrp and hrp.Position) or destPos)
    if CONFIG.CloneDelay > 0 then
        print(("[AYDN] Clone delay: %.2fs"):format(CONFIG.CloneDelay))
        task.wait(CONFIG.CloneDelay)
    end

    -- SOFT PIN (keep looking forward): freeze exactly here so the clone drops on our
    -- spot. AYDN FIX: never Anchored=true on/next to the brainrot -- that is
    -- what teleported you into the void. A Heartbeat CFrame pin does the same
    -- freeze with physics fully live and zero anchoring.
    hrp = getHRP()
    local faceDir
    local pinConn
    if hrp then
        -- EXACT COCOX: hold the snapped grid CFrame (position + facing) while
        -- the clone drops -- the same CFrame the arrival snap set.
        faceDir = facingDir
        local pinCF = CFrame.new(destPos, destPos + facingDir)
        pinConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local cur = getHRP()
            if not cur or not cur.Parent then return end
            pcall(function()
                cur.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                cur.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
                cur.CFrame = pinCF
            end)
        end)
        setStatus("pinned - placing clone...")
        print("[AYDN] soft-pinned (no anchor) until clone is placed")
    end

    local okInstant, errInstant = instantCloner(faceDir)
    print(("[AYDN] Instant %s"):format(okInstant and "OK" or ("FAIL: " .. tostring(errInstant))))

    -- clone IS placed (or placement failed) -> release the soft pin, never frozen
    if pinConn then
        pcall(function() pinConn:Disconnect() end)
        pinConn = nil
    end
    -- KEEP CLONER OUT: no unequip here -- it stays in your hand for YOUR click
    destroyAllPlatforms()
    hrp = getHRP()
    if hrp then
        pcall(function() hrp.Anchored = false end) -- safety: never stay anchored
        vZero(hrp)
    end

    if okInstant then
        -- FULL AUTO: fire the game's own "Change With Clone" (TeleportToClone)
        -- button the moment the clone is placed, then keep going by itself.
        setStatus("clone placed - swapping with clone...")
        print("[AYDN] clone placed - auto 'Change With Clone'")
        local swapLanded = false
        _G.isCloning = true
        task.delay(12, function() _G.isCloning = false end)
        for attempt = 1, 3 do
            if stopRequested then break end
            pcall(function()
                local pg = LOCAL_PLAYER:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
                local tf = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("ToolsFrames")
                local qc = tf and tf:FindFirstChild("QuantumCloner")
                local tb = qc and qc:FindFirstChild("TeleportToClone")
                if tb then
                    tb.Visible = true
                    local fsg = firesignal or _G.firesignal
                    if fsg then
                        pcall(fsg, tb.MouseButton1Click)
                        pcall(fsg, tb.MouseButton1Up)
                        pcall(fsg, tb.Activated)
                    end
                end
            end)
            -- swap lands = character replaced or moved off the clone spot
            local startHrp = getHRP()
            local startSwapPos = startHrp and startHrp.Position
            local deadline = os.clock() + 5
            while os.clock() < deadline do
                if stopRequested then break end
                local cur = getHRP()
                if not cur or not cur.Parent then swapLanded = true break end
                if cur ~= startHrp then swapLanded = true break end
                if startSwapPos and ((cur.Position - startSwapPos) * XZ).Magnitude > 3 then
                    swapLanded = true
                    break
                end
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
            if swapLanded or stopRequested then break end
            print("[AYDN] swap did not land - retry 'Change With Clone'")
            task.wait(0.3)
        end
        _G.isCloning = false
        destroyClonePlatform() -- EXACT COCOX: the platform dies once the swap has landed

        if stopRequested then
            destroyAllPlatforms()
            running = false
            stopRequested = false
            return
        end
        if swapLanded then
            setStatus("swap landed - going to brainrot...")
            print("[AYDN] swap landed")
        else
            setStatus("swap failed - going to brainrot anyway...")
            print("[AYDN] swap did not land - continuing to brainrot")
        end

        -- safety unanchor in case the swap respawned/replaced the character
        hrp = getHRP()
        if hrp then
            pcall(function() hrp.Anchored = false end)
            vZero(hrp)
        end

        -- now that we're in the base, actually go to the brainrot
        task.wait(0.1)
        equipTool(isCloner, 0.5) -- KEEP CLONER OUT after the swap respawn
        -- AYDN (user spec): the clone swap already put the fresh body on the
        -- spot the CocoX flight landed you on -- STAY THERE. No re-dock to the
        -- precomputed grid coord, no sideways turn after the swap; auto-go
        -- below walks the rest of the way to the brainrot.
        -- AYDN: if the grab already landed (Stealing attribute true) the TP
        -- run is DONE -> turn TP off instead of walking on.
        if LOCAL_PLAYER:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then
            destroyAllPlatforms()
            running = false
            stopRequested = false
            setStatus("done - stealing (tp off)")
            return
        end
        task.spawn(function()
            while running and not stopRequested do
                if LOCAL_PLAYER:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then
                    stopRequested = true
                    local h2 = getHRP()
                    if h2 then vZero(h2) end
                    break
                end
                task.wait(0.15)
            end
        end)
        autoGoToBrainrot(target)

        if CONFIG.AutoGrab and not stopRequested then
            task.wait(0.1)
            -- THE ELITE GRAB: the grab after a successful arrival is now the
            -- elite grab from the main script -- we (re)arm its steal system
            -- on the pet we just reached, and its armed loop performs the grab
            -- with the steal bar + hold callbacks + huge MaxActivationDistance.
            setStatus("elite grab...")
            print("[AYDN] elite grab: arming main steal system on arrival pet")
            pcall(function()
                if _G.Elite_SwitchStealTarget then _G.Elite_SwitchStealTarget(target) end
            end)
            -- small grace window for the elite grab to take, then one plain
            -- prompt fire as a safety net (same prompt, harmless double-fire)
            task.wait(1.0)
            if not stopRequested then
                local ok, why = autoGrab(target)
                print(("[AYDN] EliteGrab %s"):format(ok and "OK" or ("SKIP: " .. tostring(why))))
                setStatus(ok and "done - grabbed (elite)" or ("grab skip: " .. tostring(why)))
            end
        else
            setStatus("done (auto-grab off)")
        end
    else
        setStatus("instant fail: " .. tostring(errInstant))
    end

    -- KEEP CLONER OUT: never unequip at the end -- it stays in your hand
    task.wait(0.1)
    destroyAllPlatforms()
    running = false
    stopRequested = false
end

setStatus = function(s)
    STATUS_TEXT = tostring(s or "")
end

-- ===========================================================================
-- AYDN TP API -- ALL controls live in the MAIN AYDN GUI now:
--   * TP GO button (+" backslash hotkey)      -> _G.AYDN_TP.Toggle()
--   * TP Speed / Close Speed / Close Dist / Clone Delay / Auto-Grab sliders
--     in AYDN SETTINGS -> stored in Config.TpSettings, saved to EliteHub.json
-- ===========================================================================
local function startRun(forced)
    if running then stopRun() end
    stopRequested = false
    syncConfig()
    task.spawn(function()
        _G.AYDN_TP_ACTIVE = true
        local ok, err = pcall(runSequence, forced)
        _G.AYDN_TP_ACTIVE = false
        if not ok then
            warn("[AYDN] runSequence error: " .. tostring(err))
            setStatus("error: " .. tostring(err))
        end
    end)
end
local function toggleGo(forced)
    if running then
        stopRun()
        return
    end
    startRun(forced)
end
-- adapt a main-script pet record (Elite_StealTarget) to this engine's format
local function toEnginePet(p)
    if type(p) ~= "table" then return nil end
    local pos = p.position or p.plotHint
    if typeof(pos) ~= "Vector3" then return nil end
    return {
        name = tostring(p.name or p.index or ""),
        index = tostring(p.index or p.name or ""),
        mps = tonumber(p.mps) or 0,
        mutation = p.mutation,
        traits = p.traits,
        position = pos,
        plot = tostring(p.plot or ""),
        slot = tostring(p.slot or ""),
        uid = tostring(p.plot or "") .. "_" .. tostring(p.slot or ""),
        prompt = p.prompt,
    }
end
_G.AYDN_TP = {
    Toggle = function(forced)
        toggleGo(type(forced) == "table" and toEnginePet(forced) or nil)
    end,
    Start = function(forced)
        startRun(type(forced) == "table" and toEnginePet(forced) or nil)
    end,
    Stop = function()
        if running then stopRun() end
    end,
    Running = function()
        return running
    end,
    ManualTP = function(pet)
        -- AYDN (user rule): selecting a new brainrot mid-flight redirects the
        -- TP -- stop the current run, then launch straight at the new pick.
        if running then
            stopRun()
            local _mt0 = os.clock()
            while running and os.clock() - _mt0 < 2 do task.wait(0.05) end
        end
        local eng = toEnginePet(pet)
        if pet ~= nil and eng == nil then
            setStatus("manual tp: target has no position")
            warn("[AYDN] Manual TP: selected target has no position yet")
            return
        end
        -- arm the same pet in the main script (steal bar + auto prompt fire)
        pcall(function()
            if eng and _G.Elite_SwitchStealTarget then
                _G.Elite_SwitchStealTarget(pet)
            end
        end)
        startRun(eng)
    end,
    SyncConfig = function()
        syncConfig()
    end,
    Status = function()
        return STATUS_TEXT
    end,
}

syncConfig()
task.spawn(function()
    loadDataModules()
    loadSynchronizer()
    syncConfig()
    print("[AYDN] TP engine merged | TP GO / speed sliders live in the main GUI")
end)

end

local function wipeGui(name)
local seen = {}
local function kill(parent)
if not parent or seen[parent] then return end
seen[parent] = true
local o = parent:FindFirstChild(name)
if o then o:Destroy() end
end
kill(root)
pcall(function() kill(_G.AYDN_GuiHost()) end)
kill(LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui"))
end
wipeGui("EliteNearest")
wipeGui("eliteNearest")
wipeGui("ElitePrivate")
wipeGui("ChatWindowSettings") -- renamed hub: clean old copies of the new name too
if _G.__Elite_PsKickConn then
pcall(function() _G.__Elite_PsKickConn:Disconnect() end)
_G.__Elite_PsKickConn = nil
end
local g = Instance.new("ScreenGui")

-- flag -- the hub ships as a boring settings gui under the protected host.

end)
local COL_CARD = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 15)
local COL_OFF = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 22)
local COL_ON = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)
local COL_ACCENT = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)
local COL_TEXT = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)
local COL_MUTED = Color3.fromRGB(140, 140, 140)
local COL_STROKE = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
local COL_ON_STROKE = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
local function uiCorner(p, r)
local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r or 12)
c.Parent = p
return c
end
local function uiStroke(p, color, thick, trans)
local s = Instance.new("UIStroke")
s.Color = color or COL_STROKE
s.Thickness = thick or 1
s.Transparency = trans or 0.86
s.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
s.Parent = p
return s
end
local function setOnLook(b, on)
b:SetAttribute("On", on == true)
b.BackgroundColor3 = on and COL_ON or COL_OFF
b.TextColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(9, 9, 9) or COL_TEXT
local st = b:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
if st then
st.Color = on and COL_ON_STROKE or COL_STROKE
st.Transparency = on and 0.35 or 0.86
st.Thickness = 1
end
end

local function savePos(name, frame)
local p = frame.Position
uiPos[name] = { p.X.Scale, p.X.Offset, p.Y.Scale, p.Y.Offset }
_G.Elite_UiPos = uiPos
if type(_G.EliteSaveUis) == "function" then
pcall(_G.EliteSaveUis)
end
if type(_G.EliteSaveSettings) == "function" then
pcall(_G.EliteSaveSettings)
end
end
local function loadPos(name, fallback)
local p = uiPos[name]
if type(p) == "table" and p[2] ~= nil then
return UDim2.new(p[1] or 0, p[2], p[3] or 0, p[4])
end
return fallback
end
local function makeDrag(frame, handle, name)
local dragging, origin, startPos
handle.Active = true
handle.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
dragging = true
origin = i.Position
startPos = frame.Position
local conn
conn = i.Changed:Connect(function()
if i.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
dragging = false
savePos(name, frame)
conn:Disconnect()
end
end)
end
end)
UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
if dragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
local d = i.Position - origin
frame.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
end
end)
end
local function makePanel(o)
-- AYDN black & white (slim + cooler)
local f = Instance.new("Frame")
f.Name = o.name
f.Size = o.h and UDim2.fromOffset(o.w, o.h) or UDim2.fromOffset(o.w, 0)
if not o.h then
f.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
end
f.Position = o.pos
f.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 12)
f.BackgroundTransparency = 0
f.BorderSizePixel = 0
f.Active = true
f.Visible = o.visible ~= false
f.ZIndex = o.z or 1
f.Parent = g
uiCorner(f, 8)
local st = uiStroke(f, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), 1.35, 0)
-- traveling edge highlight
local edgeGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
edgeGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)),
})
edgeGrad.Rotation = 0
edgeGrad.Parent = st
pcall(function()
    game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(edgeGrad, TweenInfo.new(6, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1), {Rotation = 360}):Play()
end)
local grad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
grad.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18), Color3.fromRGB(6, 6, 6))
grad.Rotation = 90
grad.Parent = f
-- slim header (compact = extra small header for the tiny AP panel)
local head = Instance.new("Frame")
head.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, o.compact and 24 or 34)
head.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18)
head.BorderSizePixel = 0
head.ZIndex = (o.z or 1) + 1
head.Parent = f
uiCorner(head, 8)
local headGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
headGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 28), Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 12))
headGrad.Rotation = 90
headGrad.Parent = head
local headSq = Instance.new("Frame")
headSq.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -10)
headSq.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 8)
headSq.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 12)
headSq.BorderSizePixel = 0
headSq.ZIndex = (o.z or 1) + 1
headSq.Parent = head
-- white A logo
local logo = Instance.new("TextLabel")
logo.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(o.compact and 6 or 8, o.compact and 3 or 6)
logo.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(o.compact and 16 or 20, o.compact and 16 or 20)
logo.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)
logo.BorderSizePixel = 0
logo.Text = "A"
logo.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(9, 9, 9)
logo.TextSize = o.compact and 9 or 11
logo.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
logo.ZIndex = (o.z or 1) + 2
logo.Parent = head
uiCorner(logo, 6)
local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
title.Size = UDim2.new(1, o.compact and -30 or -38, 0, o.compact and 12 or 14)
title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(o.compact and 25 or 32, o.compact and 1 or 5)
title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
title.TextSize = o.compact and 9 or 10
title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)
title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
title.Text = o.title
title.Active = true
title.ZIndex = (o.z or 1) + 2
title.Parent = head
local sig = Instance.new("TextLabel")
sig.BackgroundTransparency = 1
sig.Size = UDim2.new(1, o.compact and -30 or -38, 0, 10)
sig.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(o.compact and 25 or 32, o.compact and 13 or 19)
sig.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
sig.TextSize = o.compact and 6 or 7
sig.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 130)
sig.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
sig.Text = "AYDN PUBS"
sig.ZIndex = (o.z or 1) + 2
sig.Parent = head
local line = Instance.new("Frame")
line.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
line.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
line.BorderSizePixel = 0
line.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, o.compact and 23 or 33)
line.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
line.ZIndex = (o.z or 1) + 2
line.Parent = f
local body = Instance.new("Frame")
body.BackgroundTransparency = 1
body.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, o.compact and 25 or 36)
if o.h then
body.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, o.compact and -33 or -44)
else
body.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
body.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
end
body.ZIndex = (o.z or 1) + 1

local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")

lay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
lay.Parent = body
local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
pad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, o.compact and 4 or 6)
pad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, o.compact and 5 or 8)
pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, o.compact and 6 or 8)
pad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, o.compact and 6 or 8)
pad.Parent = body
makeDrag(f, head, o.name)
makeDrag(f, title, o.name)
return f, body
end

local panel, panelBody = makePanel({
name = "Main",
title = "AYDN PUBS",
w = 138,
