local tgtBtns = {}
local function petKey(p)
if not p then return "" end
return tostring(p.plot) .. "|" .. tostring(p.slot) .. "|" .. tostring(p.index or p.name) .. "|" .. tostring(p.mutation or "")
end
local function currentKey()
return petKey(_G.Elite_StealTarget)
end
-- AYDN (user rule): the white SELECTED highlight made the button unreadable
-- (white text on the white selected background). Selected buttons now paint
-- dark text and drop the white text stroke, so everything stays readable.
local function paintTargetBtn(b, on)
b.BackgroundColor3 = on and COL_ON or COL_OFF
b.TextColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(9, 9, 9) or COL_TEXT
if b:GetAttribute("AYDN_StrokeWhite") then
b.TextStrokeTransparency = on and 1 or 0.8
end
end
-- AYDN: mutation colors (user palette) -- base = no color, just the name
local MUT_COLORS = {
gold = { "FFC933" },
diamond = { "35C8FF" },
bloodrot = { "B01616" },
candy = { "FF8FB8" },
lava = { "FF7A29" },
galaxy = { "A17BFF" },
radioactive = { "3FE03F" },
phantom = { "050505" },
["yin yang"] = { "0A0A0A", "F2F2F2" },
eclipse = { "8B3FE8", "FFC933" },
hacker = { "2FD55B", "0A0A0A" },
cyber = { "2BE0FF", "0A0A0A" },
divine = { "FFD75E", "FFFFFF" },
cursed = { "E5484D", "0A0A0A" },
crystal = { "A6E3FF" },
celestial = { "7FD4FF" },
}
local RAINBOW = { "FF3B30", "FF9F0A", "FFCC00", "34C759", "32ADE6", "5E5CE6", "BF5AF2" }
local function mutKey(mut)
local s = string.lower(tostring(mut or ""))
s = string.gsub(s, "[^%w%s]", " ")
s = string.gsub(s, "%s+", " ")
s = string.gsub(s, "^%s+", "")
s = string.gsub(s, "%s+$", "")
if s == "" or s == "none" or s == "base" then return nil end
if s == "yinyang" then return "yin yang" end
return s
end
local function escRich(s)
s = tostring(s or "")
s = string.gsub(s, "&", "&amp;")
s = string.gsub(s, "<", "&lt;")
s = string.gsub(s, ">", "&gt;")
return s
end
local function fmtGen(v)
v = tonumber(v) or 0
local units = { { 1e12, "T" }, { 1e9, "B" }, { 1e6, "M" }, { 1e3, "K" } }
for _, u in ipairs(units) do
if v >= u[1] then return string.format("$%.1f%s/s", v / u[1], u[2]) end
end
return string.format("$%d/s", math.floor(v))
end
-- "Name  $gen/s  MUTATION": gen shown WITHOUT the word gen; mutation colored
local function targetLabel(pet)
local name = escRich(tostring(pet.name or pet.index or "?"))
local gen = fmtGen(pet.mps)
local key = mutKey(pet.mutation)
local mutText = ""
local strokeWhite = false
if key then
local word = tostring(pet.mutation)
if key == "rainbow" then
local segs = {}
for i = 1, #word do
segs[#segs + 1] = string.format('<font color="#%s">%s</font>',
RAINBOW[((i - 1) % #RAINBOW) + 1], escRich(string.sub(word, i, i)))
end
mutText = table.concat(segs)
else
local cols = MUT_COLORS[key]
if cols and #cols == 2 and #word >= 2 then
local half = math.ceil(#word / 2)
mutText = string.format('<font color="#%s">%s</font><font color="#%s">%s</font>',
cols[1], escRich(string.sub(word, 1, half)),
cols[2], escRich(string.sub(word, half + 1)))
strokeWhite = (cols[1] == "050505") or (cols[2] == "0A0A0A")
elseif cols then
mutText = string.format('<font color="#%s">%s</font>', cols[1], escRich(word))
strokeWhite = (cols[1] == "050505")
else
mutText = escRich(word)
end
end
end
return name .. '  <font color="#9AA0A6">' .. gen .. "</font>  " .. mutText, strokeWhite
end
local function mkTargetBtn(pet, order)
local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
b.Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, 20)
b.BackgroundColor3 = COL_OFF
b.TextColor3 = COL_TEXT
b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
b.TextSize = 8
b.TextWrapped = true
b.AutoButtonColor = false
b.RichText = true
b.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
b.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
b.LayoutOrder = order
b.Parent = scroll
uiCorner(b, 8)
uiStroke(b)
local label, strokeWhite = targetLabel(pet)
b.Text = label
if strokeWhite then
b.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.8
b:SetAttribute("AYDN_StrokeWhite", true)
end
b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
-- AYDN (user rules, round 3):
--   * clicking a brainrot SELECTS it and AUTO GRABS it when you get close
--     -- a plain click never TPs anymore.
--   * clicking the ALREADY-SELECTED brainrot is the TP: it flies straight
--     to that one. One shared handler for BOTH tabs.
local curSel = _G.Elite_StealTarget
local sameSel = curSel and curSel.plot == pet.plot
and tostring(curSel.slot) == tostring(pet.slot)
if sameSel then
if _G.AYDN_TP and _G.AYDN_TP.ManualTP then
pcall(function() _G.AYDN_TP.ManualTP(pet) end)
end
return
end
-- new selection: arm it (steal bar + auto grab follow it) -- NO TP. If a
-- re-click flight is still running, stop it so the grab takes over.
if _G.AYDN_TP and _G.AYDN_TP.Running and _G.AYDN_TP.Running() and _G.AYDN_TP.Stop then
pcall(function() _G.AYDN_TP.Stop() end)
end
if _G.Elite_SwitchStealTarget then
pcall(_G.Elite_SwitchStealTarget, pet)
end
end)
return b
end
local lastSig = ""
-- AYDN (user rule): join tracking, rebuilt -- seenEver never forgets a pet
-- key this session, plotSeen remembers when a base last held a priority pet
-- (60s memory) and robbedPlots marks bases the local player robbed. The join
-- sound can only ring for a never-seen key on a plot that is neither
-- recently seen nor robbed: tab switches and post-steal replacements stay
-- silent forever.
local seenEver = {}
local plotSeen = {}
local robbedPlots = {}
local prioBooted = false
local prioSnd = Instance.new("Sound")
prioSnd.Name = "AydnPrioAppear"
prioSnd.SoundId = "rbxassetid://828172750"
prioSnd.Volume = 0.9
prioSnd.Parent = g
task.defer(function()
    pcall(function()
        prioSnd:Play()
        task.wait(0.03)
        prioSnd:Stop()
        prioSnd.TimePosition = 0
    end)
end)
local function playPrioSound()
    pcall(function()
        if prioSnd.IsPlaying then prioSnd:Stop() end
        prioSnd.TimePosition = 0
        prioSnd:Play()
    end)
end
-- AYDN (user rule): a base you STEAL from never rings the join sound again
-- this session -- the replacement that spawns there is not a priority join.
local _stealMarkLP = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
_stealMarkLP:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
if _stealMarkLP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then
local t = _G.Elite_StealTarget
if type(t) == "table" and t.plot ~= nil then
robbedPlots[tostring(t.plot)] = true
end
end
end)
local function listForMode()
local pets = {}
if tgtMode == "Priority" then
if _G.Elite_ListPrioPets then
local ok, listed = pcall(_G.Elite_ListPrioPets)
if ok and type(listed) == "table" then
pets = listed
end
end
return pets
end
-- Target mode: the TOP N best (highest gen) brainrots in the whole server
if _G.Elite_ListAllPets then
local ok, all = pcall(_G.Elite_ListAllPets)
if ok and type(all) == "table" then
local copy = {}
for i = 1, #all do copy[#copy + 1] = all[i] end
table.sort(copy, function(a, b)
return (tonumber(a._mp or a.mps) or 0) > (tonumber(b._mp or b.mps) or 0)
end)
local n = math.clamp(tonumber(topN) or 3, 1, 20)
for i = 1, math.min(n, #copy) do pets[i] = copy[i] end
end
end
return pets
end
local function refreshTargets()
-- AYDN (user rule): PRIORITY JOIN watch -- runs on EVERY refresh no matter
-- which tab is open. Sound + auto jump to the Priority tab fire ONLY for a
-- genuinely NEW priority pet: a key this server never had, on a base that
-- has not held a priority pet recently and was never robbed. Tab switches
-- and the replacement that spawns after a steal can never ring it again.
do
local priList = {}
if _G.Elite_ListPrioPets then
local ok2, listed2 = pcall(_G.Elite_ListPrioPets)
if ok2 and type(listed2) == "table" then priList = listed2 end
end
local nowT = os.clock()
if not prioBooted then
prioBooted = true
local bootList = priList
if #bootList == 0 and _G.Elite_ListAllPets then
local ok3, all3 = pcall(_G.Elite_ListAllPets)
if ok3 and type(all3) == "table" and #all3 > 0 then bootList = all3 end
end
for i = 1, #bootList do
seenEver[petKey(bootList[i])] = true
plotSeen[tostring(bootList[i].plot or bootList[i].uid or "?")] = nowT
end
else
local joinPet = nil
for i = 1, #priList do
local p2 = priList[i]
local k2 = petKey(p2)
local plt2 = tostring(p2.plot or p2.uid or "?")
local wasSeen = plotSeen[plt2]
plotSeen[plt2] = nowT
if not joinPet and not seenEver[k2]
and ((wasSeen == nil) or (nowT - wasSeen > 60))
and not robbedPlots[plt2] then
joinPet = p2
end
seenEver[k2] = true
end
if joinPet then
playPrioSound()
-- AYDN: PRIORITY INTERVENE -- a priority pet just joined, so the panel
-- jumps to the Priority tab by itself (sound plays once, for the join).
if tgtMode ~= "Priority" then
tgtMode = "Priority"
_G.AYDN_TargetMode = tgtMode
Config.AydnTargetMode = tgtMode
if type(_G.EliteSaveSettings) == "function" then pcall(_G.EliteSaveSettings) end
paintModes()
lastSig = "" -- the rebuild below now shows the Priority list
end
end
end
end
local pets = listForMode()
local sig = {}
for i = 1, #pets do
    sig[i] = petKey(pets[i])
end
local now = table.concat(sig, ";")
if now ~= lastSig then
    lastSig = now
    for i = 1, #tgtBtns do
        if tgtBtns[i] then tgtBtns[i]:Destroy() end
    end
    tgtBtns = {}
    empty.Visible = #pets == 0
    for i = 1, #pets do
        tgtBtns[i] = mkTargetBtn(pets[i], i)
    end
end
local cur = currentKey()
for i = 1, #tgtBtns do
    paintTargetBtn(tgtBtns[i], sig[i] == cur)
end
end
bTgtMode.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
if tgtMode == "Target" then return end
tgtMode = "Target"
_G.AYDN_TargetMode = tgtMode
Config.AydnTargetMode = tgtMode
if type(_G.EliteSaveSettings) == "function" then pcall(_G.EliteSaveSettings) end
paintModes()
lastSig = "" -- force a rebuild
pcall(refreshTargets)
end)
bPriMode.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
if tgtMode == "Priority" then return end
tgtMode = "Priority"
_G.AYDN_TargetMode = tgtMode
Config.AydnTargetMode = tgtMode
if type(_G.EliteSaveSettings) == "function" then pcall(_G.EliteSaveSettings) end
paintModes()
lastSig = ""
pcall(refreshTargets)
end)
topBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
local n = tonumber(topBox.Text)
if n then
topN = math.clamp(math.floor(n), 1, 20)
Config.AydnTargetTopN = topN
if type(_G.EliteSaveSettings) == "function" then pcall(_G.EliteSaveSettings) end
topBox.Text = tostring(topN)
lastSig = ""
pcall(refreshTargets)
else
topBox.Text = tostring(topN)
end
end)
paintModes()
_G.Elite_OnStealTarget = function()
pcall(refreshTargets)
end
-- AYDN (user rule): the panel auto-updates ITSELF every 4 seconds now --
-- no more list that only moves when you switch tabs. Each tick also runs
-- the priority-join watch above. The loop never exits.
task.spawn(function()
while true do
pcall(refreshTargets)
task.wait(4)
end
end)
-- ===========================================================================
-- AYDN TARGET ENGINE (always on, no toggle):
--   * PRIORITY INSTANT LOCK: the moment a priority brainrot shows up, ONLY
--     that gets grabbed (best rank wins; the top of the priority list always
--     outranks everything else). No TP by itself -- grab radius handles it.
--   * AUTO HIGHEST: the whole map is scanned EVERY 5 SECONDS and the highest
--     value brainrot is armed -- while the grab radius loop keeps grabbing
--     whatever comes in range.
--   * STEAL NEAREST (T / button) still overrides with the nearest brainrot.
-- ===========================================================================
local _selHighestAt = 0
-- ===========================================================================
-- AYDN: PODIUM ESP (best-pet highlight + tag) REMOVED (user rule) -- the ESP
-- best marker is gone entirely. Base visuals live in SHOW BASE IF 30M+ below.
-- ===========================================================================
task.spawn(function()
while true do
task.wait(0.5)
pcall(function()
-- AYDN (user rule): keep charging even while carrying -- the engine keeps
-- re-arming the best target (latched) while you steal one home.
local prios = {}
if _G.Elite_ListPrioPets then
local ok, listed = pcall(_G.Elite_ListPrioPets)
if ok and type(listed) == "table" then prios = listed end
end
-- PRIORITY INSTANT LOCK: only grab that one (Target mode is selection-driven;
-- a user-armed pick stays absolute while it is still on the prio list)
if #prios > 0 and _G.AYDN_TargetMode ~= "Target" then
local cur0 = _G.Elite_StealTarget
local curInList = false
if cur0 then
for i0 = 1, #prios do
local p0 = prios[i0]
if p0 and cur0.plot == p0.plot and tostring(cur0.slot) == tostring(p0.slot) then
curInList = true
break
end
end
end
if not curInList or not (_G.Elite_IsManualPick and _G.Elite_IsManualPick()) then
local best = prios[1]
local cur = _G.Elite_StealTarget
local same = cur and best and cur.plot == best.plot and cur.slot == best.slot
if not same and _G.Elite_SwitchStealTarget and _selShouldSwitch(best, cur) then
_G.Elite_SwitchStealTarget(best)
end
end
return
end
-- AUTO HIGHEST: full rescan every 5 seconds (hands off while a user-armed
-- selection is absolute -- re-click the selected brainrot to go back to auto)
if _G.Elite_IsManualPick and _G.Elite_IsManualPick() then return end
local now = os.clock()
if now - _selHighestAt < 5 then return end
_selHighestAt = now
local all = nil
if _G.Elite_ListAllPets then
local ok, listed = pcall(_G.Elite_ListAllPets)
if ok and type(listed) == "table" then all = listed end
end
if not all then return end
local best
for i = 1, #all do
local p = all[i]
if p._pri == nil then best = p break end -- sorted: prio first, then value
end
best = best or all[1]
if not best then return end
local cur = _G.Elite_StealTarget
local same = cur and cur.plot == best.plot and cur.slot == best.slot
if not same and _G.Elite_SwitchStealTarget and _selShouldSwitch(best, cur, all) then
_G.Elite_SwitchStealTarget(best)
end
end)
end
end)


-- ===========================================================================
-- AYDN PRIO LASER (ported from the twilight hub AutoLaser):
--   * PRIORITY MODE ONLY: fires only while the target GUI mode is "Priority"
--     AND a priority pet is armed (selected).
--   * Targets ONLY players actually STEALING (Stealing attribute) within 14
--     studs of the armed pet's podium.
--   * NEVER lasers friends of the local player.
--   * Laser Cape up close (<= 80 studs), Laser Gun at range (twilight strike
--     chain: LaserGun_Fire -> local projectile -> LaserGun_Impact).
--   * No highlight is drawn anywhere (white ESP highlight removed).
-- ===========================================================================
do
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local LP = Players.LocalPlayer
if _G.AYDN_PrioLaserOn == nil then _G.AYDN_PrioLaserOn = true end
local PET_RADIUS = 14
local CAPE_RANGE = 80
local GUN_FAR = 40
local GUN_SPEED = 100
local GUN_MIN_AGE = 5
local GUN_TRACK = { 3, 0.005, 10 }
local GUN_RADIUS = 6
local GUN_MAX_BOUNCE = 8
local GUN_CD = 0.4
local GUN_CD_HIT = 2.3
local cool = { Cape = 0, Gun = 0 }
local striking = false
local friendCache = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local function isFriend(plr)
if plr == LP then return true end
local hit = friendCache[plr]
if hit ~= nil then return hit end
local ok, res = pcall(function()
return LP:IsFriendsWith(plr.UserId) == true
end)
friendCache[plr] = (ok and res == true) or false
return friendCache[plr]
end
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
friendCache[plr] = nil
end)
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
task.delay(3, function()
pcall(function()
friendCache[plr] = LP:IsFriendsWith(plr.UserId) == true
end)
end)
end)
task.spawn(function()
while true do
task.wait(5)
for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if plr ~= LP and friendCache[plr] == nil then
pcall(function()
friendCache[plr] = LP:IsFriendsWith(plr.UserId) == true
end)
end
end
end
end)
local function netRemote(name)
if type(_G.Elite_GetRemoteByHash) ~= "function" then return nil end
local ok, r = pcall(_G.Elite_GetRemoteByHash, name)
if ok and r and r.Parent then return r end
return nil
end
local function findTool(name)
local char = LP.Character
local bp = LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
local t = (char and char:FindFirstChild(name)) or (bp and bp:FindFirstChild(name))
if t and t:IsA("Tool") then return t end
return nil
end
local function cooling(tool)
local ct = tool:GetAttribute("CooldownTime")
local cu = tool:GetAttribute("CooldownUntil")
return (type(ct) == "number" and ct > 0)
or (type(cu) == "number" and cu > Workspace:GetServerTimeNow())
end
local function ready(kind, tool)
return tool ~= nil and not cooling(tool) and cool[kind] <= os.clock()
end
local function ragdolled(plr)
local t = plr:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime")
return type(t) == "number" and t > Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
end
local function equipTool(tool)
local char = LP.Character
local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not hum or not tool or not tool.Parent then return false end
if tool.Parent == char then return true end
pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(tool) end)
local t0 = os.clock()
while os.clock() - t0 < 1 do
if tool.Parent == char then return true end
RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
end
return tool.Parent == char
end
local function restoreTool(prev)
local char = LP.Character
local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not hum then return end
if prev and prev.Parent and prev.Parent ~= char then
pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(prev) end)
else
pcall(function() hum:UnequipTools() end)
end
end
local function pingWait()
local p = 0.1
pcall(function() p = LP:GetNetworkPing() end)
return math.clamp(p * 3 + 0.25, 0.3, 1)
end
-- Laser Cape: UseItem(position, target root) knocks the thief off the podium
local function strikeCape(tool, victim, root)
local useItem = netRemote("UseItem")
if not useItem then return false end
local prev = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
if not equipTool(tool) then return false end
local ok = pcall(function()
useItem:FireServer(root.Position, root)
end)
task.wait(0.35)
restoreTool(prev)
return ok
end
-- Laser Gun: LaserGun_Fire + local projectile + LaserGun_Impact (twilight)
local function strikeGun(tool, victim, root)
local fire = netRemote("LaserGun_Fire")
local impact = netRemote("LaserGun_Impact")
if not (fire and impact) then return false end
local projClass
pcall(function()
local classes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Classes")
local pm = classes and classes:FindFirstChild("Projectile")
if pm then projClass = require(pm) end
end)
if type(projClass) ~= "table" or type(projClass.new) ~= "function" then return false end
local template
pcall(function()
local shared = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Shared")
local lgs = shared and shared:FindFirstChild("LaserGunsShared")
local models = lgs and lgs:FindFirstChild("Models")
template = models and models:FindFirstChild("Projectile")
end)
local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
local muzzle = handle and handle:FindFirstChild("Muzzle")
if not (muzzle and muzzle:IsA("Attachment")) then return false end
local prev = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
if not equipTool(tool) then return false end
local origin = muzzle.WorldCFrame.Position
local id = tostring(HttpService:GenerateGUID(false)):gsub("-", "")
local dir = (root.Position - origin).Unit
local now = Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
fire:FireServer(id, origin, dir, now)
local dist = (root.Position - origin).Magnitude
local proj
pcall(function()
proj = projClass.new{
Id = id,
CreationTime = now,
Origin = origin,
LinearVelocity = dir * GUN_SPEED,
Radius = 0,
Gravity = Vector3.zero,
MaxAge = math.max(GUN_MIN_AGE, dist / GUN_SPEED + 2),
TrackingRadius = GUN_TRACK[1],
TrackingFalloff = GUN_TRACK[2],
TrackingResponse = GUN_TRACK[3],
FilterDescendantsInstances = (LP.Character and { LP.Character }) or {},
TemplateModel = template,
}
end)
if not proj then
restoreTool(prev)
return false
end
pcall(function() proj.Radius = GUN_RADIUS end)
local impacts = {}
local bounces = 0
pcall(function()
proj.Hit:Connect(function(hitInfo)
bounces += 1
local hitPart = hitInfo and hitInfo.Instance
local rec = {
Timestamp = Workspace:GetServerTimeNow(),
CFrame = proj.CFrame,
LinearVelocity = proj.LinearVelocity,
Age = proj.Age,
HitPart = hitPart,
HitNormal = hitInfo and hitInfo.Normal or nil,
HitPosition = hitInfo and hitInfo.Position or nil,
HitEnding = hitInfo and hitInfo.Ending or nil,
HitAlpha = hitInfo and hitInfo.Alpha or nil,
}
if hitPart then
rec.HitSize = hitPart.Size
rec.HitCFrame = hitPart.CFrame
end
local model = hitPart and hitPart:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
if model and model.PrimaryPart and model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
rec.Character = model
end
table.insert(impacts, rec)
pcall(function()
local v = proj.LinearVelocity
local n = hitInfo.Normal
proj.LinearVelocity = v - 2 * v:Dot(n) * n
if proj.TrackingFalloff ~= nil then proj.TrackingFalloff = proj.TrackingFalloff * 2 end
if proj.TrackingResponse ~= nil then proj.TrackingResponse = proj.TrackingResponse / 2 end
proj.MaxAge = proj.Age + GUN_MIN_AGE
end)
if rec.Character or bounces >= GUN_MAX_BOUNCE then
pcall(function() proj:Destroy() end)
end
end)
end)
local steer
steer = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
local dead = false
pcall(function() dead = proj.Destroyed == true end)
if dead or not root.Parent or not proj then
steer:Disconnect()
return
end
pcall(function()
local d = root.Position - proj.CFrame.Position
if d.Magnitude > 0.1 then
proj.LinearVelocity = d.Unit * GUN_SPEED
end
end)
end)
pcall(function()
proj.Destroying:Connect(function()
pcall(function() steer:Disconnect() end)
pcall(function() impact:FireServer(id, impacts) end)
end)
end)
task.wait(pingWait())
local gotHit = false
pcall(function()
gotHit = type(victim:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime")) == "number"
end)
restoreTool(prev)
return gotHit
end
-- main laser loop
task.spawn(function()
while true do
task.wait(0.15)
if not _G.AYDN_PrioLaserOn then continue end
if _G.AYDN_TargetMode ~= "Priority" then continue end -- ONLY ON PRIORITY
if striking then continue end
if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then continue end
local pet = _G.Elite_StealTarget
if type(pet) ~= "table" or typeof(pet.position) ~= "Vector3" then continue end
local myChar = LP.Character
local myHum = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not myHum or myHum.Health <= 0 then continue end
local best, bestD = nil, math.huge
for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if plr ~= LP and not isFriend(plr) and not ragdolled(plr)
and plr:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then
local ch = plr.Character
local hum = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local vroot = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if hum and hum.Health > 0 and vroot then
local d = (vroot.Position - pet.position).Magnitude
if d <= PET_RADIUS and d < bestD then
bestD, best = d, plr
end
end
end
end
if not best then continue end
local vroot = best.Character and best.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not vroot then continue end
local cape = findTool("Laser Cape")
local gun = findTool("Laser Gun")
local useGun = ready("Gun", gun) and (bestD > GUN_FAR or not cape)
local useCape = (not useGun) and bestD <= CAPE_RANGE and ready("Cape", cape)
if not useGun and not useCape then continue end
striking = true
local victim, vChar = best, vroot
local vDist = bestD
task.spawn(function()
local ok = false
if useGun then
ok = strikeGun(gun, victim, vChar)
else
ok = strikeCape(cape, victim, vChar)
end
cool.Gun = os.clock() + ((useGun and ok) and GUN_CD_HIT or GUN_CD)
cool.Cape = os.clock() + GUN_CD
print("[AYDN] prio laser ->", victim.Name, math.floor(vDist))
striking = false
end)
end
end)
end

local OFF = COL_OFF
local ON = COL_ON
local function mkBtn(text, parent)
local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
b.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
b.BackgroundColor3 = OFF
b.TextColor3 = COL_TEXT
b.Text = text
b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
b.TextSize = 9
b.AutoButtonColor = false
b.Parent = parent or panelBody
uiCorner(b, 8)
uiStroke(b)
b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
if b:GetAttribute("On") then return end
b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(38, 38, 46)
end)
b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
if b:GetAttribute("On") then
b.BackgroundColor3 = COL_ON
else
b.BackgroundColor3 = COL_OFF
end
end)
return b
end

setOnLook(b, on)
b.Text = label .. "  " .. (on and "ON" or "OFF")
end

local function mkSlide(parent, label, minV, maxV, get, set, fmt, order)
local hold = Instance.new("Frame")
hold.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
hold.BackgroundTransparency = 1
hold.LayoutOrder = order or 0
hold.Parent = parent
local lab = Instance.new("TextLabel")
lab.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 12)
lab.BackgroundTransparency = 1
lab.TextColor3 = COL_MUTED
lab.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
lab.TextSize = 9
lab.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
lab.Parent = hold
local sbar = Instance.new("Frame")
sbar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 5)
sbar.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 17)
sbar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(36, 36, 42)
sbar.BorderSizePixel = 0
sbar.Parent = hold
uiCorner(sbar, 99)
local sfill = Instance.new("Frame")
sfill.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)
sfill.BackgroundColor3 = COL_ACCENT
sfill.BorderSizePixel = 0
sfill.Parent = sbar
uiCorner(sfill, 99)
local dragging = false
local function apply(v)
v = math.clamp(tonumber(v) or minV, minV, maxV)
set(v)
sfill.Size = UDim2.new((v - minV) / math.max(0.001, maxV - minV), 0, 1, 0)
lab.Text = label .. ": " .. fmt(v)
end
apply(get())
sbar.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
dragging = true
local rel = math.clamp((i.Position.X - sbar.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, sbar.AbsoluteSize.X), 0, 1)
apply(minV + (maxV - minV) * rel)
end
end)
return {
apply = apply,
bar = sbar,
isDrag = function() return dragging end,
setDrag = function(v) dragging = v end,
minV = minV,
maxV = maxV,
}
end

-- AYDN: INSTANT RESET -- one click: teleport the player to math.huge
local bInstantReset = mkBtn("INSTANT RESET")
bInstantReset.LayoutOrder = 5
bInstantReset.Text = "INSTANT RESET"
bInstantReset.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
-- duck the anti-reset guard for 4s so the reset actually lands
_G.AYDN_ANTI_RESET_OFF = true
task.delay(4, function() _G.AYDN_ANTI_RESET_OFF = nil end)
local plr = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
local char = plr.Character
local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not hrp then
print("[AYDN] instant reset: no character yet")
return
end
task.spawn(function()
print("[AYDN] instant reset: teleporting to math.huge")
-- 1) the math.huge teleport (as specced) -- some engines reject inf, so
-- 2) guaranteed void fallback below FallenPartsDestroyHeight (parts get
--    destroyed server-side) and 3) Health = 0 while the guard is ducked
--    so Died fires and the respawn happens
for _ = 1, 3 do
pcall(function()
hrp.Anchored = false
hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
end)
task.wait(0.1)
end
pcall(function()
hrp.Anchored = false
hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -50000, 0)
end)
task.wait(0.2)
pcall(function()
if hum and hum.Parent then
hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, true)
hum.Health = 0
end
end)
end)
end)


-- ===========================================================================
-- AYDN ANTI RESET v2 -- runs in the background (pasted spec, hardened):
--   * MaxHealth / Health pinned to math.huge; HealthChanged + MaxHealth
--     watchers re-pin on every change; Dead state disabled locally so the
--     humanoid can't enter the death state; BreakJointsOnDeath off
--   * Heartbeat: top Health back up + shove the character back into
--     workspace if something removes it
--   * workspace.ChildRemoved: reclaim the character before respawn kicks in
--   * CharacterAdded: re-arms automatically on every respawn
--   * INSTANT RESET ducks this whole guard for 4s via _G.AYDN_ANTI_RESET_OFF
--     (all watchers + Heartbeat check the flag) so the reset actually lands
--   * fully self-contained scope (services + player captured locally)
--   NOTE: this is client-side. If the SERVER force-kills you or calls
--   LoadCharacter, nothing client-side can block that; this stops local
--   death handling and re-arms instantly after every respawn.
-- ===========================================================================
do
    if type(_G.AYDN_ANTI_RESET_UNLOAD) == "function" then pcall(_G.AYDN_ANTI_RESET_UNLOAD) end
    local PS = game:GetService("Players")
    local RS = game:GetService("RunService")
    local PLR = PS.LocalPlayer
    local arAlive = true
    local arConns = {}
    local function arBind(c)
        if c then arConns[#arConns + 1] = c end
        return c
    end
    local function ducked()
        return _G.AYDN_ANTI_RESET_OFF == true
    end
    local function protect(char)
        local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
        if not hum then return end
        hum.MaxHealth = math.huge
        hum.Health = math.huge
        pcall(function()
            hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
            hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
        end)
        arBind(hum.HealthChanged:Connect(function()
            if arAlive and not ducked() then hum.Health = math.huge end
        end))
        arBind(hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("MaxHealth"):Connect(function()
            if arAlive and not ducked() and hum.MaxHealth ~= math.huge then
                hum.MaxHealth = math.huge
            end
        end))
    end
    if PLR.Character then task.spawn(protect, PLR.Character) end
    arBind(PLR.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
        task.spawn(protect, c)
    end))
    arBind(RS.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not arAlive or ducked() then return end
        local c = PLR.Character
        if not c then return end
        local h = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if h then
            if h.MaxHealth ~= math.huge then h.MaxHealth = math.huge end
            if h.Health < h.MaxHealth then h.Health = h.MaxHealth end
            pcall(function()
                if h.BreakJointsOnDeath then h.BreakJointsOnDeath = false end
                h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
            end)
        end
        -- if character got removed from workspace, shove it back
        if c.Parent ~= workspace then
            pcall(function() c.Parent = workspace end)
        end
    end))
    arBind(workspace.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
        if not arAlive or ducked() then return end
        -- reclaim the character before respawn kicks in
        if child == PLR.Character then
            pcall(function() child.Parent = workspace end)
        end
    end))
    _G.AYDN_ANTI_RESET_UNLOAD = function()
        arAlive = false
        for _, c in ipairs(arConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
        arConns = {}
        _G.AYDN_ANTI_RESET_UNLOAD = nil
    end
    print("[AYDN] ANTI RESET v2 active (background)")
end

-- AYDN: speed boost logic only (no new GUI) — velocity boost + on-steal enable
-- AYDN: LAGGER REMOVED — no more forced Incoming/OutgoingReplicationLag on steal
local speedConn = nil
local currentHRP = nil
local signParts = {}
local signRefreshAccum = 0
local STOP_RADIUS = 20
-- one-time cleanup: make sure no lag left over from an older build is stuck on
pcall(function()
    settings().Network.IncomingReplicationLag = 0
    settings().Network.OutgoingReplicationLag = 0
end)

local function buildMyBaseTexts()
    local texts = {}
    local dn = LocalPlayer.DisplayName
    local un = LocalPlayer.Name
    for _, name in ipairs({ dn, un }) do
        local lname = tostring(name or ""):lower()
        texts[#texts + 1] = lname .. "'s base"
        texts[#texts + 1] = lname .. " base"
        texts[#texts + 1] = lname .. "'s  base"
    end
    return texts
end
local myBaseTexts = buildMyBaseTexts()
pcall(function()
    LocalPlayer:GetPropertyChangedSignal("DisplayName"):Connect(function()
        myBaseTexts = buildMyBaseTexts()
    end)
end)

local function textIsMyBase(txt)
    if not txt or txt == "" then return false end
    local lower = tostring(txt):lower()
    for _, pat in ipairs(myBaseTexts) do
        if lower:find(pat, 1, true) then return true end
    end
    return false
end

local function partHasMyBaseText(part)
    if not part then return false end
    for _, gui in ipairs(part:GetChildren()) do
        if gui:IsA("SurfaceGui") or gui:IsA("BillboardGui") then
            for _, obj in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
                if (obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextBox")) and textIsMyBase(obj.Text) then
                    return true
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if (part:IsA("TextLabel") or part:IsA("TextBox")) and textIsMyBase(part.Text) then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

local function refreshSigns()
    signParts = {}
    pcall(function()
        for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                if partHasMyBaseText(obj) then
                    signParts[#signParts + 1] = obj
                end
            elseif obj:IsA("SurfaceGui") or obj:IsA("BillboardGui") then
                local part = obj.Adornee or obj.Parent
                if part and part:IsA("BasePart") and partHasMyBaseText(part) then
                    local found = false
                    for i = 1, #signParts do
                        if signParts[i] == part then found = true break end
                    end
                    if not found then signParts[#signParts + 1] = part end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function nearMyBaseSign(hrp)
    if not hrp then return false end
    for _, part in ipairs(signParts) do
        if part and part.Parent and (hrp.Position - part.Position).Magnitude <= STOP_RADIUS then
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end

local function stopBoost()
    if speedConn then
        pcall(function() speedConn:Disconnect() end)
        speedConn = nil
    end
    currentHRP = nil
end

local function startBoost()
    stopBoost()
    refreshSigns()
    speedConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function(_, dt)
        if not speedActive then return end
        if _G.AYDN_TP_ACTIVE then return end
        if speedOnlySteal and LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= true then return end
        signRefreshAccum = signRefreshAccum + (dt or 0)
        if signRefreshAccum >= 1 then
            signRefreshAccum = 0
            refreshSigns()
        end
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hrp then return end
        currentHRP = hrp
        if nearMyBaseSign(hrp) then
            -- stop applying near own base
            hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, hrp.Velocity.Y, 0)
            return
        end
        local dir = (hum and hum.MoveDirection) or Vector3.zero
        if dir.Magnitude < 0.01 then
            hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, hrp.Velocity.Y, 0)
            pcall(function() hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, hrp.Velocity.Y, 0) end)
            return
        end
        local spd = tonumber(speedValue) or 31
        local targetVel = dir.Unit * spd
        -- keep vertical from current so jumps still work a bit
        local y = hrp.Velocity.Y
        hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(targetVel.X, y, targetVel.Z)
        pcall(function() hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(targetVel.X, hrp.Velocity.Y, targetVel.Z) end)
    end)
end

-- ACTIVE toggle starts/stops the velocity loop
bSpActive.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if speedActive then
        startBoost()
    else
        stopBoost()
    end
end)
if speedActive then
    task.defer(startBoost)
end

local function fireSetPlayerBlockList()
    pcall(function()
        if Players.SetPlayerBlockList then
            local blockIds = {}
            for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
                    blockIds[#blockIds + 1] = plr.UserId
                end
            end
            if #blockIds > 0 then
                Players:SetPlayerBlockList(blockIds)
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function fireWhyTargeting()
    pcall(function()
        local function findTargetRemote(container)
            if not container then return nil end
            for _, obj in ipairs(container:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj:IsA("RemoteEvent") or obj:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
                    local n = obj.Name:lower()
                    if n:find("target", 1, true)
                        or n:find("why", 1, true)
                        or n:find("targeting", 1, true)
                        or n:find("whotarget", 1, true)
                    then
                        return obj
                    end
                end
            end
            return nil
        end
        local remote = findTargetRemote(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"))
            or findTargetRemote(game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst"))
            or findTargetRemote(workspace)
        if remote then
            if remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                remote:FireServer()
            else
                pcall(function() remote:InvokeServer() end)
            end
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                local n = tostring(obj.ActionText or ""):lower()
                if n:find("target", 1, true) or n:find("why", 1, true) then
                    if fireproximityprompt then
                        fireproximityprompt(obj)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function useGrappleHook()
    pcall(function()
        local controllers = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Controllers")
        if not controllers then return end
        local itemController = controllers:FindFirstChild("ItemController")
        if not itemController then return end
        local grappleHook = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook")
        if not grappleHook then return end
        local getconns = getconnections or (syn and syn.getconnections) or (fluxus and fluxus.getconnections)
        if not getconns then return end
        local function tryRunFunc(func)
            if type(func) ~= "function" then return false end
            local upvalues = debug and debug.getupvalues and debug.getupvalues(func)
            if upvalues then
                for _, up in pairs(upvalues) do
                    if type(up) == "table" and up["Grapple Hook"] then
                        pcall(function() func(grappleHook, 3) end)
                        return true
                    end
                end
            end
            return false
        end
        for _, conn in ipairs(getconns(itemController.Changed)) do
            if tryRunFunc(conn.Function) then return end
        end
        for _, obj in ipairs(itemController:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("BindableEvent") then
                for _, conn in ipairs(getconns(obj.Event)) do
                    if tryRunFunc(conn.Function) then return end
                end
            end
        end
        for _, obj in ipairs(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):GetDescendants()) do
            if (obj:IsA("RemoteEvent") or obj:IsA("RemoteFunction")) and #obj.Name >= 8 then
                for _, conn in ipairs(getconns(obj.OnClientEvent)) do
                    if tryRunFunc(conn.Function) then return end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function onStealing()
    fireSetPlayerBlockList()
    fireWhyTargeting()
    useGrappleHook()
    -- ENABLE speed boost on steal if ONLY ON STEAL toggle is enabled
    if speedOnlySteal then
        if not speedActive then
            speedActive = true
            Config.AydnSpeedActive = true
            paintSpActive()
            if type(_G.EliteSaveSettings) == "function" then pcall(_G.EliteSaveSettings) end
        end
        startBoost()
    end
end

pcall(function()
    LocalPlayer:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
        if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then
            onStealing()
        end
    end)
    if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then
        onStealing()
    end
end)

LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    stopBoost()
    if speedActive then
        task.defer(startBoost)
    end
end)
end

-- ============================================================
-- AYDN AP PANEL (real chat-command spam from Aydn AP source)
-- AYDN REWORK: HORIZONTAL chip panel -- players flow left->right and the
-- panel auto-grows to fit everyone (rebuilt on PlayerAdded/Removing).
-- AYDN v5: compact = the whole panel made SMALLER (tiny header, tighter
-- rows, smaller chips -- same buttons and features as before).
-- ============================================================

    name = "AydnAP",
    title = "AYDN AP",
    w = 124,
    compact = true,
    pos = loadPos("AydnAP", UDim2.fromOffset(16, 20)),
})

local TextChatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")
local function sendCmd(msg)
    local ok = pcall(function()
        local channel = TextChatService.TextChannels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral")
        if channel then
            channel:SendAsync(msg)
            return
        end
        local rStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local remote = rStorage:FindFirstChild("SayMessageRequest", true)
        if remote then
            remote:FireServer(msg, "All")
        end
    end)
end

local SPAM_LIST = {
    ";rocket ", ";tiny ", ";ragdoll ", ";balloon ",
    ";jumpscare ", ";morph ", ";inverse ", ";blind "
}

local function runSpam(name)
    for _, c in ipairs(SPAM_LIST) do
        sendCmd(c .. name)
        task.wait(0.2)
    end
end

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local function headThumb(plr)
    return "rbxthumb://type=AvatarHeadShot&id=" .. tostring(plr.UserId) .. "&w=48&h=48"
end

-- BASE OWNER (live): nearest plot within 90 studs -> its sign text -> player
local function currentOwnerPlr()
    local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not plots or not hrp then return nil end
    local best, bestD = nil, math.huge
    for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
        local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
        local part = sign and ((sign:IsA("BasePart") and sign) or sign:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true))
        if part then
            local d = (Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X, 0, hrp.Position.Z)
                - Vector3.new(part.Position.X, 0, part.Position.Z)).Magnitude
            if d < bestD then bestD, best = d, plot end
        end
    end
    if not best or bestD > 90 then return nil end
    local name
    local sign = best:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
    local gui = sign and sign:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SurfaceGui", true)
    local label = gui and gui:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)
    local text = label and label.Text
    if text and text ~= "" and not text:lower():find("empty") then
        name = text:match("^(.+)'s Base$") or text
    end
    if not name or name == "" then return nil end
    local want = name:lower()
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= LocalPlayer and (p.Name:lower() == want or (p.DisplayName or ""):lower() == want) then
            return p
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local ownerRow = Instance.new("Frame")
ownerRow.Name = "OwnerRow"
ownerRow.BackgroundTransparency = 1

            ownerLbl.Text = "OWNER: " .. own.Name

            ownerAv.Visible = true
        else
            ownerLbl.Text = "OWNER: none (stand in a base)"
            ownerLbl.TextColor3 = COL_MUTED
            ownerAv.Visible = false
        end
        task.wait(1.0)
    end
end)

    if not own then
        spamOwner.Text = "NO OWNER IN RANGE"
        task.delay(1.2, function()
            if spamOwner and spamOwner.Parent then spamOwner.Text = "SPAM OWNER" end
        end)
        return
    end
    spamOwner.Text = "SPAMMING " .. string.upper(own.Name) .. "..."
    setOnLook(spamOwner, true)
    task.spawn(function()
        runSpam(own.Name)
        if spamOwner and spamOwner.Parent then
            spamOwner.Text = "SPAM OWNER"
            setOnLook(spamOwner, false)
        end
    end)
end)

local spamAll = mkBtn("SPAM ALL", apBtnRow)

spamAll.TextSize = 7

    setOnLook(spamAll, true)
    spamAll.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(9, 9, 9)
    task.spawn(function()
        -- one command per player (rotate through list), everyone once
        local idx = 1
        local list = SPAM_LIST
        for _, p in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= LocalPlayer then
                local cmd = list[((idx - 1) % #list) + 1]
                sendCmd(cmd .. p.Name)
                idx = idx + 1
                task.wait(0.2)
            end
        end
        if spamAll and spamAll.Parent then
            spamAll.Text = "SPAM ALL"
            setOnLook(spamAll, false)
        end
    end)
end)

local COL_STEAL = Color3.fromRGB(196, 40, 42)
local function rebuildAPPlayers()
    for _, c in ipairs(apGrid:GetChildren()) do
        if c:IsA("GuiObject") then c:Destroy() end
    end
    local order = 0
    local others = 0
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
            others = others + 1
            -- CHIP (horizontal layout): [avatar] [name -> spam] [STEALING]
            local chip = Instance.new("Frame")
            chip.Name = "APChip"
            chip:SetAttribute("PlayerName", plr.Name)
            chip.BackgroundColor3 = COL_CARD
            chip.BorderSizePixel = 0
            chip.LayoutOrder = order
            order = order + 1
            chip.Parent = apGrid
            uiCorner(chip, 8)
            uiStroke(chip)
            local av = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
            av.Name = "Avatar"
            av.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10)
            av.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -5, 0, 1)
            av.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 24)
            pcall(function() av.Image = headThumb(plr) end)
            av.Parent = chip
            uiCorner(av, 6)
            uiStroke(av)
            local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
            b.Name = "SpamBtn"
            b.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 10)
            b.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 11)
            b.BackgroundColor3 = COL_OFF
            b.TextColor3 = COL_TEXT
            b.Text = plr.Name
            b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
            b.TextSize = 7
            b.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
            b.AutoButtonColor = false
            b.Parent = chip
            uiCorner(b, 6)
            uiStroke(b)
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                local old = b.Text
                b.Text = "-> " .. old
                task.spawn(function()
                    runSpam(plr.Name)
                    if b and b.Parent then b.Text = old end
                end)
            end)
            local stealL = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            stealL.Name = "StealingLbl"
            stealL.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            stealL.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 7)
            stealL.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 21)
            stealL.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            stealL.TextScaled = true
            stealL.TextColor3 = COL_STEAL
            stealL.Visible = false
            stealL.Text = "STEALING"
            stealL.Parent = chip
        end
    end
    if others == 0 then
        local none = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        none.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        none.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 12)
        none.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        none.TextSize = 7
        none.TextColor3 = COL_MUTED
        none.Text = "No other players."
        none.LayoutOrder = 99
        none.Parent = apGrid
    end
end

task.spawn(function()

            for _, chip in ipairs(apGrid:GetChildren()) do
                if chip:IsA("Frame") then
                    local nm = chip:GetAttribute("PlayerName")
                    local plr = nm and Players:FindFirstChild(nm)
                    local stealing = plr ~= nil and plr:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
                    local lbl = chip:FindFirstChild("StealingLbl")
                    if lbl then lbl.Visible = stealing end
                    local btn = chip:FindFirstChild("SpamBtn")
                    if btn then
                        btn.BackgroundColor3 = stealing and COL_STEAL or COL_OFF
                        btn.TextColor3 = stealing and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or COL_TEXT
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
        task.wait(0.5)
    end
end)

-- ============================================================
-- AYDN PROXIMITY AP: a neon ground circle follows you (radius = PROX
-- RADIUS slider in the main GUI, up to 30 studs). Any player that steps
-- inside the circle gets auto-spammed with the AP command list
-- (debounced so nobody gets double-spammed within 12s).
-- ============================================================
do
local circle = Instance.new("Part")
circle.Name = "AydnProxCircle"
circle.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
circle.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
circle.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 64, 64)
circle.Transparency = 1
circle.Anchored = true
circle.CanCollide = false
circle.CanQuery = false
circle.CanTouch = false
circle.CastShadow = false
pcall(function() circle.Parent = workspace end)
local lastSpam = {}
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(0.5)
        pcall(function()
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local r = math.clamp(tonumber(Config.ProximityRadius) or 12, 0, 30)
            local on = (Config.ProximityAP ~= false) and r >= 2
            if not hrp or not on then
                if circle.Transparency ~= 1 then circle.Transparency = 1 end
                return
            end
            circle.Transparency = 0.55
            circle.Size = Vector3.new(0.1, r * 2, r * 2)
            circle.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position.X, hrp.Position.Y - 2.8, hrp.Position.Z)
                * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90))
            local now = os.clock()
            for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
                    local pc = plr.Character
                    local ph = pc and pc:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if ph and (ph.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude <= r then
                        local last = lastSpam[plr.Name]
                        if not last or (now - last) > 12 then
                            lastSpam[plr.Name] = now
                            task.spawn(runSpam, plr.Name)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end)
end
-- AYDN: AUTO SPAM OWNER -- when you are stealing (Stealing attribute true)
-- the base owner gets the full command spam automatically after 10 seconds.
-- Fires once per steal; resets when the steal ends.
do
local spammedThisSteal = false
LocalPlayer:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
    if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= true then
        spammedThisSteal = false
    end
end)
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(0.5)
        pcall(function()
            if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true and not spammedThisSteal then
                local started = os.clock()
                while LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
                    and (os.clock() - started) < 10 do
                    task.wait(0.5)
                end
                if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true and not spammedThisSteal then
                    spammedThisSteal = true
                    local own = currentOwnerPlr()
                    if own then
                        task.spawn(runSpam, own.Name)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end)
end
end

UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
rotSlide.setDrag(false)
depSlide.setDrag(false)
end
end)
UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
if not (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
return
end
if rotSlide.isDrag() then
local rel = math.clamp((i.Position.X - rotSlide.bar.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, rotSlide.bar.AbsoluteSize.X), 0, 1)
rotSlide.apply(rotSlide.minV + (rotSlide.maxV - rotSlide.minV) * rel)
end
if depSlide.isDrag() then
local rel = math.clamp((i.Position.X - depSlide.bar.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, depSlide.bar.AbsoluteSize.X), 0, 1)
depSlide.apply(depSlide.minV + (depSlide.maxV - depSlide.minV) * rel)
end
end)
local waveConn
local carpetOn = false
local function setCarpetSpeed(on)
carpetOn = on == true
Config.WaveriderSpeed = 140
Config.WaveriderSpeedEnabled = carpetOn
if type(_G.EliteSaveSettings) == "function" then pcall(_G.EliteSaveSettings) end
if waveConn then
waveConn:Disconnect()
waveConn = nil
end
if not carpetOn then
return
end
local lastTick = 0
waveConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if _G.__Elite_VelFlying or _G.__Elite_TPStarted or _G.isCloning or _G.EliteInvisActive or _G.AYDN_TP_ACTIVE then return end
local now = tick()
if now - lastTick < 0.066 then return end
lastTick = now
local c = LocalPlayer.Character
if not c then return end
local hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local hrp = c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not hum or not hrp then return end
local tn = (Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.Tool) or _G.Elite_CarpetTool or "Waverider"
if not c:FindFirstChild(tn) then
local tb = LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild(tn)
if not tb then
local names = _G.Elite_CarpetNames or TP_TOOLS
for i = 1, #names do
tb = LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild(names[i]) or c:FindFirstChild(names[i])
if tb then
tn = names[i]
break
end
end
end
if tb and _G.__Elite_GrappleFired then
pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(tb) end)
end
end
if not c:FindFirstChild(tn) then return end
local spd = 140
local md = hum.MoveDirection
if md.Magnitude > 0 then
hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(md.X * spd, hrp.Velocity.Y, md.Z * spd)
else
hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, hrp.Velocity.Y, 0)
end
end)
end
local function toggleCarpetSpeed()
setCarpetSpeed(not carpetOn)
if syncBindLabels then
syncBindLabels()
else
paint(bCarpet, "CARPET SPEED (" .. (keys.CarpetSpeed or "Q") .. ")", carpetOn)
end
end
local infJumpConn, lastJump = nil, 0
local function startInfiniteJump()
Config.InfiniteJump = true
if infJumpConn then
infJumpConn:Disconnect()
infJumpConn = nil
end
infJumpConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if _G.__Elite_VelFlying or _G.__Elite_TPStarted or _G.AYDN_TP_ACTIVE then return end
if not UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then return end
local now = tick()
if now - lastJump < 0.1 then return end
local c = LocalPlayer.Character
if not c then return end
local hrp = c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
lastJump = now
hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(hrp.Velocity.X, 55, hrp.Velocity.Z)
end)
end
local function letterOf(input)
local letter = ""
pcall(function()
letter = string.upper(tostring(UIS:GetStringForKeyCode(input.KeyCode) or ""))
end)
if letter == "" or letter == " " then
letter = string.upper(tostring(input.KeyCode and input.KeyCode.Name or ""))
end
return letter
end

{ id = "StealNearest", label = "STEAL NEAREST" },
{ id = "TPGo", label = "TP GO" },
{ id = "Invis", label = "INVIS STEAL" },
{ id = "CarpetSpeed", label = "CARPET SPEED" },
{ id = "AutoClone", label = "AUTO CLONE" },
{ id = "OpenGui", label = "OPEN GUI" },
}
local function paintBind(id)
local row = bindRows[id]
if not row then return end
if listening == id then
row.btn.Text = "..."
setOnLook(row.btn, true)
row.btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 92, 40)
else
row.btn.Text = keys[id] or "?"
setOnLook(row.btn, true)
end
end
local function syncBindLabels()
bSteal.Text = "STEAL NEAREST  [" .. (keys.StealNearest or "T") .. "]"
paint(bTPGo, "TP GO (" .. (keys.TPGo or "\\") .. ")", (_G.AYDN_TP and _G.AYDN_TP.Running()) or false)
paint(bCarpet, "CARPET SPEED (" .. (keys.CarpetSpeed or "Q") .. ")", carpetOn)
paint(bClone, "AUTO CLONE (" .. (keys.AutoClone or "V") .. ")", Config.AutoClone ~= false)
paintTool()
for i = 1, #bindDefs do
paintBind(bindDefs[i].id)
end
end
local function applyBind(id, letter)
keys[id] = letter
Config.Keybinds = keys
if type(_G.EliteSaveSettings) == "function" then pcall(_G.EliteSaveSettings) end
if id == "StealNearest" then
Config.StealNearestKey = letter
end
listening = nil
syncBindLabels()
end
local function startListen(id)
listening = (listening == id) and nil or id
syncBindLabels()
end

local function setKeysOpen(on)
keysOpen = on == true
keysFrame.Visible = keysOpen
if not keysOpen then
listening = nil
end
openBtn.Text = keysOpen and "CLOSE GUI" or "OPEN GUI"
setOnLook(openBtn, not keysOpen)
if keysOpen then
openBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(9, 9, 9)
end
syncBindLabels()
end

-- ============================================================
-- SHOW BASE IF 30M+ (AYDN user rule, reworked -- no prompts):
-- every base on the map is HIDDEN -- only bases holding a
-- 30M+/s brainrot (or a priority pet) stay visible. Your own
-- base is never hidden. Threshold: SHOW_BASE_MIN_MPS below.
-- ============================================================
do
local SHOW_BASE_MIN_MPS = 30e6 -- "30M plus" -- tweak here
local hiddenPlots = {} -- [plotName] = true while we hid it
local lastBaseCheck = 0
local lastReassertAt = 0
local REASSERT_EVERY = 3 -- sec; LocalTransparencyModifier can reset on respawn
local function setPlotHidden(plot, hidden)
    if not plot then return end
    local folders = {"Base", "PlotSign", "FriendPanel", "Cash", "Laser", "Decorations", "Skin"}
    for _, fname in ipairs(folders) do
        local f = plot:FindFirstChild(fname)
        if f then
            for _, d in ipairs(f:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("BasePart") then
                    if hidden then
                        if d:GetAttribute("_AydnOrigT") == nil then
                            d:SetAttribute("_AydnOrigT", d.LocalTransparencyModifier)
                        end
                        d.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
                    else
                        local o = d:GetAttribute("_AydnOrigT")
                        if o ~= nil then
                            d.LocalTransparencyModifier = o
                            d:SetAttribute("_AydnOrigT", nil)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    -- toggle gate: OFF = everything visible again (and nothing re-hidden)
    if Config.ShowBase30m ~= true then
        if next(hiddenPlots) ~= nil then
            local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
            for pn in pairs(hiddenPlots) do
                local plot = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(pn)
                if plot then setPlotHidden(plot, false) end
            end
            table.clear(hiddenPlots)
        end
        return
    end
    local now = os.clock()
    if now - lastBaseCheck < 0.35 then return end
    lastBaseCheck = now
    local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not plots then return end
    -- ONE pet-list scan per tick, bucketed per plot: best mps + priority flag
    local bestMps, hasPrio = {}, {}
    if _G.Elite_ListAllPets then
        local ok, all = pcall(_G.Elite_ListAllPets)
        if ok and type(all) == "table" then
            for _, p in ipairs(all) do
                if type(p) == "table" then
                    local pn = tostring(p.plot or "")
                    if pn ~= "" then
                        local v = tonumber(p.mps) or 0
                        if not bestMps[pn] or v > bestMps[pn] then bestMps[pn] = v end
                        if p._pri ~= nil then hasPrio[pn] = true end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local reassert = (now - lastReassertAt) > REASSERT_EVERY
    if reassert then lastReassertAt = now end
    for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
        local pn = tostring(plot.Name)
        -- own base is never hidden
        local own = false
        pcall(function() own = isMyPlot(getPlotChannel(pn)) == true end)
        local show = own or (bestMps[pn] or 0) >= SHOW_BASE_MIN_MPS or hasPrio[pn] == true
        local wantHidden = not show
        if hiddenPlots[pn] ~= wantHidden then
            hiddenPlots[pn] = wantHidden
            setPlotHidden(plot, wantHidden)
        elseif wantHidden and reassert then
            setPlotHidden(plot, true) -- safety re-assert after respawn resets
        end
    end
end)
end

do
local dragging, origin, startPos, moved
openBtn.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
dragging = true
moved = false
origin = i.Position
startPos = openBtn.Position
end
end)
UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
if dragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
local d = i.Position - origin
if math.abs(d.X) + math.abs(d.Y) > 4 then
moved = true
end
openBtn.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
end
end)
UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
if dragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
dragging = false
savePos("OpenGui", openBtn)
if not moved then
setKeysOpen(not keysOpen)
end
end
end)
end

Config.AutoClone = not (Config.AutoClone ~= false)
syncBindLabels()
if type(_G.EliteSaveSettings) == "function" then pcall(_G.EliteSaveSettings) end
end)
bInv.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
Config.InvSteal = not (Config.InvSteal ~= false)
paint(bInv, "INV STEAL", Config.InvSteal)
if type(_G.EliteSaveSettings) == "function" then pcall(_G.EliteSaveSettings) end
if _G.Elite_InvStealSet then
pcall(_G.Elite_InvStealSet, Config.InvSteal)
end
end)

setCarpetSpeed(false)
syncBindLabels()
UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
return
end
local letter = letterOf(input)
if letter == "" then
return
end
if listening then
if processed then return end
applyBind(listening, letter)
return
end
if processed then return end
if letter == keys.OpenGui then
setKeysOpen(not keysOpen)
elseif letter == keys.StealNearest then
pcall(_G.Elite_StealNearestPrio)
elseif letter == keys.TPGo then
if _G.AYDN_TP and _G.AYDN_TP.Toggle then
_G.AYDN_TP.Toggle()
end
elseif letter == keys.Invis then
Config.InvSteal = not (Config.InvSteal ~= false)
if _G.Elite_InvStealSet then
pcall(_G.Elite_InvStealSet, Config.InvSteal)
end
if type(paint) == "function" and bInv then
pcall(paint, bInv, "INV STEAL", Config.InvSteal)
end
if type(_G.EliteSaveSettings) == "function" then
pcall(_G.EliteSaveSettings)
end
elseif letter == keys.CarpetSpeed then
toggleCarpetSpeed()
elseif letter == keys.AutoClone and Config.AutoClone ~= false then
task.spawn(instantClone)
end
end)
end

local okAK, errAK = pcall(function()
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local Http = game:GetService("HttpService")
local Input = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
local LP = Players.LocalPlayer
if not LP then return end
local guiParent
pcall(function()
guiParent = _G.AYDN_GuiHost()
end)
if not guiParent then return end
local PG = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
if not PG then
pcall(function()
PG = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 8)
end)
end
local CFG_FILE = "V_Utility.json"
local cfg = {}
if readfile then
local ok, v = pcall(function()
return Http:JSONDecode(readfile(CFG_FILE))
end)
if ok and type(v) == "table" then
cfg = v
end
end
local Store = {
get = function()
return cfg
end,
save = function()
if writefile then
pcall(function()
writefile(CFG_FILE, Http:JSONEncode(cfg))
end)
end
end,
}
local UI = {
PAD = 16,
BTN_H = 32,
FONT = Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
C = {
PANEL = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 15),
BG = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 22),
BTN = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 22),
BTN_ON = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245),
TEXT = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245),
MUTED = Color3.fromRGB(140, 140, 140),
DANGER = Color3.fromRGB(178, 45, 46),
STROKE = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
ACCENT = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245),
},
}
function UI.corner(o, r)
local c = Instance.new("UICorner", o)
c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r or 12)
return c
end
function UI.stroke(o, color, t)
local st = Instance.new("UIStroke", o)
st.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
st.Color = color or UI.C.STROKE
st.Thickness = t or 1
st.Transparency = 0.86
return st
end
function UI.setEnabled(b, on)
b.BackgroundColor3 = on and UI.C.BTN_ON or UI.C.BTN
local st = b:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke") or UI.stroke(b)
st.Color = on and UI.C.ACCENT or UI.C.STROKE
st.Transparency = on and 0.35 or 0.86
st.Thickness = 1
end
function UI.panel(o)
local f = Instance.new("Frame", o.parent)
f.Name = o.name
f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(o.w, o.h)
f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(o.x, o.y)
f.BackgroundColor3 = UI.C.PANEL
f.BackgroundTransparency = 0.06
f.BorderSizePixel = 0
f.Active = true
UI.corner(f, 16)
UI.stroke(f)
local title = Instance.new("TextLabel", f)
title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 0)
title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 40)
title.Text = o.title
title.TextColor3 = UI.C.TEXT
title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
title.TextSize = 13
title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
title.Active = true
local line = Instance.new("Frame", f)
line.BorderSizePixel = 0
line.BackgroundColor3 = UI.C.STROKE
line.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 40)
line.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 1)
local body = Instance.new("Frame", f)
body.BackgroundTransparency = 1
body.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 46)
body.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -46)
local dragging, start, pos
title.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
dragging = true
start = i.Position
pos = f.Position
local conn
conn = i.Changed:Connect(function()
if i.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
dragging = false
if o.onDrop then o.onDrop(f) end
conn:Disconnect()
end
end)
end
end)
Input.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
if dragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
local d = i.Position - start
f.Position = UDim2.new(pos.X.Scale, pos.X.Offset + d.X, pos.Y.Scale, pos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
end
end)
return f, body, title
end

b.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center

if o.onClick then
b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(o.onClick)
end
return b, function(on)
UI.setEnabled(b, on)
end
end

end

game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", {
Title = title,
Text = tostring(msg),
Duration = 4,
})
end)
end
if _G.AutoKickOnSteal == nil then _G.AutoKickOnSteal = false end
if _G.AutoKickKeyword == nil then _G.AutoKickKeyword = "you stole" end
if _G.ElitePrivateCode == nil then _G.ElitePrivateCode = _G.elitePrivateCode or "" end
_G.EliteKickAfterBuy = true
local panelX, panelY = 240, 300
do
if type(cfg.on) == "boolean" then _G.AutoKickOnSteal = cfg.on end
if tonumber(cfg.x) then panelX = tonumber(cfg.x) end
if tonumber(cfg.y) then panelY = tonumber(cfg.y) end
if type(cfg.psCode) == "string" then _G.ElitePrivateCode = cfg.psCode end
end

cfg.on = _G.AutoKickOnSteal

end
local fired = false
local function doKick()
if fired then return end
fired = true
local code = _G.ElitePrivateCode
if type(code) == "string" and code ~= "" then
task.delay(0.2, function()
pcall(function()
game:GetService("ExperienceService"):LaunchExperience({
placeId = game.PlaceId,
linkCode = code,
})
end)
end)
return
end
pcall(function() game:Shutdown() end)
pcall(function() LP:Kick("\nAuto Kick") end)
end
_G.EliteDoAutoKick = doKick
local function check(txt)
if not _G.AutoKickOnSteal then return end
if type(txt) ~= "string" or txt == "" then return end
local kw = string.lower(tostring(_G.AutoKickKeyword or "you stole"))
if string.find(string.lower(txt), kw, 1, true) then
doKick()
end
end
local hooked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local function hookObj(obj)
if obj:FindFirstAncestor("AutoKickUI") then return end
if hooked[obj] then return end
hooked[obj] = true
check(obj.Text)
obj:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
check(obj.Text)
end)
end
local function isText(o)
return o:IsA("TextLabel") or o:IsA("TextButton") or o:IsA("TextBox")
end
local function watchRoot(root)
for _, obj in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
if isText(obj) then hookObj(obj) end
end
root.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(desc)
if isText(desc) then hookObj(desc) end
end)
end
if PG then
for _, gui in ipairs(PG:GetChildren()) do
pcall(watchRoot, gui)
end
PG.ChildAdded:Connect(function(gui)
pcall(watchRoot, gui)
end)
end
local function rejoin()
local jobId = game.JobId
task.spawn(function()
if not jobId or jobId == "" then return end
for _ = 1, 4 do
local ok = pcall(function()
TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, jobId, LP)
end)
if ok then break end
task.wait(1.5)
end
end)
end
_G.Rejoin = rejoin
pcall(function()
local old = guiParent:FindFirstChild("AutoKickUI")
if old then old:Destroy() end
if PG then
local leftover = PG:FindFirstChild("AutoKickUI")
if leftover then leftover:Destroy() end
end
end)

return pcall(writefile, AJ_FILE, src)
end
